### [1] [Layout-Aware Representation Learning for Open-Set ID Fraud Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05215)
*Jinxing Li,Nicholas Ren,Cathy Chang,Hongkai Pan,Daniel George*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于布局感知表示学习的开放集证件欺诈检测方法，利用DINOv3结合上下文感知SimMIM微调和复合损失度量学习，仅用美国ID训练即可高精度识别加拿大证件布局，并在嵌入空间中发现了大量传统检测器无法发现的物理欺诈案例。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 证件欺诈检测不是静态的二分类问题，攻击者会自适应修改模板和伪造流程，导致历史欺诈标签过时，且成功伪造会以大规模战役形式重复出现。因此需要面向开放集的欺诈发现，而非仅做封闭集分类。

Method: 将DINOv3适配到证件领域：通过上下文感知的SimMIM微调和带复合损失的监督度量学习（提升类间可分性和类内紧凑性），仅用美国ID训练得到布局感知的嵌入表示，支持轻量级MLP+softmax分类器和嵌入空间分析。

Result: 在加拿大证件布局分类上达到99.83%准确率；在20,448张加拿大ID数据集中，嵌入空间分析发现276例自适应物理欺诈，其中222例未被传统检测器发现；嵌入支持从单个种子案例基于相似性扩展到其他相关案例。

Conclusion: 布局感知的证件嵌入为在分布偏移下发现新型和大规模战役级欺诈提供了可投入生产的基准方法。

Original Abstract: Identity-document fraud detection is not a stationary binary classification problem. Adaptive attackers modify templates and fabrication pipelines, making historical fraud labels stale, and successful forgeries recur at scale as coherent campaigns. We therefore study layout-aware representation learning for open-set fraud discovery rather than only closed-set classification. We adapt DINOv3 to the document domain via context-aware SimMIM fine-tuning and supervised metric learning with composite loss that encourages inter-class separability and intra-class compactness. The model is trained with U.S. IDs only. With a lightweight MLP and softmax classifier, the embedding achieves 99.83% layout classification accuracy on Canadian layouts. Moreover, on a dataset of 20,448 Canadian IDs, embedding-space analysis surfaces 276 adaptive physical-fraud cases, including 222 not surfaced by incumbent detectors. The embedding supports similarity-based expansion from a single confirmed seed to additional related cases not linked by conventional metadata graphs. The layout-aware document embeddings provide a production-aligned basis for discovering novel and campaign-scale fraud under distribution shift.

中文摘要翻译: 证件欺诈检测并非一个静态的二分类问题。自适应攻击者会修改模板和伪造流程，使得历史欺诈标签失效，并且成功的伪造会以连贯的战役形式大规模重复出现。因此，我们研究面向开放集欺诈发现的布局感知表示学习，而不仅仅是封闭集分类。我们通过上下文感知的SimMIM微调和带有复合损失的监督度量学习（增强类间可分性和类内紧凑性），将DINOv3适配到证件领域。该模型仅使用美国ID进行训练。借助轻量级MLP和softmax分类器，该嵌入在加拿大证件布局上达到了99.83%的布局分类准确率。此外，在一个包含20,448张加拿大ID的数据集上，嵌入空间分析发现了276例自适应物理欺诈案例，其中222例未被现有检测器发现。该嵌入支持从单个确认的种子案例通过相似性扩展，发现传统元数据图谱无法关联的其他相关案例。布局感知的证件嵌入为在分布偏移下发现新型和大规模战役级欺诈提供了可投入生产的基准方法。

</details>


### [2] [Query2Uncertainty: Robust Uncertainty Quantification and Calibration for 3D Object Detection under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05328)
*Till Beemelmanns,Alexey Nekrasov,Stefan Vilceanu,Jonas Steinhaus,Timo Woopen,Bastian Leibe,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种密度感知的校准方法，利用DETR风格3D检测器中潜在目标查询的特征密度，联合校准分类和边界框回归的不确定性，在分布内和分布偏移场景下均优于标准事后校准方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的3D目标检测器在分布偏移场景下校准效果差，虽然事后校准方法在分布内测试中表现良好，但无法适应分布偏移场景。

Method: 将事后校准器与DETR风格3D检测器中潜在目标查询的特征密度相结合，对这些查询特征拟合密度估计器，同时重新校准分类和边界框回归的不确定性。

Result: 在多视角相机和基于LiDAR的检测器上，该方法在分布内和分布偏移场景下均一致优于标准事后校准方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的密度感知校准方法能够有效提升3D目标检测器在分布偏移场景下的校准性能，为部署安全自主系统提供了更可靠的 uncertainty 估计。

Original Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimation for 3D object detection is critical for deploying safe autonomous systems, yet modern detectors remain poorly calibrated, especially under distribution shifts. Although post-hoc calibration methods address this issue and provide improved calibration for in-distribution tests, they fail to adapt in distribution-shifted scenarios. In this work, we address this issue and introduce a density-aware calibration method that couples post-hoc calibrators with the feature density of latent object queries from DETR-style 3D object detectors. These queries form a compact, location and class-aware feature, ideal for density estimation, allowing our approach to adjust model confidences in distribution-shift scenarios. By fitting a density estimator on these query features, our approach jointly recalibrates both classification and bounding box regression uncertainties. On both a multi-view camera and LiDAR-based detector, our approach consistently outperforms standard post-hoc methods in both in-distribution and distribution-shifted scenarios. Code available https://tillbeemelmanns.github.io/query2uncertainty/ .

中文摘要翻译: 对于部署安全的自主系统而言，3D目标检测的可靠不确定性估计至关重要。然而，现代检测器的校准效果仍然较差，尤其是在分布偏移场景下。尽管事后校准方法解决了这一问题，并能在分布内测试中提供更好的校准，但它们无法在分布偏移场景中自适应。在本文中，我们解决了这一问题，并引入了一种密度感知校准方法，将事后校准器与来自DETR风格3D目标检测器中潜在目标查询的特征密度相结合。这些查询形成了紧凑的、感知位置和类别的特征，非常适合密度估计，使我们的方法能够在分布偏移场景中调整模型置信度。通过对这些查询特征拟合密度估计器，我们的方法联合重新校准了分类和边界框回归的不确定性。在多视角相机和基于LiDAR的检测器上，我们的方法在分布内和分布偏移场景下均一致优于标准事后校准方法。代码可访问 https://tillbeemelmanns.github.io/query2uncertainty/。

</details>


### [3] [ViTok-v2: Scaling Native Resolution Auto-Encoders to 5 Billion Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05331)
*Philippe Hansen-Estruch,Jiahui Chen,Vivek Ramanujan,Orr Zohar,Yan Ping,Animesh Sinha,Markos Georgopoulos,Edgar Schoenfeld,Ji Hou,Felix Juefei-Xu,Sriram Vishwanath,Ali Thabet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViTok-v2 是一个大规模图像自编码器，通过原生分辨率支持（NaFlex）和DINOv3感知损失替代GAN与LPIPS，解决了ViT自编码器在分辨率泛化和稳定训练上的局限，在256p及以上分辨率达到SOTA重构效果，并通过联合缩放生成器推进了重构-生成帕累托前沿。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ViT自编码器在训练分辨率之外性能下降，且依赖对抗损失导致无法稳定扩展；同时压缩比r决定了重构与生成之间的权衡，提升重构质量是获得更优帕累托前沿的关键。

Method: 1) 采用NaFlex实现原生分辨率支持，使其能泛化到不同分辨率和宽高比；2) 提出DINOv3感知损失替代LPIPS和GAN目标，实现任意规模下的稳定训练；3) 在约20亿图像上训练，参数规模达50亿。

Result: 在256p分辨率上匹配或超越SOTA重构，在512p及以上分辨率全面超越所有基线；联合缩放自编码器和生成器能推进重构-生成权衡的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 通过原生分辨率支持和稳定的感知损失设计，大规模缩放自编码器能显著提升重构质量，并推动重构与生成之间的帕累托前沿向更优方向移动。

Original Abstract: Vision Transformer (ViT) autoencoders have emerged as compelling tokenizers for images, offering improved reconstruction over convolutional tokenizers. However, existing ViT tokenizers cannot explore this landscape as performance degrades outside training resolutions, and reliance on adversarial losses prevents stable scaling. ViTok (Hansen-Estruch et al., 2025) found that the compression ratio r mediates a reconstruction-generation trade-off where lower r means better reconstructions but harder generations, so improving tokenizer reconstruction is key to more Pareto-optimal tokenizers. We introduce ViTok-v2, which addresses these limitations with native resolution support via NaFlex for generalization across resolutions and aspect ratios, and a novel DINOv3 perceptual loss that replaces both LPIPS and GAN objectives for stable training at any scale. ViTok-v2 is trained on about 2B images and scaled to 5B parameters, the largest image autoencoder to date. ViTok-v2 matches or exceeds state-of-the-art reconstruction at 256p and outperforms all baselines at 512p and above. In joint scaling experiments with flow matching generators, we show that scaling both the autoencoder and the generator advances the Pareto frontier of this trade-off.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉Transformer（ViT）自编码器已成为引人注目的图像分词器，相比卷积分词器提供了改进的重构效果。然而，现有ViT自编码器无法充分探索这一领域，因为其在训练分辨率之外的性能会下降，且对对抗损失的依赖阻碍了稳定扩展。ViTok（Hansen-Estruch等人，2025）发现，压缩比r调节着重构与生成之间的权衡——较低的r意味着更好的重构但更困难的生成，因此提升分词器的重构质量是获得更优帕累托最优分词器的关键。我们提出了ViTok-v2，通过NaFlex实现原生分辨率支持以泛化到不同分辨率和宽高比，以及一种新颖的DINOv3感知损失来替代LPIPS和GAN目标，从而在任何规模下实现稳定训练。ViTok-v2在约20亿张图像上训练，参数量达50亿，是迄今为止最大的图像自编码器。ViTok-v2在256p分辨率上匹配或超越最先进的重构效果，在512p及以上分辨率全面超越所有基线。在与流匹配生成器的联合缩放实验中，我们证明了同时缩放自编码器和生成器能够推进这一权衡的帕累托前沿。

</details>


### [4] [Open-SAT: LLM-Guided Query Embedding Refinement for Open-Vocabulary Object Retrieval in Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05344)
*Md Adnan Arefeen,Biplob Debnath,Ravi K. Rajendran,Murugan Sankaradas,Srimat T. Chakradhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Open-SAT，一种无需训练的查询嵌入精炼算法，利用LLM在推理时优化文本嵌入，提升开放词汇卫星图像检索的准确率，F1最高提升16.04%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 卫星应用中用户的自然语言查询是开放词汇的，超出预定义类别范围，现有VLM（如CLIP）即使微调也难以准确对齐这类查询与卫星图像内容。

Method: Open-SAT是一种训练无关的推理时查询嵌入精炼算法：使用VLM计算图像块嵌入并存入向量数据库；在查询时利用LLM通过引入目标物体及其周围环境的上下文信息来精炼文本嵌入；采用无阈值的检索机制提升精度和效率。

Result: 在三个公开基准上，F1分数最高提升16.04%，同时检索的图像块数量与基线相当。

Conclusion: Open-SAT借助LLM引导，无需额外训练或监督，即可有效提升开放词汇卫星图像检索的性能。

Original Abstract: In satellite applications, user queries often take the form of open-ended natural language, extending beyond a fixed set of predefined categories. This open-vocabulary nature poses significant challenges for retrieving relevant image tiles, as the retrieval system must generalize to a wide range of unseen objects and concepts. While vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP are widely used for text-image retrieval, even fine-tuned variants often struggle to accurately align such queries with satellite imagery. To address this, we propose Open-SAT, a training-free query embedding refinement algorithm that operates at inference time to improve alignment between user queries and satellite image content. Open-SAT uses VLMs to compute embeddings for image tiles, which are stored in a vector database for efficient retrieval. At query time, it leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to refine the text embeddings by incorporating contextual information about objects of interest and their surroundings. A threshold-free retrieval mechanism further enhances accuracy and efficiency. Experimental results in three public benchmarks demonstrate that Open-SAT improves the F1 score by up to 16.04%, while retrieving a comparable number of image tiles. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of Open-SAT in open-vocabulary satellite image retrieval, leveraging LLM guidance without the need for additional training or supervision.

中文摘要翻译: 在卫星应用中，用户的查询通常以开放式的自然语言形式出现，超出固定预定义类别集合的范围。这种开放词汇特性给相关图像瓦片的检索带来了巨大挑战，因为检索系统必须推广到大量未见过的物体和概念。虽然视觉-语言模型（VLM）如CLIP被广泛用于文本-图像检索，但即使是微调后的变体也常常难以将这些查询与卫星图像精确对齐。为解决这一问题，我们提出了Open-SAT，一种无需训练的查询嵌入精炼算法，在推理时运行以改善用户查询与卫星图像内容之间的对齐。Open-SAT使用VLM计算图像瓦片的嵌入，并存储在向量数据库中以便高效检索。在查询时，它利用大语言模型（LLM）通过引入目标物体及其周围环境的上下文信息来精炼文本嵌入。一种无阈值的检索机制进一步提升了精度和效率。在三个公开基准上的实验结果表明，Open-SAT将F1分数最高提升了16.04%，同时检索了数量相当的图像瓦片。这些结果证明了Open-SAT在开放词汇卫星图像检索中的有效性，借助LLM的引导而无需额外训练或监督。

</details>


### [5] [egenioussBench: A New Dataset for Geospatial Visual Localisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05351)
*Phillipp Fanta-Jende,Francesco Vultaggio,Alexander Kern,Yasmin Loeper,Markus Gerke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了egenioussBench，一个基于地理空间参考数据（城市级航空3D网格和CityGML LoD2模型）的视觉定位基准，使用智能手机图像和厘米级精度地面真值，通过非共视子集和分箱评估指标实现公平比较。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉定位基准多基于SfM方法构建，难以真正扩展到城市级大规模场景，且缺乏可部署的真实地理空间参考数据。为了解决可扩展性和真实跨域挑战，作者提出了基于现成地理空间数据（航空3D网格和CityGML LoD2模型）的基准。

Method: 1) 使用城市级航空3D网格和CityGML LoD2模型作为地理空间参考数据；2) 通过PPK和GCP/CP辅助平差获取厘米级精度、独立于地图的地面真值；3) 从2709张图像中，通过渲染深度估计完整共视矩阵，选择最大独立集得到非共视子集（测试集42张非共视图像，验证集412张序列图像）；4) 采用多姿态误差阈值下的分箱评估指标，结合全局统计（中位数、RMSE、离群率）进行公平比较。

Result: 构建了包含42张非共视测试图像（真值保密）和412张序列验证图像的数据集；提供了公开排行榜，采用分箱指标评估网格和LoD2两种参考数据上的方法性能。

Conclusion: 该基准通过引入真实地理空间参考数据、非共视子集设计和公平的分箱评估指标，为大规模视觉定位提供了严谨且可扩展的评测路径，暴露了真实的跨视角和跨领域挑战。

Original Abstract: We present egenioussBench, a visual localisation benchmark built on geospatial reference data: a city-scale airborne 3D mesh and a CityGML LoD2 model. This pairing reflects deployable mapping assets and supports true scalability beyond traditional SfM-based approaches. The query data comprise smartphone images with centimetre-accurate, map-independent ground truth obtained via PPK and GCP/CP-aided adjustment. From 2,709 images, we derive a non-co-visible subset by estimating the full co-visibility matrix from rendered depth and selecting a maximum independent set; the released data include a test split of 42 non-co-visible images with withheld ground truth and a validation split of 412 sequential images with poses, e.g. for training of pose regressors and self-validation. The benchmark features a public leaderboard evaluated with binning metrics at multiple pose-error thresholds alongside global statistics (median, RMSE, outlier ratio), ensuring fair, like-for-like comparison across mesh- and LoD2-based methods. Together, these design choices expose realistic cross-view and cross-domain challenges while providing a rigorous, scalable path for advancing large-scale visual localisation. We make the evaluation code and data availeable at https://github.com/fratopa/egenioussBench and https://www.egeniouss.eu/

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了egenioussBench，一个基于地理空间参考数据构建的视觉定位基准：城市级航空3D网格和CityGML LoD2模型。这一组合反映了可部署的地图资产，并支持超越传统基于SfM方法的真正可扩展性。查询数据包括智能手机图像，通过PPK和GCP/CP辅助平差获得厘米级精度、独立于地图的地面真值。从2,709张图像中，我们通过渲染深度估计完整共视矩阵并选择最大独立集，推导出一个非共视子集；发布的数据包括42张真值保密的非共视测试图像和412张带有位姿的序列验证图像（例如用于位姿回归器的训练和自验证）。该基准设有一个公开排行榜，采用多姿态误差阈值下的分箱评估指标及全局统计量（中位数、RMSE、离群率），确保基于网格和LoD2的方法之间进行公平、对等的比较。这些设计选择共同暴露了真实的跨视角和跨领域挑战，同时为推进大规模视觉定位提供了严谨且可扩展的路径。我们在https://github.com/fratopa/egenioussBench 和 https://www.egeniouss.eu/ 上提供评估代码和数据。

</details>


### [6] [LAMP: Localization Aware Multi-camera People Tracking in Metric 3D World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05390)
*Nan Yang,Julian Straub,Fan Zhang,Richard Newcombe,Jakob Engel,Lingni Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAMP提出了一种解耦观察者与目标运动的框架，通过将2D关键点提升到统一3D世界坐标系后再拟合3D人体运动，实现了在自我中心多摄像头场景下的高效3D人体追踪，并在单目基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单目人体运动追踪方法在自我中心多摄像头场景下表现脆弱，因为它们通常假设静态或缓慢移动的相机，无法有效利用多视角、已标定和定位的多摄像头输入，尤其是在强烈的自我运动、局部遮挡和训练数据不足的情况下。

Method: LAMP采用"提升-然后-拟合"两阶段方法：首先利用已知的设备6自由度运动和标定信息，将时间窗口内所有相机的2D人体关键点转换到统一的3D世界坐标系中；然后通过端到端训练的时空Transformer直接从这个3D射线云中拟合3D人体运动。

Result: LAMP在单目基准测试上达到了最先进的结果，同时在面向的自我中心多摄像头设置中显著优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: LAMP通过早期解耦观察者与目标运动，能够学习和利用自然的人体运动先验，并灵活地整合来自多个时序异步、部分观测和移动的摄像头的信息，有效解决了自我中心多摄像头场景下的3D人体追踪难题。

Original Abstract: Tracking 3D human motion from egocentric multi-camera headset is challenged by severe egomotion, partial visibility or occlusions and lack of training data. Existing methods designed for monocular video often require static or slowly-moving cameras and cannot efficiently leverage multi-view, calibrated and localized input. This makes them brittle and prone to fail on dynamic egocentric captures. We propose LAMP (Localization Aware Multi-camera People Tracking): a novel, simple framework to solve this via early disentanglement of observer and target motion. LAMP introduces a two-step process. First, we leverage the known device 6 DoF motion and calibration to convert detected 2D body keypoints from all cameras over a temporal window into a unified 3D world reference frame. Second, an end-to-end-trained spatio-temporal transformer fits 3D human motion directly to this 3D ray cloud. This "lift-then-fit" approach allows LAMP to learn and leverage a natural human motion prior in the world-space, as well as providing an elegant framework to flexibly incorporate information from multiple temporally asynchronous, partially observing and moving cameras. LAMP achieves state-of-the-art results on monocular benchmarks, while significantly outperforming baselines for our targeted egocentric setting.

中文摘要翻译: 从自我中心多摄像头头戴设备追踪3D人体运动面临着强烈的自我运动、部分可见性或遮挡以及训练数据不足的挑战。现有为单目视频设计的方法通常要求静态或缓慢移动的摄像头，且无法有效利用多视角、已标定和已定位的输入。这使得它们在动态的自我中心捕捉场景中表现脆弱且容易失败。我们提出LAMP（定位感知的多摄像头人体追踪）：一个新颖而简单的框架，通过早期解耦观察者与目标运动来解决这一问题。LAMP引入了一个两步流程。首先，利用已知的设备六自由度运动和标定信息，将时间窗口内所有摄像头检测到的2D人体关键点转换到统一的3D世界参考系中。其次，一个端到端训练的时空Transformer直接从这个3D射线云中拟合3D人体运动。这种"提升-然后-拟合"的方法使LAMP能够学习和利用世界空间中的自然人体运动先验，并提供了一个优雅的框架来灵活地整合来自多个时序异步、部分观测和移动的摄像头的信息。LAMP在单目基准测试上达到了最先进的结果，同时在我们所针对的自我中心场景中显著优于基线方法。

</details>


### [7] [EchoXFlow: A Beamspace Echocardiography Dataset for Cardiac Motion, Flow, and Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05447)
*Elias Stenhede,Joanna Sulkowska,Eivind Bjørkan Orstad,Henrik Schirmer,Arian Ranjbar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoXFlow是一个临床超声心动图数据集，保留了超声原始采集几何信息（而非扫描转换后的笛卡尔视频），包含37125条记录、多模态Doppler流和同步ECG，支持跨模态和采集感知的物理驱动学习任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有公开超声心动图数据集存在以下问题：Doppler信息通常缺失或仅以RGB叠加方式呈现；数据经过有损的厂商显示处理后发布；无法研究心脏解剖、心肌运动与血流之间的跨模态关系。因此需要构建一个保留原始采集几何、时序和模态关系的数据集。

Method: 从666例常规临床检查中收集37125条记录，保留原始超声采集的时序、几何和模态关系。每条记录以可分离的模态特定流形式保存，包括时间分辨的1D/2D/3D数据及多种Doppler模态，并配有同步ECG。提供基于指南的临床标注、密集2D心肌轮廓和3D左心室内膜网格。

Result: 构建了包含37125条记录、666例检查的大规模数据集；提供从指南测量到密集2D/3D解剖标注的多层次标注；附带开源工具，支持传统扫描转换视频无法实现的跨模态、采集感知学习任务。

Conclusion: EchoXFlow作为4D视觉和物理驱动多模态学习的测试平台，使研究者能够利用原始采集几何信息进行跨模态学习，推动了超声心动图领域从后处理视频向原始采集数据的学习范式转变。

Original Abstract: We introduce EchoXFlow, a clinical echocardiography dataset for learning from ultrasound in its native acquisition geometry rather than from scan-converted Cartesian videos. Existing public datasets offer limited opportunities to study cross-modal relationships between cardiac anatomy, myocardial motion, and blood flow, as Doppler is typically absent or fused as RGB overlays, and acquisitions are released after lossy vendor display processing. EchoXFlow comprises 37125 recordings from 666 routine-care examinations, preserving the timing, geometry, and modality relationships needed for physically grounded echo learning. Each recording is retained as separable modality-specific streams: temporally resolved 1D, 2D, and 3D data alongside multiple Doppler modalities, paired with a synchronized ECG. Clinical annotations span guideline-based measurements to dense 2D myocardial contours and 3D left-ventricular endocardial meshes. With its associated open-source tooling, EchoXFlow enables cross-modal, acquisition-aware learning tasks that cannot be formulated from conventional scan-converted videos alone, and serves as a testbed for 4D vision and physically grounded multi-modal learning more broadly.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了EchoXFlow，一个临床超声心动图数据集，旨在从超声的原始采集几何（而非扫描转换后的笛卡尔视频）中学习。现有公开数据集在研究心脏解剖、心肌运动和血流之间的跨模态关系方面机会有限，因为Doppler通常缺失或仅以RGB叠加方式融合，且数据在经有损的厂商显示处理后发布。EchoXFlow包含来自666例常规临床检查的37125条记录，保留了物理驱动超声学习所需的时序、几何和模态关系。每条记录以可分离的模态特定流形式保存：时间分辨的1D、2D和3D数据，以及多种Doppler模态，并配有同步ECG。临床标注涵盖从基于指南的测量到密集的2D心肌轮廓和3D左心室内膜网格。借助其配套开源工具，EchoXFlow能够支持从传统扫描转换视频中无法构建的跨模态、采集感知学习任务，并更广泛地作为4D视觉和物理驱动多模态学习的测试平台。

</details>


### [8] [The First Controllable Bokeh Rendering Challenge at NTIRE 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05510)
*Tim Seizinger,Florin-Alexandru Vasluianu,Jeffrey Chen,Zhuyun Zhou,Zongwei Wu,Radu Timofte,Dafeng Zhang,Yipeng Lin,Qi Yan,Junhao Chen,Yang Yang,Divyavardhan Singh,Hariom Thacker,Hammad Mohammad,Aanchal Maurya,Kishor Upla,Kiran Raja,Wei Zhou,Hongyu Huang,Yujin Cho,Grigory Malivenko,Jiachen Tu,Yaokun Shi,Guoyi Xu,Yaoxin Jiang,Jiajia Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NTIRE首届可控散景渲染挑战赛的结果，8支队伍提交了有效方案，比赛包含定量指标评估和专家用户研究两个赛道，多数参赛者基于Bokehlicious基线方法进行改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 推动可控散景渲染领域的发展，通过举办首届挑战赛系统性地评估和比较不同方法在复杂肖像与精细主体上的散景效果，并同时关注定量保真度和感知质量。

Method: 比赛设置两个赛道：第一赛道基于定量保真度指标评估；第二赛道由专家小组进行定性用户研究。多数参赛者采用改进和扩展Bokehlicious基线方法的方式。

Result: 44人注册参赛，8支队伍通过最终测试阶段提交了有效方案。所有方案在未见过的图像上评估，重点考察肖像和复杂精细主体的散景效果。大多数参赛者聚焦于改进Bokehlicious基线方法。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛为可控散景渲染领域提供了首次系统性的基准评估，展示了当前方法的性能水平，并指出了未来改进方向。

Original Abstract: This study presents the outcomes of the first Controllable Bokeh Rendering Challenge at NTIRE and highlights the most effective submitted methodologies. In total, 44 participants registered for the competition, of which 8 teams submitted valid solutions after the conclusion of the final test phase. All submissions were evaluated on unseen images, focusing on portraits and intricate subjects with complex and visually appealing bokeh phenomena. In addition to the first track focusing on established quantitative fidelity metrics, we conducted a qualitative user study with a panel of experts for a second track focusing on perceptual assessment. As this was the inaugural challenge on this topic, most of the participants focused on refining and extending the Bokehlicious baseline method.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究展示了NTIRE首届可控散景渲染挑战赛的成果，并重点介绍了最有效的参赛方法。共有44名参与者注册参赛，其中8支团队在最终测试阶段结束后提交了有效解决方案。所有提交的方案均在未见过的图像上进行评估，重点关注肖像和具有复杂且视觉上吸引人的散景现象的精细主体。除了第一个专注于建立定量保真度指标的赛道外，我们还针对第二个专注于感知评估的赛道，组织了一个专家小组进行了定性用户研究。作为该主题的首次挑战赛，大多数参赛者聚焦于改进和扩展Bokehlicious基线方法。

</details>


### [9] [Characterizing Brazilian Atlantic Forest Restoration Outcomes with Geospatial AlphaEarth Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05547)
*Alice Heiman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用卫星嵌入（AlphaEarth Foundation模型）分析巴西大西洋森林1,729个恢复站点，提出“参考轨迹嵌入”概念，基于余弦相似度评估早期恢复成功程度，发现不同土地利用类型在嵌入空间中呈现明显聚类，但信号存在噪声且需进一步微调。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大西洋森林是生物多样性热点，但仅剩12-15%原始覆盖。大规模监测森林恢复至关重要，然而传统方法受限于实地报告的不可行性以及NDVI等遥感指数的饱和效应，且造林是渐进过程，不像毁林那样产生快速的光谱变化。

Method: 使用AlphaEarth Foundation模型的卫星嵌入分析巴西圣保罗1,729个恢复站点；提出“参考轨迹嵌入”概念，通过计算与成熟次生林参考站点的余弦相似度来定义恢复成功度量指标。

Result: 不同土地利用/土地覆盖类型在嵌入空间中呈现明显聚类；能识别出具有清晰变化向量的站点；但信号存在噪声，嵌入可能需要进一步微调才能捕捉和预测超越土地利用分类的站点元数据。

Conclusion: 卫星嵌入方法可有效表征早期恢复成功并区分土地利用类型，但当前信号存在噪声，需要进一步微调以提升对站点元数据的预测能力。

Original Abstract: The Atlantic Forest in Brazil is a critical biodiversity hotspot, yet less than 12-15% of its original cover remains. Although monitoring forest restoration on a large scale is essential, traditional methods are limited by the impracticality of on-the-ground reporting on such a scale and by the saturation of remote-sensing indices such as NDVI. Furthermore, reforestation is a gradual process as opposed to the rapid spectral changes caused by deforestation. In this study, we examine 1,729 restoration sites in São Paulo, using satellite embeddings from the AlphaEarth Foundation's model to evaluate their effectiveness in characterising early restoration success. We introduce the concept of a 'Reference Trajectory Embedding', defining a metric of restoration success based on cosine similarity to reference sites of mature secondary forest. We observe distinct clusters in embedding space according to different land use and land cover (LULC) types, and we can identify sites with clear change vectors. However, the signal can be noisy, and embeddings may require further fine-tuning to capture and predict site metadata beyond LULC.

中文摘要翻译: 巴西大西洋森林是一个关键的生物多样性热点，但其原始覆盖仅剩不到12-15%。尽管大规模监测森林恢复至关重要，但传统方法受到实地报告在此规模上不可行以及NDVI等遥感指数饱和效应的限制。此外，造林是一个渐进过程，不同于毁林造成的快速光谱变化。在本研究中，我们利用AlphaEarth Foundation模型的卫星嵌入，考察了圣保罗的1,729个恢复站点，评估其在表征早期恢复成功方面的有效性。我们引入了"参考轨迹嵌入"的概念，基于与成熟次生林参考站点的余弦相似度，定义了一个恢复成功的度量指标。我们观察到不同土地利用和土地覆盖类型在嵌入空间中呈现明显的聚类，并且能够识别出具有清晰变化向量的站点。然而，信号可能存在噪声，嵌入可能需要进一步微调以捕捉和预测超越土地利用分类的站点元数据。

</details>


### [10] [A Novel Graph-Regulated Disentangling Mamba Model with Sparse Tokens for Enhanced Tree Species Classification from MODIS Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05549)
*Motasem Alkayid,Zhengsen Xu,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Yimin Zhu,Megan Greenwood,Quinn Ledingham,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Naser El-Sheimy,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种图正则化解耦稀疏Mamba模型（GDS-Mamba），用于MODIS时间序列数据的树种分类。该模型通过图正则化增强大规模上下文建模、设计解耦Mamba架构分离时空光谱信息、以及引入稀疏令牌机制提升效率与细粒度特征学习，在两个加拿大省份的大规模实验中取得了优于12个最先进模型的分类性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MODIS时间序列数据的树种分类面临三大挑战：树种间信号差异微弱、空-谱-时信息高度耦合、大规模拓扑上下文信息难以建模。现有方法难以同时有效解决这些问题。

Method: (1) 设计小批量图正则化方法显式建模输入图像间的拓扑相关性，增强大规模上下文建模；(2) 提出解耦Mamba架构，分别捕获MODIS时间序列中的独立空间模式、光谱特征和时间物候行为；(3) 设计新型稀疏令牌方法，自适应学习最优令牌子集，缓解标准Mamba模型中的相关衰减问题。

Result: 基于加拿大阿尔伯塔省和萨斯喀彻温省的大规模年度MODIS数据，在阿尔伯塔省内分类总体精度达93.94%，跨省评估达80.19%，优于12个最先进的分类模型。

Conclusion: GDS-Mamba通过图正则化、解耦Mamba架构和稀疏令牌机制的有机结合，有效解决了MODIS时间序列树种分类中的关键难题，显著提升了分类精度和效率。

Original Abstract: Although tree species classification from Moderate Resolution Imaging Spectroradiometer (MODIS) time series data is critical for supporting various environmental applications, it is a challenging task due to several key difficulties: the subtle signature differences among tree species, strong spatial-spectral-temporal information coupling, and the difficulty of modeling large-scale topological context information. To better address these challenges, this paper presents a novel Graph-regulated Disentangled Sparse Mamba model (GDS-Mamba) for enhanced tree species classification, with the following contributions. (1) First, to improve large-scale context modeling, we design a mini-batch graph-regulated approach that explicitly explores topological correlation effects among input images. (2) Second, to disentangle the high-dimensional spatial-spectral-temporal information coupling for improved feature extraction, we propose a novel disentangling Mamba architecture tailored for capturing independent spatial patterns, spectral signatures, and temporal phenology behaviors in MODIS time series. (3) Third, to improve efficiency and subtle feature learning, we design novel sparse token approaches that adaptively learn the optimum subset of tokens to better address the correlation decay problem that bottlenecks standard Mamba models. Extensive experiments using large-scale annual MOD13Q1 data across two Canadian provinces (i.e., Alberta and Saskatchewan) achieved an overall accuracy of 93.94\% in Alberta and 80.19\% in cross-provincial evaluations, outperforming twelve state-of-the-art classification models.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管基于中分辨率成像光谱仪（MODIS）时间序列数据的树种分类对于支持各种环境应用至关重要，但由于几个关键难点，这仍然是一项具有挑战性的任务：树种间微弱的信号差异、强烈的空-谱-时信息耦合以及大规模拓扑上下文信息难以建模。为了更好地应对这些挑战，本文提出了一种新颖的图正则化解耦稀疏Mamba模型（GDS-Mamba），用于增强树种分类，主要贡献如下：（1）首先，为改进大规模上下文建模，我们设计了一种小批量图正则化方法，显式探索输入图像间的拓扑相关效应；（2）其次，为解耦高维空-谱-时信息耦合以改进特征提取，我们提出了一种新颖的解耦Mamba架构，专门用于捕获MODIS时间序列中的独立空间模式、光谱特征和时间物候行为；（3）第三，为提高效率和细粒度特征学习，我们设计了新颖的稀疏令牌方法，自适应学习最优令牌子集，以更好地解决阻碍标准Mamba模型的相关衰减问题。基于两个加拿大省份（即阿尔伯塔省和萨斯喀彻温省）的大规模年度MODIS数据进行的广泛实验，在阿尔伯塔省实现了93.94%的总体精度，在跨省评估中达到80.19%，优于12个最先进的分类模型。

</details>


### [11] [Text-to-CAD Retrieval: a Strong Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05572)
*Honghu Pan,Zibo Du,Daxiang Liu,Chengliang Liu,Xiaoling Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文正式提出文本到CAD检索这一新任务，并构建了基于Text2CAD数据集的基准；提出统一框架，通过序列编码器（构造逻辑）、点编码器（几何特征）和文本编码器学习多模态CAD嵌入，并引入特征解码器实现隐式多模态对齐，在推理时移除该解码器以实现高效检索。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的CAD模型检索主要依赖文件名或目录，效率、可扩展性和准确性受限，而文本到CAD检索这一跨模态检索任务尚未被正式研究。

Method: 提出统一框架：1）序列编码器捕捉CAD模型的构造逻辑（程序序列）；2）点编码器提取显式几何特征（点云）；3）文本编码器学习查询的语义表示；4）训练时引入特征解码器，通过交叉注意力机制利用文本和点特征重建被掩蔽的序列特征，实现隐式多模态对齐；5）推理时移除该解码器，直接拼接序列-点特征进行高效检索。

Result: 该框架作为文本到CAD检索的强基线，为后续CAD生成范式（如检索增强生成）奠定基础，代码将开源。

Conclusion: 该框架有效实现了文本到CAD的跨模态检索，为工业设计复用提供了新范式，并为下游CAD生成任务提供了基础支持。

Original Abstract: Text-based retrieval of Computer-Aided Design (CAD) models is a critical yet underexplored task for the reuse of legacy industrial designs. Existing CAD repositories are typically searched using filenames or directories, which limits the efficiency, scalability, and accuracy of design retrieval. In this paper, we formally introduce text-to-CAD retrieval as a new cross-modal retrieval task, aiming to retrieve semantically relevant CAD models from large-scale databases given natural language queries. Leveraging paired text-CAD annotations from the Text2CAD dataset, we establish a practical benchmark for this task. To achieve text-based retrieval, we propose a unified framework that learns multi-modal CAD embeddings from both procedural sequences and geometric point clouds. Specifically, a sequence encoder captures the construction logic of CAD models, while a point encoder extracts explicit geometric features. A text encoder is used to learn semantic representations of textual queries. During training, we introduce a novel feature decoder that reconstructs masked sequence features via cross-attention with text and point features, encouraging implicit multi-modal alignment. At inference time, we remove this auxiliary decoder to enable efficient retrieval using concatenated sequence-point features. Our framework serves as a strong baseline for text-to-CAD retrieval and lays the foundation for downstream CAD generation paradigms, such as retrieval-augmented generation. The source code will be released.

中文摘要翻译: 基于文本的计算机辅助设计（CAD）模型检索是一项关键但尚未充分探索的任务，用于复用遗留的工业设计。现有的CAD库通常通过文件名或目录进行搜索，这限制了设计检索的效率、可扩展性和准确性。本文正式将文本到CAD检索定义为一个新的跨模态检索任务，旨在根据自然语言查询从大规模数据库中检索语义相关的CAD模型。借助Text2CAD数据集中的配对文本-CAD标注，我们为该任务建立了一个实用的基准。为了实现基于文本的检索，我们提出了一个统一框架，该框架从程序序列和几何点云中学习多模态CAD嵌入。具体地，序列编码器捕捉CAD模型的构造逻辑，点编码器提取显式几何特征，文本编码器则学习文本查询的语义表示。在训练阶段，我们引入了一种新颖的特征解码器，通过交叉注意力机制利用文本和点特征重建被掩蔽的序列特征，从而促进隐式多模态对齐。在推理阶段，我们移除该辅助解码器，以便利用拼接后的序列-点特征实现高效检索。该框架作为文本到CAD检索的强基线，并为下游CAD生成范式（如检索增强生成）奠定了基础。源代码将公开发布。

</details>


### [12] [Leveraging Image Generators to Address Training Data Scarcity: The Gen4Regen Dataset for Forest Regeneration Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05627)
*Gabriel Jeanson,David-Alexandre Duclos,William Larrivée-Hardy,Noé Cochet,Matěj Boxan,Anthony Deschênes,François Pomerleau,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大型视觉-语言模型（Nano Banana Pro）为细粒度森林再生树种语义分割生成合成训练数据，结合真实数据（WilDReF-Q-V2）和合成数据（Gen4Regen）进行统一训练，在应对数据稀缺和类别严重不平衡问题上取得了显著效果，F1分数提升超过15%，部分稀有树种的F1提升达30%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统地面调查劳动密集且地理受限，无人机数据采集虽可扩展，但深度学习解释受限于专家标注图像的严重稀缺，尤其是在视觉复杂、异质性强的再生区域中，细粒度树种分类面临数据匮乏和极端类别不平衡的双重挑战。

Method: 利用大规模视觉-语言模型Nano Banana Pro从文本提示（prompts）中同时生成高保真图像及其像素级对齐语义掩码；构建了两个数据集：WilDReF-Q-V2（13977张无标签和50张有标签真实图像）和Gen4Regen（2101对合成图像与语义掩码）；将真实数据与AI生成数据联合训练进行语义分割。

Result: 统一训练相比纯监督基线在F1分数上提升超过15%；少量prompt生成的数据即显著提升稀有树种的性能，部分树种每类F1提升高达30%；AI生成数据与真实数据高度互补。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言模型可作为灵活的数据生成器，有效引导专业AI领域（专家标注稀缺或不可用）中的感知任务。

Original Abstract: Sustainable forest management relies on precise species composition mapping, yet traditional ground surveys are labour-intensive and geographically constrained. While Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offer scalable data collection, the transition to deep learning-based interpretation is bottlenecked by the severe scarcity of expert-annotated imagery, particularly in complex, visually heterogeneous regeneration zones. This paper addresses the dual challenges of data scarcity and extreme class imbalance in the semantic segmentation of fine-grained forest regeneration species by providing a scalable framework that reduces reliance on manual photo-interpretation for high-resolution, millimetre-level aerial imagery. Importantly, we leverage the large-scale vision-language Nano Banana Pro model to simultaneously generate high-fidelity images and their corresponding pixel-aligned semantic masks from prompts. We introduce WilDReF-Q-V2, an expansion of a natural forest dataset with 13 977 new unlabelled and 50 labelled real images, as well as the Gen4Regen dataset, featuring 2101 pairs of synthetic images and semantic masks. Our methodology integrates real-world data with AI-generated images, highlighting that AI-generated data is highly complementary to real-world data, with unified training yielding an F1 score improvement of over 15 %pt compared to purely supervised baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate that even small quantities of prompt-generated data significantly improve performance for underrepresented species, some of which saw per-species F1 score gains of up to 30 %pt. We conclude that vision-language models can serve as agile data generators, effectively bootstrapping perception tasks for niche AI domains where expert labels are scarce or unavailable. Our datasets, source code, and models will be available at https://norlab-ulaval.github.io/gen4regen.

中文摘要翻译: 可持续森林管理依赖于精确的树种组成映射，然而传统地面调查劳动密集且受地理条件限制。虽然无人机（UAV）提供了可扩展的数据采集方式，但向基于深度学习的解释过渡受到了专家标注图像严重稀缺的瓶颈制约，尤其是在视觉复杂、异质性强的再生区域。本文针对细粒度森林再生树种语义分割中的数据稀缺和极端类别不平衡的双重挑战，提出了一个可扩展的框架，减少了对高分辨率毫米级航空影像人工照片判读的依赖。重要的是，我们利用大规模视觉-语言模型Nano Banana Pro，从提示（prompts）中同时生成高保真图像及其对应的像素级对齐语义掩码。我们引入了WilDReF-Q-V2，这是一个自然森林数据集的扩展，包含13977张新的无标签图像和50张有标签真实图像，以及Gen4Regen数据集，包含2101对合成图像和语义掩码。我们的方法将真实数据与AI生成图像相结合，突显了AI生成数据与真实数据的高度互补性，统一训练相比纯监督基线在F1分数上提升了超过15个百分点。此外，我们证明了即使少量提示生成的数据也能显著提高稀有树种的性能，其中部分树种的每类F1分数提升高达30个百分点。我们得出结论：视觉-语言模型可作为灵活的数据生成器，在专家标签稀缺或不可用的专业AI领域中有效引导感知任务。我们的数据集、源代码和模型将在https://norlab-ulaval.github.io/gen4regen公开。

</details>


### [13] [AffectSeek: Agentic Affective Understanding in Long Videos under Vague User Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05640)
*Zhen Zhang,Yuhang Yang,Yunxiang Jiang,Yuhuan Lu,Haifeng Lu,Zheng Lian,Runhao Zeng,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出模糊查询驱动的视频情感理解新任务（VQAU），构建了包含长视频、模糊情感查询、时间片段标注、情感标签和理由解释的统一评估基准VQAU-Bench，并提出了一个名为AffectSeek的智能体框架，通过多步骤推理主动定位、验证和解释长视频中的情感片段。实验表明现有模型在该任务上仍有挑战，AffectSeek提供了一种简单有效的方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有情感理解研究主要基于已裁剪好的图像、音频或视频片段，被动地识别情感证据，未能反映用户与长视频交互并通过自然语言查询表达需求的真实场景。因此，本文研究在模糊用户查询驱动下对长视频进行情感理解的更具挑战性的新任务。

Method: 提出AffectSeek智能体框架，将VQAU分解为意图理解、候选定位、片段验证、情感推理和理由生成五个步骤，通过角色专化推理和跨阶段验证逐步对齐模糊用户意图与长视频情感证据。

Result: VQAU任务对现有情感识别模型和单步视觉-语言模型仍具有挑战性，而AffectSeek为智能体驱动的长视频情感理解提供了一个简单但有效的框架。

Conclusion: 本文通过定义新任务VQAU、构建基准VQAU-Bench和提出方法AffectSeek，系统推进了长视频场景下的模糊查询驱动情感理解研究，证明了多步骤智能体推理在该任务上的有效性。

Original Abstract: Existing affective understanding studies have mainly focused on recognizing emotions from images, audio signals, or pre-cliped video clips, where the affective evidence is already given. This passive and clip-centered setting does not fully reflect real-world scenarios, in which users often interact with long videos and express their needs through natural-language queries. In this paper, we study \textbf{Vague-Query-driven video Affective Understanding (VQAU)}, a new task that requires models to localize affective moments in long videos, predict their emotion categories, and generate evidence-grounded rationales under vague user queries. To support this task, we construct \textbf{VQAU-Bench}, a benchmark that integrates long videos, vague affective queries, temporal clip annotations, emotion labels, and rationale explanations into a unified evaluation framework. VQAU-Bench enables systematic assessment of semantic-temporal-affective alignment, affective moment localization, emotion classification, and rationale generation. To address the multi-step reasoning challenges of VQAU, we further propose \textbf{AffectSeek}, an agentic framework that actively seeks, verifies, and explains affective moments in long videos. AffectSeek decomposes VQAU into intent interpretation, candidate localization, clip verification, emotion reasoning, and rationale generation, and progressively aligns vague user intent with long-video evidence through role-specialized reasoning and cross-stage verification. Experiments show that VQAU remains challenging for existing affective recognition models and single-step vision-language models, while AffectSeek provides a simple yet effective framework for agentic long-video affective understanding.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的情感理解研究主要集中于从图像、音频信号或预先裁剪好的视频片段中识别情感，而情感证据在这种设定下是已经给定的。这种被动的、以片段为中心的模式未能完全反映真实场景——用户通常与长视频交互，并通过自然语言查询表达其需求。本文研究了**模糊查询驱动的视频情感理解（VQAU）**，这是一个新任务，要求模型在模糊用户查询下定位长视频中的情感片段、预测情感类别并生成基于证据的推理说明。为支持该任务，我们构建了**VQAU-Bench**，一个将长视频、模糊情感查询、时间片段标注、情感标签和理由解释整合到统一评估框架中的基准数据集。VQAU-Bench能够系统评估语义-时间-情感对齐、情感片段定位、情感分类和理由生成能力。为应对VQAU中的多步骤推理挑战，我们进一步提出**AffectSeek**，一个主动寻找、验证和解释长视频中情感片段的智能体框架。AffectSeek将VQAU分解为意图理解、候选定位、片段验证、情感推理和理由生成等步骤，通过角色专化推理和跨阶段验证逐步对齐模糊用户意图与长视频证据。实验表明，VQAU对现有情感识别模型和单步视觉-语言模型仍具有挑战性，而AffectSeek为智能体驱动的长视频情感理解提供了一个简单而有效的框架。

</details>


### [14] [MUSE: Resolving Manifold Misalignment in Visual Tokenization via Topological Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05646)
*Panqi Yang,Haodong Jing,Jiahao Chao,Tingyan Xiang,Li Lin,Yao Hu,Yang Luo,Yongqiang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MUSE通过拓扑正交性在Transformer中解耦重建梯度与语义梯度，将结构作为正交桥实现像素重建与语义抽象的双向增强，打破了两者之间的零和博弈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 统一视觉tokenization面临高保真像素重建（空间等变性）与语义抽象（概念不变性）之间的根本性权衡，传统联合优化会产生对抗梯度，导致重建与感知之间存在零和博弈。作者将这一冲突归因于流形错位（Manifold Misalignment）。

Method: 提出MUSE框架，基于拓扑正交性（Topological Orthogonality）。将结构（Structure）作为正交桥，在Transformer中解耦优化：结构梯度精炼注意力拓扑，语义梯度更新特征值，将破坏性干扰转化为相互增强（Mutual Reinforcement）。

Result: 打破重建与感知的权衡，生成质量达到SOTA（gFID 3.08）；在linear probing上超越其教师模型InternViT-300M（85.2% vs 82.5%），证明结构对齐的重建可增强语义感知。

Conclusion: 通过拓扑正交性解耦重建与语义优化，可以打破统一视觉tokenization中像素重建与语义抽象之间的固有矛盾，实现两者的相互增强。

Original Abstract: Unified visual tokenization faces a fundamental trade-off between high-fidelity pixel reconstruction (spatial equivariance) and semantic abstraction (conceptual invariance). We attribute this conflict to Manifold Misalignment: naive joint optimization induces opposing gradients, creating a zero-sum game between reconstruction and perception. To address this, we propose MUSE, a framework based on Topological Orthogonality. By treating Structure as an orthogonal bridge, MUSE decouples optimization within Transformers: structural gradients refine attention topology, while semantic gradients update feature values. This turns destructive interference into Mutual Reinforcement. Experiments show that MUSE breaks the trade-off, achieving state-of-the-art generation quality (gFID 3.08) and surpassing its teacher InternViT-300M in linear probing (85.2\% vs. 82.5\%), demonstrating that structurally aligned reconstruction can enhance semantic perception. Code is available at https://github.com/PanqiYang1/MUSE.

中文摘要翻译: 统一的视觉tokenization面临着高保真像素重建（空间等变性）与语义抽象（概念不变性）之间的根本性权衡。我们将这一冲突归因于流形错位（Manifold Misalignment）：朴素的联合优化会诱导出相反的梯度，在重建与感知之间形成零和博弈。为解决这一问题，我们提出MUSE，一个基于拓扑正交性（Topological Orthogonality）的框架。通过将结构（Structure）作为正交桥，MUSE在Transformer内部解耦优化：结构梯度精炼注意力拓扑，而语义梯度更新特征值。这将破坏性干扰转化为相互增强（Mutual Reinforcement）。实验表明，MUSE打破了这一权衡，在生成质量上达到了最先进的水平（gFID 3.08），并在线性探测（linear probing）上超越了其教师模型InternViT-300M（85.2% vs 82.5%），证明结构对齐的重建能够增强语义感知。代码已开源：https://github.com/PanqiYang1/MUSE。

</details>


### [15] [Sparse-to-Complete: From Sparse Image Captures to Complete 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05664)
*Yiyang Shen,Yin Yang,Kun Zhou,Tianjia Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S2C-3D框架，通过场景特化扩散模型、无训练视图一致性采样和相机轨迹规划，仅用6-8张稀疏图像即可实现高保真、完整的3D场景重建，性能优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有稀疏视图3D重建方法在输入图像极少时，容易出现缺失区域、模糊或伪影等问题，无法兼顾高保真度和场景完整性。

Method: 1）场景特化扩散模型：对预训练扩散模型进行微调，使其适应场景分布，修复高斯渲染结果并消除域差异；2）无训练视图一致性采样：量化相邻修复图像间的一致性，将其注入冻结扩散模型的采样过程，生成视图一致的图像；3）相机轨迹规划：将新采样相机连接到两个最近邻，迭代构建路径并保留显著提升可见性的路径，实现全场覆盖。

Result: 实验表明S2C-3D在极稀疏输入（6-8张图像）下优于现有方法，能构建无缺失、无模糊、无伪影的高质量3D场景。

Conclusion: S2C-3D通过场景特化扩散模型、无训练一致性采样和轨迹规划三组件协同，有效解决了稀疏视图下的3D重建难题，实现了高保真、完整的场景重建。

Original Abstract: We introduce S2C-3D, a novel sparse-view 3D reconstruction framework for high-fidelity and complete scene reconstruction from as few as six to eight images. Our framework features three components: a specialized diffusion model for scene-specific image restoration, a training-free view-consistency conditioned sampling process in the diffusion model for refined Gaussian optimization, and a camera trajectory planning scheme to ensure comprehensive scene coverage. The specialized diffusion model is developed by finetuning a pretrained architecture on the input views and their corresponding degraded counterparts. The adaptation to the scene distribution allows the model to repair Gaussian renderings while effectively eliminating domain gaps. Meanwhile, the trajectory planning scheme optimizes scene coverage by connecting each newly sampled camera to its two nearest neighbors. By iteratively constructing paths and retaining only those that significantly enhance visibility, the scheme establishes a trajectory that covers the entire scene. To address multi-view conflicts, the view-consistency conditioned sampling process quantifies the consistency between neighboring repaired images. This information is injected as a condition into the sampling process of the frozen diffusion model, facilitating the generation of view-consistent images without additional training. Consequently, our approach produces high-fidelity 3D Gaussians that are robust to artifacts. Experimental results demonstrate that S2C-3D outperforms state-of-the-art methods, constructing high-quality scenes that are free from missing regions, blurring, or other artifacts with very sparse inputs. The source code and data are available at https://gapszju.github.io/S2C-3D.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出S2C-3D，一种新颖的稀疏视图3D重建框架，能够从仅6到8张图像中实现高保真且完整的场景重建。该框架包含三个组件：用于场景特化图像恢复的专用扩散模型、扩散模型中基于无训练视图一致性条件采样的高斯优化细化过程，以及确保场景全面覆盖的相机轨迹规划方案。专用扩散模型通过对输入视图及其对应退化版本微调预训练架构而开发。对场景分布的适配使得模型能够修复高斯渲染结果，同时有效消除域差异。与此同时，轨迹规划方案通过将每个新采样的相机连接到其两个最近邻来优化场景覆盖。通过迭代构建路径并仅保留那些显著提升可见性的路径，该方案建立起覆盖整个场景的轨迹。为解决多视图冲突，视图一致性条件采样过程量化相邻修复图像之间的一致性，将此信息作为条件注入冻结扩散模型的采样过程，从而无需额外训练即可生成视图一致的图像。因此，我们的方法能够生成对伪影具有鲁棒性的高保真3D高斯表示。实验结果表明，S2C-3D优于现有最先进方法，在极稀疏输入下构建出无缺失区域、无模糊或其他伪影的高质量场景。源代码和数据可在https://gapszju.github.io/S2C-3D获取。

</details>


### [16] [MotionGRPO: Overcoming Low Intra-Group Diversity in GRPO-Based Egocentric Motion Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05680)
*Nanjie Yao,Junlong Ren,Wenhao Shen,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MotionGRPO框架，利用强化学习后训练（GRPO）为扩散过程注入细粒度引导，解决头戴设备信号驱动的全身3D人体运动恢复中局部关节重建误差问题，通过混合奖励机制和噪声注入策略实现SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散的方法依赖全局分布匹配，导致局部关节重建误差较大，缺乏对细粒度运动细节的精确控制。

Method: 将扩散采样建模为马尔可夫决策过程，使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)进行优化；设计混合奖励机制，结合条件感知模型（全局视觉合理性）和显式约束（局部关节精度）；提出噪声注入策略增加样本方差，缓解策略优化中的梯度消失问题。

Result: 实验证明MotionGRPO在全身3D人体运动恢复任务上达到SOTA性能，视觉保真度显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习后训练和噪声注入，可有效提升扩散模型在人体运动恢复中的局部关节精度和全局视觉质量。

Original Abstract: This paper studies full-body 3D human motion recovery from head-mounted device signals. Existing diffusion-based methods often rely on global distribution matching, leading to local joint reconstruction errors. We propose MotionGRPO, a novel framework leveraging reinforcement learning post-training to inject fine-grained guidance into the diffusion process. Technically, we model diffusion sampling as a Markov decision process optimized via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). To this end, we introduce a hybrid reward mechanism that combines a learned conditioned perceptual model for global visual plausibility and explicit constraints for local joint precision. Our key technical insight is that policy optimization in diffusion-based recovery suffers from vanishing gradients due to limited intra-group sample diversity. To address this, we further introduce a noise-injection strategy that explicitly increases sample variance and stabilizes learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MotionGRPO achieves state-of-the-art performance with superior visual fidelity

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究从头戴设备信号中恢复全身3D人体运动。现有基于扩散的方法通常依赖全局分布匹配，导致局部关节重建误差较大。我们提出MotionGRPO，一种利用强化学习后训练为扩散过程注入细粒度引导的新框架。技术上，我们将扩散采样建模为马尔可夫决策过程，并通过组相对策略优化（GRPO）进行优化。为此，我们引入了一种混合奖励机制，该机制结合了用于全局视觉合理性的学习型条件感知模型和用于局部关节精度的显式约束。我们的关键技术洞察在于，基于扩散恢复的策略优化会因组内样本多样性不足而遭受梯度消失问题。针对此问题，我们进一步引入了一种噪声注入策略，显式增加样本方差并稳定学习过程。大量实验表明，MotionGRPO在视觉保真度方面取得了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [17] [CFE-PPAR: Compression-friendly encryption for privacy-preserving action recognition leveraging video transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05692)
*Haiwei Lin,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个压缩友好的加密隐私保护动作识别方法CFE-PPAR，在视频加密后压缩时仍能保持高识别性能和视觉质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于加密的PPAR方法在加密视频被压缩时，识别性能和视觉质量会急剧下降，即这些方法不具备压缩友好性。

Method: 提出CFE-PPAR方法，使用秘密密钥对视频进行加密，并采用相同密钥转换后的参数来驱动视频Transformer直接对加密视频进行识别。

Result: 在UCF101和HMDB51数据集上，分别在Motion-JPEG和H.264压缩条件下，CFE-PPAR的识别性能优于以往方法。

Conclusion: CFE-PPAR有效地解决了加密视频压缩后识别性能下降的问题，是一种压缩友好的、兼具强隐私保护和高识别性能的PPAR方法。

Original Abstract: Privacy-preserving action recognition (PPAR) enables machines to understand human activities in videos without revealing sensitive visual content. Among the various strategies for PPAR, encryption-based methods achieve strong privacy protection while maintaining high recognition performance. However, these methods lead to a catastrophic decrease in recognition performance and visual quality when the encrypted videos are compressed. That is, the previous methods are not compression-friendly. To address these issues, in this paper, we propose the first compression-friendly encryption method for PPAR, called CFE-PPAR. In CFE-PPAR, videos encrypted with secret keys can be directly recognized by a video transformer, which uses parameters transformed by the same keys as those used for video encryption. In experiments, it is verified that CFE-PPAR outperforms previous methods on the UCF101 and HMDB51 datasets under Motion-JPEG and H.264 compression.

中文摘要翻译: 隐私保护动作识别（PPAR）使机器能够在视频中理解人类活动，同时不泄露敏感的视觉内容。在PPAR的各种策略中，基于加密的方法在保持高识别性能的同时实现了强隐私保护。然而，这些方法在加密视频被压缩时会导致识别性能和视觉质量的急剧下降。也就是说，以往的方法不具备压缩友好性。为了解决这些问题，本文提出了首个压缩友好的加密PPAR方法，称为CFE-PPAR。在CFE-PPAR中，使用秘密密钥加密的视频可以直接被视频Transformer识别，该Transformer使用与视频加密相同的密钥转换后的参数。实验结果表明，在Motion-JPEG和H.264压缩条件下，CFE-PPAR在UCF101和HMDB51数据集上的表现均优于以往方法。

</details>


### [18] [Closing the Loop: Unified 3D Scene Generation and Immersive Interaction via LLM-RL Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05711)
*Anh H. Vo,Sungyo Lee,Phil-Joong Kim,Soo-Mi Choi,Yong-Guk Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，将语言驱动的3D场景生成与沉浸式用户交互闭环集成，利用LLM构建场景表示、强化学习优化布局，并在VR中通过人机交互反馈持续优化生成内容，在ALFRED基准上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将3D场景生成与用户交互视为分离过程，导致生成内容缺乏适应性和沉浸感，无法实现闭环反馈优化。

Method: 首先利用LLM根据自然语言指令构建结构化场景表示，然后通过强化学习在几何与语义约束下优化空间布局，最后将生成环境部署到VR中实现人机交互循环，用户交互提供持续反馈以对齐生成内容与人类感知和可用性。

Result: 在ALFRED基准上达到任务导向场景生成的SOTA性能；定性与用户研究显示在沉浸感、交互质量和任务效率方面均有一致改进。

Conclusion: 生成与交互的闭环集成对于下一代多媒体系统至关重要，能够实现更响应、更自适应、更逼真的多媒体体验。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have significantly improved language-driven 3D content generation, but most existing approaches still treat scene generation and user interaction as separate processes, limiting the adaptability and immersive potential of interactive multimedia systems. This paper presents a unified framework that closes the loop between language-driven 3D scene generation and immersive user interaction. Given natural language instructions, the system first constructs structured scene representations using LLMs, and then optimizes spatial layouts via reinforcement learning under geometric and semantic constraints. The generated environments are deployed in a virtual reality setting to facilitate HRI-in-the-loop, where user interactions provide continuous feedback to align generated content with human perception and usability. By tightly coupling generation and interaction, the proposed framework enables more responsive, adaptive, and realistic multimedia experiences. Experiments on the ALFRED benchmark demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in task-based scene generation. Furthermore, qualitative results and user studies show consistent improvements in immersion, interaction quality, and task efficiency, highlighting the importance of closed-loop integration of generation and interaction for next-generation multimedia systems. Our project page can be found at https://proj-showcase.github.io/h3ds/.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLM）的最新进展显著提升了语言驱动的3D内容生成能力，但现有方法大多仍将场景生成与用户交互视为独立过程，限制了交互式多媒体系统的适应性和沉浸潜力。本文提出了一个统一框架，实现语言驱动3D场景生成与沉浸式用户交互之间的闭环。该系统首先利用LLM根据自然语言指令构建结构化场景表示，然后在几何与语义约束下通过强化学习优化空间布局。生成的环境被部署到虚拟现实环境中，以支持人在回路中的人机交互，用户交互提供持续反馈，使生成内容与人类感知和可用性对齐。通过紧密耦合生成与交互，所提出的框架能够实现更响应、更自适应且更逼真的多媒体体验。在ALFRED基准上的实验表明，该方法在任务导向的场景生成中达到了最先进的性能。此外，定性结果和用户研究显示，沉浸感、交互质量和任务效率均得到一致提升，突显了生成与交互闭环集成对于下一代多媒体系统的重要性。我们的项目页面见https://proj-showcase.github.io/h3ds/。

</details>


### [19] [TriRelVLA: Triadic Relational Structure for Generalizable Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05714)
*Hanyu Zhou,Chuanhao Ma,Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TriRelVLA，一种三元关系型视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架，通过构建显式的物体-手-任务三元关系表示和任务驱动的关系图，将关系结构压缩为瓶颈空间输入大语言模型进行动作预测，从而减少对视觉外观统计的依赖，显著提升机器人操作任务在跨场景、跨物体和跨任务组合上的泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLA模型在训练见过的任务上表现良好，但难以泛化到未见过的场景和物体。其根本原因在于隐式视觉表示将物体外观、背景和场景布局纠缠在一起，使得策略对视觉变化敏感。先前的结构化中间表示主要捕捉场景语义而非与动作相关的关系，导致动作预测仍受限于外观统计特征。

Method: 1) 从多模态输入构建显式的物体-手-任务三元组表示作为关系基元；2) 构建任务驱动的关系图，通过任务引导的交叉注意力形成节点，并使用关系感知的图变换器建模节点间交互；3) 执行关系条件化的动作生成，将关系结构压缩到瓶颈空间并投影到大语言模型中进行动作预测。

Result: 实验表明，TriRelVLA在微调任务上表现强劲，并在跨场景、跨物体和跨任务泛化方面取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 三元关系瓶颈表示有效减少了动作预测对视觉外观统计的依赖，使模型能够在场景、物体和任务组合之间实现迁移泛化，为具身操作任务提供了一种通用的可泛化框架。

Original Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models perform well on training-seen robotic tasks but struggle to generalize to unseen scenes and objects. A key limitation lies in their implicit visual representations, which entangle object appearance, background, and scene layout. This makes policies sensitive to visual variations. Prior work improves transferability through structured intermediate representations that objectify visual content. However, these representations mainly capture scene semantics instead of action-relevant relations. As a result, action prediction remains tied to appearance statistics. We observe that manipulation actions depend on the object-hand-task relational structure, which governs interactions among task requirements, robot states, and object properties. Based on this observation, we propose TriRelVLA, a triadic relational VLA framework for generalizable embodied manipulation. Our approach consists of three components: 1) We construct explicit object-hand-task triadic representations from multimodal inputs as relational primitives. 2) We build a task-grounded relational graph. Task-guided cross-attention forms nodes, and a relation-aware graph transformer models interactions among them. 3) We perform relation-conditioned action generation. The relational structure is compressed into a bottleneck space and projected into the LLM for action prediction. This triadic relational bottleneck reduces reliance on appearance statistics and enables transfer across scenes, objects, and task compositions. We further introduce a real-world robotic dataset for fine-tuning. Experiments show strong performance on fine-tuned tasks and clear gains in cross-scene, cross-object, and cross-task generalization.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在训练见过的机器人任务上表现良好，但难以泛化到未见过的场景和物体。一个关键限制在于其隐式视觉表示将物体外观、背景和场景布局纠缠在一起，这使得策略对视觉变化敏感。先前的工作通过结构化中间表示对视觉内容进行对象化来提升迁移性，然而这些表示主要捕捉场景语义而非与动作相关的关系，导致动作预测仍然受限于外观统计特征。我们观察到操作动作依赖于物体-手-任务关系结构，该结构控制着任务需求、机器人状态和物体属性之间的相互作用。基于这一观察，我们提出TriRelVLA，一种用于可泛化具身操作的三元关系VLA框架。我们的方法包含三个组件：1）从多模态输入构建显式的物体-手-任务三元组表示作为关系基元；2）构建任务驱动的关系图，通过任务引导的交叉注意力形成节点，并使用关系感知的图变换器建模节点间交互；3）执行关系条件化的动作生成，将关系结构压缩到瓶颈空间并投影到大语言模型中进行动作预测。这种三元关系瓶颈减少了对视觉外观统计的依赖，使模型能够在场景、物体和任务组合之间实现迁移。我们进一步引入了一个真实世界机器人数据集用于微调。实验表明，该方法在微调任务上表现强劲，并在跨场景、跨物体和跨任务泛化方面取得显著提升。

</details>


### [20] [Ray-Aware Pointer Memory with Adaptive Updates for Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05749)
*Feifei Li,Qi Song,Chi Zhang,Rui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种射线感知的指针记忆（Ray-aware Pointer Memory）机制，用于流式3D重建。该方法通过为每个记忆指针存储3D位置、射线方向和特征嵌入，实现空间位置与视角方向的联合推理，并采用保留或替换的指针更新策略替代传统融合式记忆压缩，从而提升长期重建稳定性和相机位姿精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有前馈式3D重建框架在更新记忆时主要依赖外观相似性，这种外观驱动的集成方式在视点变化时会导致观测冗余累积和几何不稳定，缺乏对空间位置和视角方向的显式建模。

Method: 提出射线感知指针记忆（Ray-aware Pointer Memory），每个指针存储3D位置、关联射线方向和特征嵌入，支持空间邻近性与视角一致性的联合推理。采用保留或替换（retain-or-replace）的自适应指针更新策略，选择性保留信息性指针并丢弃冗余指针。当检测到回环候选时，触发位姿精化以增强全局几何一致性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的射线感知记忆设计显著提升了长期重建稳定性和相机位姿精度，同时保持了高效的流式推理能力。

Conclusion: 该方法为从图像流进行可扩展、无漂移的在线3D重建提供了一种原则性框架，通过显式建模空间位置与视角方向，有效解决了长期重建中的几何漂移和记忆冗余问题。

Original Abstract: Dense 3D reconstruction from continuous image streams requires both accurate geometric aggregation and stable long-term memory management. Recent feed-forward reconstruction frameworks integrate observations through persistent memory representations, yet most rely primarily on appearance-based similarity when updating memory. Such appearance-driven integration often leads to redundant accumulation of observations and unstable geometry when viewpoint changes occur. In this work, we propose a ray-aware pointer memory for streaming 3D reconstruction that explicitly models both spatial location and viewing direction within a unified memory representation. Each memory pointer stores its 3D position, associated ray direction, and feature embedding, allowing the system to reason jointly about geometric proximity and viewpoint consistency. Based on this representation, we introduce an adaptive pointer update strategy that replaces traditional fusion-based memory compression with a retain-or-replace mechanism. Instead of averaging nearby observations, the system selectively retains informative pointers while discarding redundant ones, preserving distinctive geometric structures while maintaining bounded memory growth. Furthermore, the joint reasoning over spatial distance and ray-direction discrepancy enables the system to distinguish between local redundancy, novel observations, and potential loop revisits in a unified manner. When loop candidates are detected, pose refinement is triggered to enforce global geometric consistency across the reconstruction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed ray-aware memory design significantly improves long-term reconstruction stability and camera pose accuracy while maintaining efficient streaming inference. Our approach provides a principled framework for scalable and drift-resistant online 3D reconstruction from image streams.

中文摘要翻译: 从连续图像流中进行密集3D重建既需要精确的几何聚合，也需要稳定的长期记忆管理。近期前馈式重建框架通过持久化记忆表示来整合观测信息，但大多数方法在更新记忆时主要依赖外观相似性。这种外观驱动的集成方式在视点变化时往往导致观测的冗余累积和几何不稳定。本文提出一种用于流式3D重建的射线感知指针记忆（Ray-aware Pointer Memory），在统一的记忆表示中显式建模空间位置和视角方向。每个记忆指针存储其3D位置、关联的射线方向和特征嵌入，使系统能够联合推理几何邻近性和视角一致性。基于该表示，我们引入一种自适应指针更新策略，用保留或替换机制替代传统的融合式记忆压缩。系统不再对邻近观测进行平均，而是选择性保留信息性指针并丢弃冗余指针，在保持记忆规模受限的同时，保留具有判别性的几何结构。此外，对空间距离和射线方向差异的联合推理使系统能够统一区分局部冗余、新观测和潜在回环重访。当检测到回环候选时，触发位姿精化以增强重建的全局几何一致性。大量实验表明，所提出的射线感知记忆设计显著提升了长期重建稳定性和相机位姿精度，同时保持高效的流式推理能力。本文方法为从图像流进行可扩展、无漂移的在线3D重建提供了一种原则性框架。

</details>


### [21] [Jointly Learning Structured Representations and Stabilized Affinity for Human Motion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05753)
*Xianghan Meng,Zhiyuan Huang,Zhengyu Tong,Chun-Guang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TDSC方法，通过联合学习时序一致的结构化表示和稳定的自表达亲和度矩阵，解决真实视频中帧级特征不满足子空间并集假设导致的人体运动分割性能不佳问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于子空间聚类的人体运动分割方法假设高维时序特征符合子空间并集分布，但真实世界视频的帧级特征常违背该假设，导致分割性能不理想。

Method: 提出Temporal Deep Self-expressive subspace Clustering (TDSC)方法：1）通过编码率最大化正则化避免表示坍塌并使学习到的表示符合UoS分布；2）引入时序约束促进相邻帧被划分为同一组；3）设计时序动量平均机制稳定亲和度演化；4）采用重参数化策略实现高效优化。

Result: 在五个基准HMS数据集上，使用传统HoG特征和最新的深度特征（CLIP, DINOv2）进行实验，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的TDSC方法通过联合学习结构化表示和稳定亲和度，有效解决了真实视频中特征分布不符合UoS假设的问题，实现了准确鲁棒的人体运动分割。

Original Abstract: Human Motion Segmentation (HMS), which aims to partition a video into non-overlapping segments corresponding to different human motions, has recently attracted increasing research attention. Existing HMS approaches are predominantly based on subspace clustering, which are grounded on the assumption that the distribution of high-dimensional temporal features well aligns with a Union-of-Subspaces (UoS). For videos in the real world, however, the raw frame-level features often violate the UoS assumption and yield unsatisfactory segmentation performance. To address this issue, we propose an efficient and effective approach for HMS, named Temporal Deep Self-expressive subspace Clustering (TDSC), which jointly learns temporally consistent structured representations and stabilized affinity for accurate and robust HMS. Specifically, in TDSC, we alternately learn structured representations of the input frame features and self-expressive coefficients via a properly regularized self-expressive model, in which a coding-rate maximization regularizer is incorporated to avoid representation collapse and conform the learned representations to span a desired UoS distribution, and meanwhile, temporal constraints are incorporated to promote temporally adjacent frames to be partitioned into the same groups. Moreover, we develop a temporal momentum averaging mechanism to stabilize affinity evolution and design a reparameterization strategy to enable efficient optimization. We conduct extensive experiments on five benchmark HMS datasets using both conventional (HoG) and up-to-date deep features (i.e., CLIP, DINOv2) to validate the effectiveness of our approach.

中文摘要翻译: 人体运动分割旨在将视频划分为对应不同人体运动的非重叠片段，近年来引起了越来越多的研究关注。现有的人体运动分割方法主要基于子空间聚类，其基本假设是高维时序特征的分布与子空间并集（UoS）良好对齐。然而，对于真实世界中的视频，原始帧级特征常常违背UoS假设，导致分割性能不理想。针对这一问题，我们提出了一种高效且有效的人体运动分割方法——时序深度自表达子空间聚类（TDSC），该方法联合学习时序一致的结构化表示和稳定的亲和度，以实现准确鲁棒的人体运动分割。具体而言，在TDSC中，我们通过一个适当正则化的自表达模型交替学习输入帧特征的结构化表示和自表达系数，其中引入了编码率最大化正则化项以避免表示坍塌，并使学习到的表示符合期望的UoS分布；同时引入时序约束，促进时序相邻帧被划分到同一组。此外，我们开发了时序动量平均机制以稳定亲和度的演化，并设计了重参数化策略以实现高效优化。我们在五个基准人体运动分割数据集上，使用传统HoG特征和最新的深度特征（即CLIP、DINOv2）进行了大量实验，验证了所提出方法的有效性。

</details>


### [22] [iTRIALSPACE: Programmable Virtual Lesion Trials for Controlled Evaluation of Lung CT Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05761)
*Fakrul Islam Tushar,Umme Hafsa Momy,Joseph Y. Lo,Geoffrey D. Rubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: iTRIALSPACE是一个用于可控评估肺部CT模型的可编程框架，通过构建虚拟病变试验来替代传统静态基准测试，揭示了模型在固定分布基准下无法发现的性能偏差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有肺部CT评估基准是静态回顾性数据集，病变大小、肺叶分布、解剖结构和采集语境相互纠缠，难以确定什么因素真正驱动了模型精度，因此需要一种可控、可审计的评估基础设施。

Method: 提出四阶段流水线：1）多数据集结节画像（54属性、13140个结节）；2）显式试验规范；3）解剖感知掩膜插入；4）ControlNet条件CT合成。统一了7个公开CT源的结节数据，实例化为13种试验模式。

Result: 在55469样本虚拟病变研究中，合成数据FID在真实数据基线范围内；合成性能排名与真实临床数据高度相关（ρ=0.93, p<10^-15）。发现固定基准无法暴露的问题：肺叶均衡采样下的捷径驱动尺寸预测坍塌，以及双胞胎交叉分析中宿主-供体方差比达8.9倍和3.3倍。

Conclusion: iTRIALSPACE作为一种可审计的评估基础设施，支持超越静态回顾性基准的可控、可证伪的测试。

Original Abstract: We introduce iTRIALSPACE, a programmable evaluation framework for controlled assessment of lung CT models. Standard benchmarks are static retrospective collections that entangle lesion size, lobe prevalence, anatomy, and acquisition context, making it difficult to determine what structurally drives model accuracy. iTRIALSPACE addresses this limitation by composing real clinical CTs and lesion profiles into controlled virtual lesion trials through a four-stage pipeline: multidataset nodule profiling, explicit trial specification, anatomy-aware mask insertion, and ControlNet-conditioned CT synthesis. The framework is built on a unified 54-attribute nodule-profile dataset spanning 13,140 annotated nodules from seven public CT sources and instantiated as 13 trial modes. We evaluate iTRIALSPACE in a 55,469-sample Virtual Lesion Study spanning three medical VLMs, four spatialguidance conditions, and three clinical tasks. Across all 13 modes, the synthetic substrate remains within the real-to-real FID baseline, and synthetic performance rankings transfer strongly to real clinical data ($ρ$ = 0.93, p < 10$^{-15}$). Controlled trial modes expose findings unavailable to fixed-distribution benchmarks, including shortcut-driven size prediction collapse under lobe-equalized sampling and hostto-donor variance ratios of 8.9x and 3.3x in twin-cross analysis. These results position iTRIALSPACE as an auditable evaluation infrastructure for controlled, falsifiable testing beyond static retrospective benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了iTRIALSPACE，一个用于肺部CT模型可控评估的可编程评估框架。标准基准是静态回顾性数据集，将病变大小、肺叶患病率、解剖结构和采集语境纠缠在一起，使得难以确定什么因素在结构上驱动了模型精度。iTRIALSPACE通过将真实临床CT和病变画像组合成可控的虚拟病变试验来解决这一局限性，其四阶段流水线包括：多数据集结节画像、显式试验规范、解剖感知掩膜插入以及ControlNet条件CT合成。该框架建立在一个统一的54属性结节画像数据集上，该数据集涵盖来自7个公开CT来源的13140个标注结节，并实例化为13种试验模式。我们在包含55469个样本的虚拟病变研究中评估了iTRIALSPACE，涵盖三个医学视觉语言模型、四种空间引导条件和三种临床任务。在所有13种模式下，合成基底保持在真实到真实FID基线范围内，且合成性能排名与真实临床数据强相关（ρ=0.93, p<10^-15）。可控试验模式揭示了固定分布基准无法发现的结论，包括在肺叶均衡采样下捷径驱动的尺寸预测坍塌，以及双胞胎交叉分析中宿主-供体方差比达8.9倍和3.3倍。这些结果将iTRIALSPACE定位为一种可审计的评估基础设施，用于超越静态回顾性基准的可控、可证伪测试。

</details>


### [23] [X-OmniClaw Technical Report: A Unified Mobile Agent for Multimodal Understanding and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05765)
*Xiaoming Ren,Ru Zhen,Chao Li,Yang Song,Qiuxia Hou,Yanhao Zhang,Peng Liu,Qi Qi,Quanlong Zheng,Qi Wu,Zhenyi Liao,Binqiang Pan,Haobo Ji,Haonan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了X-OmniClaw，一个统一的移动端智能体，通过集成感知、记忆和动作的统一架构，实现对Android生态系统复杂任务的 multimodal 理解和交互。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 受OpenClaw发展的启发，移动端个人智能体需要具备处理复杂且直观交互的能力，但目前缺乏统一的架构来实现高上下文感知的移动任务处理。

Method: X-OmniClaw包含三个核心模块：1) Omni Perception：统一的多模态输入管道，融合UI状态、真实世界视觉和语音输入，使用时序对齐模块将原始数据转化为结构化多模态意图表示；2) Omni Memory：结合运行时工作记忆和从本地数据中蒸馏的长期个人记忆，增强个性化智能；3) Omni Action：混合接地策略，结合结构XML元数据和视觉感知实现鲁棒交互，通过行为克隆和轨迹回放将用户导航捕获为可复用技能。

Result: 在多种场景下的演示表明，X-OmniClaw有效提升了交互效率和任务可靠性。

Conclusion: X-OmniClaw为下一代移动端原生个人助手提供了实用的架构蓝图。

Original Abstract: Inspired by the development of OpenClaw, there is a growing demand for mobile-based personal agents capable of handling complex and intuitive interactions. In this technical report, we introduce X-OmniClaw, a unified mobile agent designed for multimodal understanding and interaction in the Android ecosystem. This unified architecture of perception, memory, and action enables the agent to handle complex mobile tasks with high contextual awareness. Specifically, Omni Perception provides a unified multimodal ingress pipeline that integrates UI states, real-world visual contexts, and speech inputs, leveraging a temporal alignment module to decompose raw data into structured multimodal intent representations. Omni Memory leverages multimodal memory optimization to enhance personalized intelligence by integrating runtime working memory for task continuity with long-term personal memory distilled from local data, enabling highly context-aware and personalized interactions. Finally, Omni Action employs a hybrid grounding strategy that combines structural XML metadata with visual perception for robust interaction. Through Behavior Cloning and Trajectory Replay, the system captures user navigation as reusable skills, enabling precise direct-access execution. Demonstrations across diverse scenarios show that X-OmniClaw effectively enhances interaction efficiency and task reliability, providing a practical architectural blueprint for the next generation of mobile-native personal assistants.

中文摘要翻译: 受OpenClaw发展的启发，人们对能够处理复杂且直观交互的移动端个人智能体的需求日益增长。在本技术报告中，我们介绍了X-OmniClaw，一个专为Android生态系统中的多模态理解和交互而设计的统一移动智能体。这种感知、记忆和动作的统一架构使智能体能够以高上下文感知能力处理复杂的移动任务。具体来说，Omni Perception提供了一个统一的多模态输入管道，融合了UI状态、真实世界视觉上下文和语音输入，利用时序对齐模块将原始数据分解为结构化的多模态意图表示。Omni Memory利用多模态记忆优化，通过整合用于任务连续性的运行时工作记忆与从本地数据中蒸馏的长期个人记忆，增强个性化智能，从而实现高度上下文感知和个性化的交互。最后，Omni Action采用了一种混合接地策略，将结构化的XML元数据与视觉感知相结合，实现鲁棒的交互。通过行为克隆和轨迹回放，系统将用户导航捕捉为可复用的技能，实现精准的直接访问执行。跨多种场景的演示表明，X-OmniClaw有效提升了交互效率和任务可靠性，为下一代移动端原生个人助手提供了实用的架构蓝图。

</details>


### [24] [Steering Visual Generation in Unified Multimodal Models with Understanding Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05781)
*Zeyu Liu,Zanlin Ni,Yang Yue,Cheng Da,Huan Yang,Di Zhang,Kun Gai,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UNO轻量框架，通过将理解作为生成任务的监督信号（编码语义与结构信息），恢复多模态模型中理解与生成之间的协同作用，实验证明理解能有效促进图像生成与编辑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型虽性能优越，但理解和生成组件高度解耦，削弱了两者之间的相互增强潜力，这种协同作用在实证上仍不确定。

Method: 提出理解导向的后训练（UNO）框架，将理解不仅作为独立任务，更作为生成表示的监督信号：通过融入字幕编码（语义抽象）和视觉回归（结构细节）两种目标，使梯度从理解流向生成。

Result: 在图像生成和编辑任务上的大量实验表明，理解可以作为生成的有效催化剂。

Conclusion: 理解任务能够作为生成任务的有效催化剂，UNO通过轻量后训练恢复了理解与生成之间的协同作用。

Original Abstract: Unified multimodal models are envisioned to bridge the gap between understanding and generation. Yet, to achieve competitive performance, state-of-the-art models adopt largely decoupled understanding and generation components. This design, while effective for individual tasks, weakens the connection required for mutual enhancement, leaving the potential synergy empirically uncertain. We propose to explicitly restore this synergy by introducing Understanding-Oriented Post-Training (UNO), a lightweight framework that treats understanding not only as a distinct task, but also a direct supervisory signal to steer generative representations. By incorporating objectives that encode semantic abstraction (captioning) and structural details (visual regression), we enable effective gradient flow from understanding to generation. Extensive experiments on image generation and editing demonstrate that understanding can serve as an effective catalyst for generation.

中文摘要翻译: 统一多模态模型被设想为弥合理解与生成之间的鸿沟。然而，为了达到有竞争力的性能，最先进的模型采用了高度解耦的理解与生成组件。这种设计虽然对单个任务有效，却削弱了相互增强所需的连接，使得潜在的协同作用在实证上不确定。我们提出通过引入理解导向的后训练（UNO）来显式恢复这种协同作用，这是一个轻量级框架，将理解不仅视为一项独立的任务，还将其作为直接监督信号来引导生成表示。通过融入编码语义抽象（字幕生成）和结构细节（视觉回归）的目标，我们实现了从理解到生成的有效梯度流动。在图像生成和编辑上的大量实验表明，理解可以作为生成的有效催化剂。

</details>


### [25] [Na-IRSTD: Enhancing Infrared Small Target Detection via Native-Resolution Feature Selection and Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05804)
*Qian Xu,Chi Zhang,Qiming Zhang,Xi Li,Haojuan Yuan,Mingjin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Na-IRSTD，一种原生分辨率特征提取与融合框架，用于红外小目标检测。通过保留原生分辨率细节特征并引入令牌缩减与选择策略，在降低计算负担的同时显著提升小目标定位精度，在四个基准数据集上达到最先进水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有红外小目标检测方法通常采用常规下采样策略，导致小目标的细节特征被丢弃，定位性能受限。因此，作者希望设计一种能够在原生分辨率下保留目标细微线索、同时不引入过大计算负担的检测框架。

Method: 提出Na-IRSTD框架，包含两部分：(1) 原生分辨率特征提取与融合模块，保留小目标的细微线索，克服分辨率限制；(2) 高效的令牌缩减与选择策略，精准选择目标补丁，增强低层细节特征，同时有效减少原生分辨率下的令牌数量，避免密集处理带来的计算负担。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上的大量实验证明了令牌缩减与选择策略的鲁棒性和有效性；Na-IRSTD模型在四个基准测试上均取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: Na-IRSTD通过原生分辨率特征处理和智能令牌缩减策略，成功解决了红外小目标检测中细节丢失与计算效率之间的矛盾，是一个有效且鲁棒的检测框架。

Original Abstract: Infrared small target detection (IRSTD) faces the inherent challenge of precisely localizing dim targets amid complex background clutter. While progress has been made, existing methods usually follow conventional strategies to downsample features and discard small targets' details, resulting in suboptimal performance. In this paper, we present Na-IRSTD, a native-resolution feature extraction and fusion framework for IRSTD. This framework elegantly incorporates native-resolution features to preserve subtle target cues, overcoming the resolution limitations of existing infrared approaches and significantly improving the model's ability to localize small targets. We also introduce an effective token reduction and selection strategy, which selects target patches with high accuracy and confidence, boosting the low-level details of the feature while effectively reducing native-resolution patch tokens compared to dense processing, thereby avoiding imposing an unbearable computational burden. Extensive experiments demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of our token reduction and selection strategy across multiple public datasets. Ultimately, our Na-IRSTD model achieves state-of-the-art performance on four benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 红外小目标检测（IRSTD）面临着在复杂背景杂波中精确定位暗淡目标的固有挑战。尽管已有进展，现有方法通常遵循常规策略进行特征下采样，从而丢弃了小目标的细节信息，导致性能欠佳。本文提出Na-IRSTD，一种用于红外小目标检测的原生分辨率特征提取与融合框架。该框架优雅地整合了原生分辨率特征以保留目标的细微线索，克服了现有红外方法的分辨率限制，显著提升了模型对小目标的定位能力。我们还引入了一种有效的令牌缩减与选择策略，该策略以高精度和高置信度选择目标补丁，在增强特征低层细节的同时，相较于密集处理有效减少了原生分辨率下的补丁令牌数量，从而避免了带来难以承受的计算负担。大量实验证明了我们的令牌缩减与选择策略在多个公开数据集上的鲁棒性和有效性。最终，我们的Na-IRSTD模型在四个基准测试上取得了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [26] [CXR-ContraBench: Benchmarking Negated-Option Attraction in Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05810)
*Zhengru Fang,Yanan Ma,Yu Guo,Senkang Hu,Yixian Zhang,Hangcheng Cao,Wenbo Ding,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现医学视觉语言模型在胸部X光片中会出现“极性反转”错误（即图片有病变但模型回答“无病变”），构建了CXR-ContraBench基准测试集，并提出QCCV-Neg方法无需重新训练即可修复此类错误。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在面对胸部X光片中的实变征象时，即使视觉证据和问题都指向阳性发现，模型仍可能选择带否定的选项（如“无实变”），这种极性反转会产生与影像相矛盾的临床陈述，构成严重的临床风险。现有研究对此类失败缺乏系统分析和基准测试。

Method: (1) 构建CXR-ContraBench基准，包含内部ReXVQA切片和外部OpenI、CheXpert协议，聚焦“存在发现”问题（选择“无X”构成主要临床风险）和“缺失发现”问题（测试模型是否仅复制否定措辞）。(2) 提出QCCV-Neg（Question-Conditioned Consistency Verifier for Negation），通过确定性方式修复被检测到的极性混淆子集，无需重新训练。

Result: 在CheXpert协议上，MedGemma和Qwen2.5-VL在直接存在性探针上分别仅达31.49%和30.21%准确率；在135,754条记录的CheXpert训练集协议上，两个模型在超过62%的存在性问题上选择了否定选项。链式思维提示可减少部分存在侧反转但无法消除，且会放大缺失侧矛盾。QCCV-Neg将MedGemma和Qwen2.5-VL在直接存在性探针上的准确率提升至96.60%和95.32%。

Conclusion: 标准准确率指标会掩盖临床上重要的推理时极性反转失败。QCCV-Neg无需重新训练即可有效修复此类错误，表明该问题可以通过推理时的一致性验证来缓解。

Original Abstract: When a chest X-ray shows consolidation but the question asks which finding is present, a medical vision-language model may answer "No consolidation." This is more than an incorrect choice: it is a polarity reversal that emits a clinical statement contradicting the image. We study this failure as negated-option attraction, where a model is drawn to a negated answer option even when it conflicts with both the visual evidence and the question. We introduce CXR-ContraBench (Chest X-Ray Contradiction Benchmark), a diagnostic benchmark spanning internal ReXVQA slices and external OpenI and CheXpert protocols. The benchmark centers on present-finding questions, where selecting "No X" despite visible X creates the main clinical risk, and uses absent-finding questions as secondary tests of whether models copy negated wording. Across CheXpert protocols, the failure is substantial and persistent. On a strict direct presence probe, MedGemma and Qwen2.5-VL reach only 31.49% and 30.21% accuracy, respectively; on a matched 135,754-record CheXpert training-split protocol, both models select negated options on over 62% of presence questions. Chain-of-thought prompting reduces some presence-side reversals but does not eliminate them and can amplify absence-side contradictions. Finally, QCCV-Neg (Question-Conditioned Consistency Verifier for Negation) deterministically repairs the measured polarity-confused subset without retraining, raising MedGemma and Qwen2.5-VL to 96.60% and 95.32% accuracy on the direct presence probe. These results show that standard accuracy can hide a clinically meaningful inference-time polarity failure. Source code and benchmark construction scripts are available at https://github.com/fangzr/cxr-contrabench-code.

中文摘要翻译: 当胸部X光片显示实变征象，但问题询问存在何种发现时，医学视觉语言模型可能回答"无实变"。这不仅仅是选择错误：它是一种极性反转，发出了与影像相矛盾的临床陈述。我们将这种失败研究为"否定选项吸引"现象，即模型被吸引到带否定的答案选项，即使该选项与视觉证据和问题均相冲突。我们引入CXR-ContraBench（胸部X光矛盾基准），一个涵盖内部ReXVQA切片和外部OpenI与CheXpert协议的诊断基准。该基准聚焦于"存在发现"问题，其中选择"无X"尽管可见X构成了主要临床风险，并使用"缺失发现"问题作为模型是否仅复制否定措辞的辅助测试。在CheXpert协议上，该失败显著且持续。在严格的直接存在性探针上，MedGemma和Qwen2.5-VL分别仅达到31.49%和30.21%的准确率；在匹配的135,754条记录的CheXpert训练集协议上，两个模型在超过62%的存在性问题上选择了否定选项。链式思维提示减少了部分存在侧反转但未能消除，且可能放大缺失侧矛盾。最后，QCCV-Neg（面向否定的问题条件一致性验证器）以确定性方式修复被检测到的极性混淆子集，无需重新训练，将MedGemma和Qwen2.5-VL在直接存在性探针上的准确率提升至96.60%和95.32%。这些结果表明，标准准确率可能隐藏临床上有意义的推理时极性失败。源代码和基准构建脚本可在https://github.com/fangzr/cxr-contrabench-code获取。

</details>


### [27] [ChartZero: Synthetic Priors Enable Zero Shot Chart Data Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05820)
*Md Touhidul Islam,Yasir Mahmud,Sujan Kumar Saha,Mark Tehranipoor,Farimah Farahmandi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChartZero 是一个利用合成数据先验实现零样本图表数据提取的解析框架，通过全局正交实例损失和开放词汇 VLM 引导的图例匹配策略，解决了曲线断裂和语义关联两大关键问题，并在无真实世界监督的情况下显著提升了泛化图表数字化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图表数据提取方法高度依赖昂贵的人工标注，难以泛化至多样的视觉风格和网格布局；同时存在曲线断裂（细交叉曲线结构碎片化）和语义关联错误（基于空间启发式的图例匹配在真实场景中鲁棒性差）两大关键失效模式，且缺乏端到端重建的评估指标。

Method: 提出 ChartZero 框架：1）仅基于简单数学函数合成的纯合成数据集训练，完全绕过真实标注瓶颈；2）提出全局正交实例（GOI）损失，解决曲线断裂与细粒度视觉细节丢失问题；3）采用开放词汇的视觉-语言模型（VLM）引导的图例匹配策略，替代脆弱的空间规则；4）配套提出专门面向端到端重建的新指标和基准。

Result: 评估表明 ChartZero 在无真实世界监督的情况下显著推进了泛化图表数字化性能，在端到端重建任务上表现优异。

Conclusion: ChartZero 通过合成先验、GOI 损失和 VLM 引导策略，成功实现了零样本图表数据提取，突破了真实标注瓶颈，并解决了现有方法在曲线完整性和语义关联上的根本缺陷。

Original Abstract: Automated data extraction from line charts remains fundamentally bottlenecked by extreme stylistic diversity and a severe scarcity of comprehensively annotated, real-world datasets. Current end-to-end pipelines depend heavily on costly manual annotations, crippling their ability to generalize across arbitrary aesthetics and grid layouts. Furthermore, existing models suffer from two critical failure modes during reconstruction. First, extracting thin, intersecting curves frequently causes structural fragmentation and the erasure of fine visual details, as standard architectures struggle against complex backgrounds. Second, semantic association is notoriously error-prone; current pipelines rely on rigid spatial heuristics that easily break down against the unpredictable legend placements of in-the-wild charts. Finally, measuring true progress is hindered by evaluation protocols that assess isolated sub-tasks rather than holistic, end-to-end data reconstruction. To address these foundational issues, we introduce ChartZero, a parsing framework that leverages synthetic priors to enable robust zero-shot chart data extraction. By training exclusively on a purely synthetic dataset of simple mathematical functions, our model completely bypasses the real-world annotation bottleneck. We overcome curve fragmentation via a novel Global Orthogonal Instance (GOI) loss, and replace brittle spatial rules with an open-vocabulary, Vision-Language Model (VLM)-guided legend matching strategy. Accompanied by a new metric and benchmark specifically designed for full end-to-end reconstruction, our evaluations demonstrate that ChartZero significantly advances generalized plot digitization without requiring real-world supervision. Code and dataset will be released upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 从折线图中自动提取数据始终受限于极端的样式多样性以及严重缺乏全面标注的真实世界数据集。当前的端到端管道高度依赖昂贵的人工标注，严重制约了其在任意美学风格和网格布局上的泛化能力。此外，现有模型在重建过程中存在两个关键的失效模式。首先，提取细且交叉的曲线常导致结构碎片化和细粒度视觉细节的丢失，因为标准架构难以应对复杂背景。其次，语义关联容易出错；当前管道依赖刚性的空间启发式规则，在面对真实图表中不可预测的图例位置时很容易失效。最后，衡量真实进展受到评估协议的限制——这些协议评估的是孤立的子任务而非整体的端到端数据重建。为解决这些根本性问题，我们提出了 ChartZero，一个利用合成先验实现鲁棒的零样本图表数据提取的解析框架。通过仅在简单数学函数的纯合成数据集上训练，我们的模型完全绕过了真实世界标注瓶颈。我们通过新颖的全局正交实例（GOI）损失克服了曲线断裂问题，并用开放词汇的视觉-语言模型（VLM）引导的图例匹配策略替代了脆弱的空间规则。配合专门为完整端到端重建设计的新指标和基准，我们的评估表明 ChartZero 在无需真实世界监督的情况下显著推进了泛化图表数字化。代码和数据集将在论文被接收后发布。

</details>


### [28] [Unifying Scientific Communication: Fine-Grained Correspondence Across Scientific Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05831)
*Megha Mariam K. M,Vineeth N. Balasubramanian,C. V. Jawahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了多模态会议数据集（MCD），这是首个将研究论文、演示视频、讲解视频和幻灯片整合在一起的基准数据集，用于评估嵌入模型和视觉-语言模型在跨格式细粒度对齐上的能力。实验表明视觉-语言模型鲁棒但细粒度对齐能力不足，嵌入模型在文本-视觉对应上表现良好但对公式和符号内容处理不佳。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 科学知识传播日益呈现多模态化，涉及论文、幻灯片、演讲录像等多种形式，但这些材料之间缺乏结构化关联，导致难以追溯概念、视觉元素和解释之间的对应关系，限制了研究内容的统一探索与分析。

Method: 构建MCD基准数据集，整合同一研究工作的论文、演示视频、讲解视频和幻灯片；在此基础上评估多种基于嵌入的模型和视觉-语言模型在跨格式细粒度对应发现任务上的表现。

Result: 视觉-语言模型表现鲁棒但在细粒度对齐上存在困难；基于嵌入的模型能较好捕获文本-视觉对应关系，但公式和符号内容在嵌入空间中形成独立聚类，对齐效果不佳。

Conclusion: 当前多模态科学理解方法各有优劣，未来研究需在细粒度跨格式对齐方面进行改进，特别是对公式和符号等特殊内容的处理。

Original Abstract: The communication of scientific knowledge has become increasingly multimodal, spanning text, visuals, and speech through materials such as research papers, slides, and recorded presentations. These different representations collectively convey a study's reasoning, results, and insights, offering complementary perspectives that enrich understanding. However, despite their shared purpose, such materials are rarely connected in a structured way. The absence of explicit links across formats makes it difficult to trace how concepts, visuals, and explanations correspond, limiting unified exploration and analysis of research content. To address this gap, we introduce the Multimodal Conference Dataset (MCD), the first benchmark that integrates research papers, presentation videos, explanatory videos, and slides from the same works. We evaluate a range of embedding-based and vision-language models to assess their ability to discover fine-grained cross-format correspondences, establishing the first systematic benchmark for this task. Our results show that vision-language models are robust but struggle with fine-grained alignment, while embedding-based models capture text-visual correspondences well but equations and symbolic content form distinct clusters in the embedding space. These findings highlight both the strengths and limitations of current approaches and point to key directions for future research in multimodal scientific understanding. To ensure reproducibility, we release the resources for MCD at https://github.com/meghamariamkm2002/MCD

中文摘要翻译: 科学知识的传播日益呈现多模态化，通过研究论文、幻灯片和录制的演示文稿等材料，跨越文本、视觉和语音等形式。这些不同的表征共同传达了一项研究的推理过程、结果和见解，提供了互补的视角，丰富了理解。然而，尽管它们目标一致，这些材料却很少以结构化方式相互关联。跨格式之间缺乏显式链接，使得追踪概念、视觉元素和解释之间的对应关系变得困难，限制了对研究内容的统一探索与分析。为了解决这一空白，我们提出了多模态会议数据集（MCD），这是首个将同一研究工作的研究论文、演示视频、讲解视频和幻灯片整合在一起的基准数据集。我们评估了多种基于嵌入的模型和视觉-语言模型，以考察它们发现跨格式细粒度对应的能力，建立了该任务的第一个系统基准。结果表明，视觉-语言模型鲁棒性强但在细粒度对齐上存在困难，而基于嵌入的模型能较好捕获文本-视觉对应关系，但公式和符号内容在嵌入空间中形成独立聚类。这些发现揭示了当前方法的优势与局限，并为多模态科学理解领域的未来研究指明了关键方向。为确保可复现性，我们在 https://github.com/meghamariamkm2002/MCD 发布了MCD的相关资源。

</details>


### [29] [VideoRouter: Query-Adaptive Dual Routing for Efficient Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05848)
*Kuanwei Lin,Wenhao Zhang,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoRouter提出了一个基于InternVL的查询自适应双路由框架，通过语义路由器和图像路由器实现预算化的证据分配，在长视频推理中实现高达67.9%的token压缩，同时保持甚至提升任务性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长视频推理中视觉token序列过长导致计算瓶颈，现有压缩方法要么是弱查询感知的，要么对所有帧应用固定压缩策略，在视觉证据随时间不均匀分布时效果不佳。

Method: VideoRouter包含两个核心组件：1) 语义路由器（Semantic Router）预测主导分配策略，在广泛时间覆盖和自适应高分辨率保留之间选择；2) 图像路由器（Image Router）利用早期LLM层对帧相关性进行评分，对不相关帧激进压缩，对关键证据帧保留细节。同时构建了Video-QTR-10K和Video-FLR-200K两个数据集用于路由器的监督训练。

Result: 在VideoMME、MLVU和LongVideoBench等基准测试上，VideoRouter在相同或更低预算下持续优于InternVL基线，实现了高达67.9%的token缩减。

Conclusion: 查询自适应的双路由框架能够有效解决长视频推理中的token预算分配问题，通过动态调整压缩策略显著降低计算开销而不牺牲性能。

Original Abstract: Video large multimodal models increasingly face a scalability bottleneck: long videos produce excessively long visual-token sequences, which sharply increase memory and latency during inference. While existing compression methods are effective in specific settings, most are either weakly query-aware or apply a fixed compression policy across frames, proving suboptimal when visual evidence is unevenly distributed over time. To address this, we present VideoRouter, a query-adaptive dual-router framework built on InternVL for budgeted evidence allocation. The Semantic Router predicts the dominant allocation policy, choosing between broad temporal coverage and adaptive high-resolution preservation, while the Image Router uses early LLM layers to score frame relevance. This enables aggressive compression on less relevant frames while preserving detail on critical evidence frames. To train both routers, we build Video-QTR-10K for allocation-policy supervision and Video-FLR-200K for frame-relevance supervision. Experiments on VideoMME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench show that VideoRouter consistently improves over the InternVL baseline under comparable or lower budgets, achieving up to a 67.9% token reduction.

中文摘要翻译: 视频大型多模态模型日益面临可扩展性瓶颈：长视频产生过长的视觉标记序列，在推理过程中急剧增加内存和延迟。虽然现有压缩方法在特定场景下有效，但大多数方法要么是弱查询感知的，要么对所有帧应用固定压缩策略，在视觉证据随时间不均匀分布时表现不佳。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了VideoRouter——一个基于InternVL构建的查询自适应双路由框架，用于预算化的证据分配。语义路由器预测主导分配策略，在广泛时间覆盖和自适应高分辨率保留之间进行选择，而图像路由器利用早期大语言模型层对帧相关性进行评分。这使得模型能够对不相关的帧进行激进压缩，同时保留关键证据帧的细节。为了训练这两个路由器，我们构建了Video-QTR-10K用于分配策略监督，以及Video-FLR-200K用于帧相关性监督。在VideoMME、MLVU和LongVideoBench上的实验表明，VideoRouter在相同或更低预算下持续优于InternVL基线，实现了高达67.9%的标记缩减。

</details>


### [30] [Align3D-AD: Cross-Modal Feature Alignment and Dual-Prompt Learning for Zero-shot 3D Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05850)
*Letian Bai,Xuanming Cao,Juan Du,Chengyu Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Align3D-AD提出了一种统一的两阶段框架，通过跨模态特征对齐和模态感知提示学习，解决零样本3D异常检测中渲染表示与RGB编码器之间的域差异问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有零样本3D异常检测方法主要依赖将3D观测投影为多视图表示，但这些表示主要捕捉几何线索而非真实视觉语义，且使用预训练于RGB数据的视觉编码器处理时存在显著的域差异，导致语义理解受限。

Method: 提出Align3D-AD两阶段框架：1) 跨模态特征对齐范式，将渲染特征映射到RGB语义空间，并引入语义一致性重加权策略优化局部区域对齐；2) 模态感知提示学习框架，为RGB对齐特征和渲染特征分配独立提示，通过双提示对比对齐增强互补语义与判别能力。

Result: 在MVTec3D-AD、Eyecandies和Real3D-AD三个数据集上，在一对多和跨数据集两种设置下均一致优于现有零样本方法，展现了优异的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过RGB模态作为跨模态指导，有效弥合了渲染特征与RGB编码器之间的域差异，显著提升了零样本3D异常检测的性能和通用性。

Original Abstract: Zero-shot 3D anomaly detection aims to identify anomalies without access to training data from target categories. However, existing methods mainly rely on projecting 3D observations into multi-view representations that primarily capture geometric cues rather than realistic visual semantics and process them with vision encoders pretrained on RGB data, leading to a significant domain gap between the encoder and the projected representations. To address this issue, we propose Align3D-AD, a unified two-stage framework that leverages the RGB modality from auxiliary categories as cross-modal guidance for zero-shot 3D anomaly detection. First, we introduce a cross-modal feature alignment paradigm that maps rendering features into the RGB semantic space. Unlike prior works that implicitly rely on pretrained encoders, our method enables direct semantic transfer from RGB observations. A semantic consistency reweighting strategy is further introduced to refine feature alignment by reweighting local regions according to holistic semantic consistency. Second, we propose a modality-aware prompt learning framework with dual-prompt contrastive alignment. By assigning independent prompts to RGB-aligned and rendering features, our method captures complementary semantics across modalities, while the contrastive alignment further enhances prompt representations to improve discriminability.
