Method: 对于非齐次半空间，采用标准一阶VC分析并证明其紧性；对于二维齐次半空间，在可实现情形下直接证明一致假设的误差为O(1/n)；在不可知情形下，提出"临界楔形局部化"（critical-wedge localization）论证，得到每个二进风险带上的无对数项的偏差界，再通过联合带间分析引入ln ln n的额外代价。

Result: 非齐次半空间：可实现情形下一致假设的泛化误差为Θ(d ln(n/d)/n)，不可知情形下偏差随真实误差τ按√(τ ln(1/τ))缩放，标准VC界本质上是紧的。二维齐次半空间：可实现情形下误差为O(1/n)；不可知情形下得到带间无对数的偏差界，联合后仅增加ln ln n代价，且匹配下界表明该代价不可避免。

Conclusion: 半空间的均匀收敛行为存在尖锐的维度和结构性阈值：齐次性与维度共同决定了收敛速率，二维齐次半空间比一般非齐次半空间具有本质更优的泛化保证，从而给出了该问题近乎完整的精细刻画。

Original Abstract: We study the fine-grained uniform convergence behavior of halfspaces beyond worst-case VC bounds. For inhomogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with $d\ge 2$, we show that standard first-order VC bounds are essentially tight: even consistent hypotheses can incur population error $Θ(d\ln(n/d)/n)$, and in the agnostic setting the deviation scales as $\sqrt{τ\ln(1/τ)}$ at true error $τ$. In contrast, homogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^2$ exhibit a markedly different behavior. In the realizable case, every hypothesis consistent with the sample has error $O(1/n)$. In the agnostic case, we prove a bandwise, log-free deviation bound on each dyadic risk band via a critical-wedge localization argument. Unioning over bands incurs only a $\ln\ln n$ overhead, and we establish a matching lower bound showing this overhead is unavoidable. Together, these results give a fine-grained and nearly complete picture of uniform convergence for halfspaces, revealing sharp dimensional and structural thresholds.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了半空间在最坏情形VC界之外的细粒度均匀收敛行为。对于$\mathbb{R}^d$（$d\ge 2$）中的非齐次半空间，我们证明标准的一阶VC界本质上是紧的：即使是一致假设也可能产生$Θ(d\ln(n/d)/n)$的总体误差，而在不可知情形下，偏差在真实误差$τ$处以$\sqrt{τ\ln(1/τ)}$的规模缩放。相比之下，$\mathbb{R}^2$中的齐次半空间表现出显著不同的行为。在可实现情形下，每个与样本一致的一致假设的误差为$O(1/n)$。在不可知情形下，我们通过一种临界楔形局部化论证，证明了每个二进风险带上无对数项的带间偏差界。对带进行联合仅引入$\ln\ln n$的额外代价，并且我们建立了一个匹配的下界，表明该额外代价是不可避免的。综合这些结果，我们给出了半空间均匀收敛的细粒度且近乎完整的图景，揭示了尖锐的维度和结构性阈值。

</details>


### [152] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机Hadamard变换（RHT）在量化预处理中的最坏情况性能，证明了组合两个RHT可使坐标边缘分布接近高斯分布（误差O(d^{-1/2})），组合三个RHT可衰减坐标协方差，从而在理论上匹配均匀随机旋转（URR）的性能。此外，提出了一种线性时间的动态检查方法，根据输入矩自适应调整RHT数量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 均匀随机旋转（URR）在量化预处理中效果良好但计算开销大，实际中常被快速Hadamard变换（RHT）替代。然而，单个RHT在最坏情况下性能不佳，单个坐标分布可能远离理想的高斯分布或Beta分布。本文旨在证明组合多个RHT能否在理论上逼近URR的性能，并解决其在向量量化（VQ）等场景中的不足。

Method: 1. 理论分析两个RHT组合后，每个固定坐标的归一化旋转向量的边际分布在Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离上达到O(d^{-1/2})的高斯逼近。2. 将上述界应用于DRIVE和QUIC-FL等压缩方案的分析。3. 证明三个RHT组合可导致坐标协方差衰减，使得针对URR优化的向量量化（VQ）码本在使用三个RHT时具有相同的期望误差（附加项随维度消失）。4. 提出一种线性时间O(d)的输入矩检查方法，动态调整运行时使用的RHT数量。

Result: 两个RHT组合可在O(d^{-1/2})误差内逼近高斯分布，在DRIVE和QUIC-FL方案中渐近匹配URR性能。三个RHT组合可确保坐标协方差衰减，使VQ码本的期望误差与URR一致（附加项随维度消失）。提出的动态检查方法可在线性时间内自适应调整RHT数量。

Conclusion: 组合多个RHT（2个或3个）可以在最坏情况下理论上匹配URR的性能，为实际量化预处理提供了高效且可靠的替代方案。同时，动态自适应方法进一步提升了实际部署中的性能。

Original Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs. With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

中文摘要翻译: 均匀随机旋转（URR）是现代量化方法中常见的预处理步骤，广泛应用于梯度压缩、推理加速、KV缓存压缩、模型权重量化以及向量数据库中的近似最近邻搜索。实际上，URR常被随机Hadamard变换（RHT）替代，后者保持正交性同时支持快速实现。遗留问题是最坏情况输入下的性能。使用URR时，每个坐标单独服从平移后的Beta分布，在高维下收敛到高斯分布。一般而言，单个RHT在最坏情况下并不合适，因为单个坐标可能远离这些分布。我们证明，对任意d维输入向量组合两个RHT后，归一化旋转向量的每个固定坐标的边际分布，在Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离上均在O(d^{-1/2})内接近标准高斯分布。然后我们将这些界应用于现代压缩方案（即DRIVE和QUIC-FL）的分析，并证明两个RHT实现了渐近匹配URR的性能。然而，我们证明两个RHT可能不足以用于向量量化（VQ），后者通常需要固定大小坐标块之间的弱相关性（而不仅仅是单个坐标的边际分布收敛）。我们证明三个RHT的组合可导致坐标协方差衰减。这保证了任何固定的、有界的、针对URR优化的多维VQ码本在使用三个RHT时具有相同的期望误差，其附加项随维度消失。最后，由于实际输入很少是对抗性的，我们提出了一种线性时间O(d)的输入矩检查方法，以在运行时动态自适应调整RHT的数量来提升性能。

</details>


### [153] [Multi-agent decision making: A Blackwell's informativeness approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06028)
*Zheng Zhang,Cuong C. Nguyen,Kevin Wells,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Blackwell信息准则框架，从理论上分析了多LLM系统中的投票与辩论方法的信息量上限，并提出一种基于后验乘积估计的实用方法，在六个QA基准上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的大语言模型多智能体决策方法（如投票和辩论）缺乏形式化的理论保证，无法确保最终决策的信息量。作者希望从信息论角度为多LLM决策提供严格的理论分析框架。

Method: 基于Blackwell信息结构抽象，证明投票和辩论诱导的信息结构不会超过所有智能体私有信息的合并信息量；提出贝叶斯合并后验最大化作为信息论上界决策规则；并设计实用方法，通过估计每个智能体的后验并采用后验乘积估计器近似合并后验。

Result: 在六个问答基准上的大量实验表明，所提出的方法优于当前最先进的多LLM辩论和投票方法。

Conclusion: Blackwell信息准则为多LLM决策提供了严格的理论分析工具；理论上界为贝叶斯合并后验最大化；所提实用方法有效且性能优异。

Original Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has motivated research on decision-making in multi-agent systems, where multiple agents collaborate to achieve shared objectives. Existing aggregation approaches, such as voting and debate, are largely ad-hoc and lack formal guarantees regarding the informativeness of the resulting decisions. In this paper, we provide a principled approach to analyse decisions made in the multi-LLM setting using Blackwell's informativeness framework. Within the Blackwell information-structure abstraction, we show that voting and debate induce information structures that are no more informative than the pooled private information of all agents. This result identifies Bayesian pooled posterior maximisation as an information-theoretic upper-bound decision rule under the Blackwell ordering. Motivated by this theoretical analysis, we introduce a practical method for LLM-based question-answering (QA) tasks that estimates each agent's posterior and approximates the pooled posterior using a product-of-posteriors estimator. Extensive experiments on six QA benchmarks demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art multi-LLM debate and voting methods.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型的快速发展推动了多智能体系统中决策制定的研究，其中多个智能体协作以实现共享目标。现有的聚合方法（如投票和辩论）大多是启发式的，缺乏关于最终决策信息量的形式化保证。在本文中，我们利用Blackwell信息准则框架提供了一种原则性方法来分析多LLM环境下的决策制定。在Blackwell信息结构抽象下，我们证明投票和辩论所诱导的信息结构不会比所有智能体私有信息的合并信息量更具信息性。这一结果将贝叶斯合并后验最大化确定为Blackwell排序下的信息论上界决策规则。受此理论分析启发，我们针对基于LLM的问答任务引入了一种实用方法，该方法估计每个智能体的后验，并使用后验乘积估计器近似合并后验。在六个问答基准上的大量实验表明，我们的方法优于当前最先进的多LLM辩论和投票方法。

</details>


### [154] [Does Synthetic Data Help? Empirical Evidence from Deep Learning Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06032)
*Hugo Cazaux,Eyjólfur Ingi Ásgeirsson,Hlynur Stefánsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究（9个实验组、4218次运行）系统评估了合成数据在时间序列预测中的增强效果，发现其效果高度依赖架构：通道混合模型（TimesNet, iTransformer）多数受益，而通道独立模型（DLinear, PatchTST）持续受损；仅季节性-趋势生成器可靠有效，硬课程切换导致性能下降（MSE增加24%）；为合成数据的有效使用提供了具体可操作的指南。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 合成数据在语言模型训练中已被广泛应用，但其在时间序列预测中的作用尚不明确，缺乏系统性的大规模实证研究来指导实践。

Method: 开展了大规模实证研究，包含9个实验组、4218次运行，系统评估了五种架构（TimesNet, iTransformer, DLinear, PatchTST等）、四种合成信号和七个数据集上的时间序列数据增强效果。

Result: (1) 效果高度依赖于架构：通道混合模型（TimesNet, iTransformer）在大多数试验中受益，而通道独立模型（DLinear, PatchTST）持续受损；(2) 在低资源场景下增益显著：TimesNet仅用10%的Weather数据配合合成增强即可超越全数据基线；(3) 所有架构平均下，67%的试验中增强反而有害；(4) 仅季节性-趋势生成器可靠有效；(5) 硬课程切换导致MSE增加24%。

Conclusion: 使用合成数据增强时应选择通道混合架构、采用渐进退火调度策略，并将低资源增强视为架构和数据集相关的问题。

Original Abstract: Synthetic data has transformed language model training, yet its role in time series forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a large-scale empirical study: nine experiment groups, 4,218 runs systematically evaluating synthetic time series augmentation across five architectures, four synthetic signals and seven datasets. The effect is sharply architecture-conditional: channel-mixing models (TimesNet, iTransformer) benefit in the majority of trials, while channel-independent models (DLinear, PatchTST) are consistently degraded. In selected low-resource settings the gains are striking: TimesNet trained on only 10\% of Weather data with synthetic augmentation surpasses the full-data baseline (4 of 16 sparsity-dataset combinations). Averaged across all architectures, augmentation hurts in 67\% of trials. We further find that only the Seasonal-Trend generator reliably helps across the tested benchmarks, and that hard curriculum switching is actively harmful (+24\% MSE degradation). These results provide concrete, actionable guidelines on how to use synthetic data: use synthetic augmentation with channel-mixing architectures, use gradual annealing schedules, and treat low-resource augmentation as architecture- and dataset-dependent. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/hugoiscracked/synthetic-ts/tree/main}

中文摘要翻译: 合成数据已经改变了语言模型的训练方式，但其在时间序列预测中的作用仍未被充分理解。我们开展了一项大规模实证研究：包含9个实验组、4218次运行，系统评估了五种架构、四种合成信号和七个数据集上的时间序列数据增强效果。其效果高度依赖于架构：通道混合模型（TimesNet, iTransformer）在大多数试验中受益，而通道独立模型（DLinear, PatchTST）则持续受损。在选定的低资源场景下，增益十分显著：仅使用10%的Weather数据并配合合成增强训练的TimesNet，超越了使用完整数据训练的基线（16个稀疏性-数据集组合中的4个）。平均所有架构来看，增强在67%的试验中造成了损害。我们进一步发现，仅季节性-趋势生成器在测试基准上可靠有效，而硬课程切换则明显有害（MSE退化24%）。这些结果为如何使用合成数据提供了具体且可操作的指南：使用通道混合架构时采用合成增强，使用渐进退火调度策略，并将低资源增强视为与架构和数据集相关的问题。代码已开源。

</details>


### [155] [Optimal Transport for LLM Reward Modeling from Noisy Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06036)
*Licheng Pan,Haochen Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yunsheng Lu,Yongqi Tong,Yinuo Wang,Shijian Wang,Zhixuan Chu,Lei Shen,Yuan Lu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SelectiveRM，一个基于最优传输理论的框架，用于解决RLHF中偏好数据噪声问题。通过联合一致性差异和松弛质量守恒机制，该框架能自动排除噪声样本，并在理论上优化了未观测干净风险的上界，实验表明其显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实RLHF数据集不可避免地包含噪声偏好，传统训练目标会过拟合这些错误，而现有去噪方法常依赖同质噪声假设，无法捕捉语言偏好的复杂性。

Method: 首先提出联合一致性差异（Joint Consistency Discrepancy）来对齐模型预测分布与偏好数据分布；然后引入基于部分传输（Partial Transport）的质量松弛（Mass Relaxation）机制，通过允许模型自动排除违背语义一致性的噪声样本，克服严格质量守恒导致模型拟合异常值的问题。

Result: 理论证明了SelectiveRM优化了未观测干净风险的一个更紧的上界；大量实验表明该方法在多个基准上显著优于现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: SelectiveRM通过最优传输框架有效处理了RLHF中的偏好噪声问题，既从分布对齐角度建模偏好一致性，又通过松弛机制实现噪声样本的自主排除，在理论和实验上均验证了其优越性。

Original Abstract: Reward models are fundamental to Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), yet real-world datasets are inevitably corrupted by noisy preference. Conventional training objectives tend to overfit these errors, while existing denoising approaches often rely on homogeneous noise assumptions that fail to capture the complexity of linguistic preferences. To handle these challenges, we propose SelectiveRM, a framework grounded in optimal transport. We first devise a Joint Consistency Discrepancy to align the distribution of model predictions with preference data. Furthermore, to address the limitation of strict mass conservation which compels the model to fit outliers, we incorporate a Mass Relaxation mechanism via partial transport. This enables the autonomous exclusion of samples with noisy preference that contradict semantic consistency. Theoretically, we demonstrate that SelectiveRM optimizes a tighter upper bound on the unobserved clean risk. Extensive experiments validate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 奖励模型是基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）的基础，但现实世界的数据集不可避免地受到噪声偏好的污染。传统的训练目标倾向于过拟合这些错误，而现有的去噪方法通常依赖于同质噪声假设，无法捕捉语言偏好的复杂性。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了SelectiveRM，一个基于最优传输的框架。我们首先设计了一种联合一致性差异（Joint Consistency Discrepancy）来对齐模型预测分布与偏好数据分布。此外，为了克服严格质量守恒迫使模型拟合异常值的局限，我们通过部分传输（Partial Transport）引入了一种质量松弛（Mass Relaxation）机制。这使得模型能够自动排除那些与语义一致性相矛盾的噪声偏好样本。理论上，我们证明了SelectiveRM优化了未观测干净风险的一个更紧的上界。大量实验验证了我们的方法在多个不同基准上显著优于现有最先进的基线模型。

</details>


### [156] [Requests of a Feather Must Flock Together: Batch Size vs. Prefix Homogeneity in LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06046)
*Saksham Rathi,Preeti,Mythili Vutukuru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Feather调度器，利用强化学习在批次大小和前缀同质性之间做最优权衡，结合分块哈希树（CHT）加速前缀检测，在vLLM和SGLang上实现2-10倍端到端吞吐量提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有前缀感知调度器仅通过最大化批次内的前缀复用来减少KV缓存内存占用，但未能意识到较小的前缀同质批次在KV缓存访问的空间和时间局部性上优于较大的异质批次，且基于基数树的前缀检测带来可观CPU开销。

Method: 提出Feather调度器，核心包含两部分：(1) 基于强化学习（RL）的调度策略，学习批次大小与前缀同质性的最优权衡；(2) 分块哈希树（CHT）轻量级数据结构，实现快速前缀检测和高效请求选择，避免昂贵的树遍历。

Result: 在vLLM和SGLang上集成后，相比现有调度器实现2-10倍端到端吞吐量提升；在缺乏足够前缀共享的工作负载下性能不低于现有方案；通过减少KV缓存访问总量，超越了具有相同目标的注意力核的性能。

Conclusion: Feather通过RL优化批次组成策略，配合CHT高效前缀检测，在保持兼容性的同时显著提升了大语言模型解码阶段的吞吐量。

Original Abstract: Auto-regressive token generation in large language models is memory-bound because it requires "attending to" key and value tensors (KV cache) of all previous tokens. Prior work aims to improve the efficiency of this decode process by batching multiple requests together, and maximizing batch size subject to GPU memory constraints. The key observation of our work is that with prefix-sharing workloads, smaller, prefix-homogeneous batches -- where all requests share a common prefix -- can achieve higher decode throughput than larger, heterogeneous batches, due to better spatial and temporal locality during KV cache accesses. However, prefix-aware schedulers in state-of-the-art inference engines maximize prefix reuse within a batch only to reduce KV cache memory footprint, but do not stop batch formation at smaller homogeneous batches that could have performed better. Further, we show that shared prefix detection in existing schedulers relies on radix-tree traversals, incurring substantial CPU overhead that is often comparable to GPU execution time. This paper presents Feather, a prefix-aware scheduler that uses reinforcement learning (RL) to learn the optimal tradeoff between batch size and prefix homogeneity. We also introduce Chunked Hash Tree (CHT), a lightweight data structure that enables fast prefix detection and efficient request selection for the RL scheduler, avoiding expensive tree traversals. We integrate Feather into vLLM and SGLang, and our evaluation shows that Feather achieves 2--10$\times$ higher end-to-end throughput as compared to existing schedulers, while doing no worse than the status quo when the workload does not have enough prefix sharing. Feather achieves these gains by reducing the total number of KV cache accesses, surpassing the performance of prefix-aware attention kernels that have the same goal.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型中的自回归词元生成受限于内存带宽，因为需要"注意力"到所有先前词元的键值张量（KV缓存）。先前的工作通过将多个请求批处理在一起，并在GPU内存约束下最大化批次大小来提升解码效率。我们的关键观察是，在具有前缀共享的工作负载中，较小且前缀同质的批次——其中所有请求共享一个公共前缀——由于KV缓存访问时更好的空间和时间局部性，可以达到比更大的异质批次更高的解码吞吐量。然而，现有推理引擎中的前缀感知调度器最大化批次内的前缀复用仅是为了减少KV缓存内存占用，并未在可能表现更好的较小同质批次处停止批次构建。此外，我们展示了现有调度器中的共享前缀检测依赖于基数树遍历，产生了通常与GPU执行时间相当的CPU开销。本文提出Feather，一种前缀感知调度器，使用强化学习（RL）来学习批次大小和前缀同质性之间的最优权衡。我们还引入了分块哈希树（CHT），一种轻量级数据结构，能够为RL调度器提供快速前缀检测和高效请求选择，避免了昂贵的树遍历。我们将Feather集成到vLLM和SGLang中，评估表明Feather相比现有调度器实现了2-10倍的端到端吞吐量提升，而在工作负载没有足够前缀共享时性能不低于现有方案。Feather通过减少KV缓存访问总量来实现这些增益，超越了具有相同目标的前缀感知注意力核的性能。

</details>


### [157] [Towards Generation-Efficient Uncertainty Estimation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06053)
*Mingcheng Zhu,Yu Liu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用部分生成内容甚至仅输入信息来高效估计LLM的不确定性，提出Logit Magnitude（基于早期停止生成前缀的top-M logit证据）和MetaUE（将基于生成的不确定性蒸馏为轻量级输入仅估计器），实验表明有效不确定性估计所需的生成量远低于常规假设。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有不确定性估计方法需要完整自回归生成，推理成本高且延迟不确定性评估。本文探索能否利用部分生成内容或仅输入信息实现有效的不确定性估计，以降低计算开销。

Method: 1. 提出统一框架，将不确定性估计建模为自回归生成过程上的早期估计问题，按观测信息量（多生成→输入仅预测）组织现有及新估计器。2. 提出Logit Magnitude：利用早期停止生成前缀的top-M logit证据估计不确定性。3. 提出MetaUE：将基于生成的不确定性蒸馏为轻量级输入仅估计器。

Result: Logit Magnitude在通用和领域特定基准上表现强劲；LLM的部分生成内容通常足以实现有效的不确定性估计；MetaUE在多种设置下提供了有竞争力的输入仅近似估计。

Conclusion: 有效的不确定性估计所需的生成量远低于常规假设，使得不可靠响应可以被更早识别，从而降低推理成本和延迟。

Original Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is important for deploying LLMs in high-stakes applications such as healthcare and finance, where hallucinations can appear fluent and plausible while being factually incorrect, making it difficult for users to judge whether an output should be trusted. Existing methods require one or more full autoregressive generations to estimate uncertainty, which introduces substantial inference cost and often delays uncertainty assessment. In this paper, we investigate whether effective uncertainty estimation can be achieved with partial generation or even input-only information. Specifically, we first develop a unified framework that formulates uncertainty estimation as an early estimation problem over the autoregressive generation process of LLMs. This framework organises existing and proposed estimators by the information they observe, ranging from multi-generation to input-only prediction, and clarifies the performance-cost trade-off underlying different uncertainty estimation methods. Building on this view, we study two largely underexplored low-cost settings: estimating uncertainty with part of the generation, and predicting uncertainty from the input prompt. We propose Logit Magnitude, which uses top-M logit evidence to estimate uncertainty from an early-stopped generation prefix, and MetaUE, which distils generation-based uncertainty into a lightweight input-only estimator trained with uncertainty scores. Extensive experiments on general and domain-specific benchmarks show that Logit Magnitude achieves strong performance, and partial generations of LLMs are often sufficient for effective uncertainty estimation. MetaUE further provides a competitive input-only approximation in several settings. These findings suggest that effective uncertainty estimation requires less generation than commonly assumed, enabling unreliable responses to be identified earlier.

中文摘要翻译: 不确定性估计对于在医疗保健和金融等高风险应用中部署大语言模型（LLM）至关重要，在这些场景中，幻觉可能以流畅且看似合理的方式出现，但实际上在事实上是错误的，使得用户难以判断输出是否应被信任。现有方法需要一次或多次完整的自回归生成才能估计不确定性，这引入了大量的推理成本，并且常常延迟不确定性评估。在本文中，我们研究是否可以通过部分生成甚至仅输入信息来实现有效的不确定性估计。具体来说，我们首先开发了一个统一框架，将不确定性估计表述为LLM自回归生成过程中的早期估计问题。该框架根据观测到的信息量（从多生成到仅输入预测）来组织现有及新提出的估计器，并阐明了不同不确定性估计方法背后的性能-成本权衡。基于这一视角，我们研究了两个很大程度上尚未被探索的低成本设置：利用部分生成内容估计不确定性，以及从输入提示预测不确定性。我们提出了Logit Magnitude，该方法使用top-M logit证据从提前停止的生成前缀中估计不确定性；以及MetaUE，该方法将基于生成的不确定性蒸馏为轻量级、仅输入估计器，并使用不确定性分数进行训练。在通用和领域特定基准上的大量实验表明，Logit Magnitude取得了强劲的性能，且LLM的部分生成内容通常足以实现有效的不确定性估计。MetaUE在多种设置下进一步提供了有竞争力的仅输入近似估计。这些发现表明，有效的不确定性估计所需的生成量远低于常规假设，使得不可靠响应可以被更早识别。

</details>


### [158] [Towards Self-Explainable Document Visual Question Answering with Chain-of-Explanation Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06058)
*Kjetil Indrehus,Adrian Duric,Changkyu Choi,Ali Ramezani-Kebrya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoExVQA，一种自解释的文档视觉问答框架，通过链式解释设计实现可验证的推理过程，在PFL-DocVQA上ANLS提升12%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有DocVQA模型将问题相关证据与答案定位纠缠在一起，作为黑盒运行，缺乏对预测如何依赖视觉证据的验证手段。

Method: CoExVQA采用链式解释设计：首先识别与问题相关的视觉证据，然后显式定位答案区域，最后仅从定位区域解码答案，实现可检验的多模态推理过程。

Result: 在PFL-DocVQA数据集上达到当前可解释DocVQA的最优性能，相比基线ANLS提升12%。

Conclusion: 将解码过程限制在已定位的证据区域，可实现透明且可验证的预测，同时提升可解释DocVQA性能。

Original Abstract: Document Visual Question Answering (DocVQA) requires vision-language models to reason not only about what information in a document is relevant to a question, but also where the answer is grounded on the page. Existing DocVQA models entangle question-relevant evidence and answer localization and operate largely as black boxes, offering limited means to verify how predictions depend on visual evidence. We propose CoExVQA, a self-explainable DocVQA framework with a grounded reasoning process through a chain-of-explanation design. CoExVQA first identifies question-relevant evidence, then explicitly localizes the answer region, and finally decodes the answer exclusively from the grounded region. Prediction via CoExVQA's chain-of-explanation enables direct inspection and verification of the reasoning process across modalities. Empirical results show that restricting decoding to grounded evidence achieves SotA explainable DocVQA performance on PFL-DocVQA, improving ANLS by 12% over the current explainable baselines while providing transparent and verifiable predictions.

中文摘要翻译: 文档视觉问答（DocVQA）要求视觉语言模型不仅要推理文档中哪些信息与问题相关，还要确定答案在页面上的位置。现有的DocVQA模型将问题相关证据与答案定位纠缠在一起，本质上作为黑盒运行，仅提供有限的手段来验证预测如何依赖视觉证据。我们提出CoExVQA，一种具有可解释性的DocVQA框架，通过链式解释设计实现基于证据的推理过程。CoExVQA首先识别与问题相关的证据，然后显式定位答案区域，最后仅从该定位区域解码答案。通过CoExVQA的链式解释进行预测，可以直接检查并验证跨模态的推理过程。实验结果表明，将解码限制在已定位的证据区域上，在PFL-DocVQA数据集上达到了当前最优的可解释DocVQA性能，相比现有的可解释基线方法ANLS提升了12%，同时提供了透明且可验证的预测。

</details>


### [159] [Causal Reinforcement Learning for Complex Card Games: A Magic The Gathering Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06066)
*Cristiano da Costa Cunha,Ajmal Mian,Tim French,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MTG-Causal-RL，一个基于《万智牌》的因果强化学习基准平台，具有高维部分观测、大规模掩码动作空间、显式结构因果模型（SCM）和丰富的因果评估指标。同时提出了CGFA-PPO作为参考因果智能体，并使用配对统计验证方法进行基准测试。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有因果强化学习缺乏同时包含序列决策、隐藏信息、大规模掩码动作空间和显式因果结构的复杂系统基准，阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 构建了MTG-Causal-RL基准平台，包含3077维部分观测、478动作掩码离散空间、五套标准赛制套牌、三种奖励方案和手动指定的SCM。提出了CGFA-PPO算法，利用SCM中胜率概率的父节点作为因子对齐的评论家目标，并加入干预校准损失。

Result: Masked PPO和CGFA-PPO达到了具有竞争力的分布内胜率，超越了随机基线；每因子校准轨迹和留一法跨套牌迁移差距揭示了标量胜率无法单独揭示的诊断结构。

Conclusion: MTG-Causal-RL为因果强化学习、世界模型和LLM智能体研究提供了一个共享测试平台，能够同时研究掩码动作空间下的因果信用分配、跨套牌的结构迁移和基于SCM的策略可审计性等当前基准无法共同提出的问题。

Original Abstract: Causal reinforcement learning (RL) lacks benchmarks for complex systems that combine sequential decision making, hidden information, large masked action spaces, and explicit causal structure. We introduce MTG-Causal-RL, a Gymnasium benchmark built on Magic: The Gathering with a 3,077-dimensional partial observation, a 478-action masked discrete action space, five competitive Standard archetypes, three reward schemes, and a hand-specified Structural Causal Model (SCM) over strategic variables. Every episode exposes causal variables, SCM-predicted intervention effects, and per-factor credit traces, making causal credit assignment, leave-one-out cross-archetype transfer, and policy auditability first-class metrics. We adapt a panel of reference baselines: random, heuristic, masked PPO, a causal-world-model PPO variant, and an architecture-matched scalar control. We propose Causal Graph-Factored Advantage PPO (CGFA-PPO) as a reference causal agent that uses SCM parents of win probability as factor-aligned critic targets with an intervention-calibration loss. All comparisons use paired seeds, paired-bootstrap confidence intervals, and Holm-Bonferroni correction within pre-registered families. Masked PPO and CGFA-PPO reach competitive in-distribution win rates and exceed the random baseline; per-factor calibration trajectories and leave-one-out transfer gaps expose diagnostic structure that scalar win rate alone cannot. We release the benchmark, reference-baseline results, and full evaluation protocol openly. By coupling a strategically rich, partially observed domain with an explicit causal interface and statistical protocol, MTG-Causal-RL gives causal-RL, world-model, and LLM-agent research a shared testbed for questions current benchmarks cannot pose together: causal credit assignment under masked action spaces, structural transfer across archetypes, and SCM-grounded policy auditability.

中文摘要翻译: 因果强化学习（Causal RL）缺乏能够同时结合序列决策、隐藏信息、大规模掩码动作空间和显式因果结构的复杂系统基准。我们提出了MTG-Causal-RL，这是一个基于《万智牌》构建的Gymnasium基准平台，具有3077维部分观测、478动作掩码离散动作空间、五套具有竞争力的标准赛制套牌、三种奖励方案以及一个手动指定的策略变量结构因果模型（SCM）。每个回合都会暴露因果变量、SCM预测的干预效应以及每因子信用轨迹，使得因果信用分配、留一法跨套牌迁移和策略可审计性成为一级指标。我们适配了一组参考基线：随机基线、启发式基线、掩码PPO、因果世界模型PPO变体以及架构匹配的标量控制基线。我们提出因果图因子化优势PPO（CGFA-PPO）作为参考因果智能体，该算法使用胜率概率的SCM父节点作为因子对齐的评论家目标，并辅以干预校准损失。所有比较均使用配对种子、配对自助法置信区间以及预注册族内的Holm-Bonferroni校正。Masked PPO和CGFA-PPO达到了具有竞争力的分布内胜率，并超越了随机基线；每因子校准轨迹和留一法迁移差距揭示了标量胜率单独无法揭示的诊断结构。我们公开了该基准平台、参考基线结果以及完整的评估协议。通过将一个策略丰富且部分可观测的领域与显式因果接口和统计协议相结合，MTG-Causal-RL为因果强化学习、世界模型和LLM智能体研究提供了一个共享测试平台，用于解决当前基准无法共同提出的问题：掩码动作空间下的因果信用分配、跨套牌的结构迁移以及基于SCM的策略可审计性。

</details>


### [160] [Normalized Architectures are Natively 4-Bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06067)
*Maxim Fishman,Brian Chmiel,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现nGPT（一种将权重和隐层表示约束在单位超球面上的架构）对低精度算术具有内在鲁棒性，无需额外干预即可实现稳定的NVFP4训练，且优势随隐层维度增大而增强。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 以4位精度训练大语言模型对计算效率至关重要，但标准架构在低精度下需要复杂干预（如随机Hadamard变换、逐张量缩放）来维持模型质量，论文旨在探索更简洁高效的解决方案。

Method: 利用nGPT的归一化架构，其权重和隐层表示被约束在单位超球面上；通过分析点积中信号与量化噪声的不同行为（超球面约束增强信号的正相关性，促进信号沿隐层维度累积，而噪声保持不相关），实现稳定的端到端NVFP4训练。

Result: 在1.2B稠密模型和高达3B/30B参数的混合（Mamba-Transformer）MoE模型上验证了有效性；发现nGPT中量化噪声不相关但信号在超球面约束下呈现增强的弱正相关，从而获得更高的有效信噪比和更平坦的损失曲面，且该优势随隐层维度增大而增强。

Conclusion: nGPT架构天然适用于低精度训练，无需额外干预即可保持模型质量，且在大规模场景下优势更显著，为高效训练大语言模型提供了一种简洁有效的方案。

Original Abstract: Training large language models at 4-bit precision is critical for efficiency. We show that nGPT, an architecture that constrains weights and hidden representations to the unit hypersphere, is inherently more robust to low-precision arithmetic. This removes the need for interventions-such as applying random Hadamard transforms and performing per-tensor scaling calculations-to preserve model quality, and it enables stable end-to-end NVFP4 training. We validate this approach on both a 1.2B dense model and hybrid (Mamba-Transformer) MoE models of up to 3B/30B parameters. We trace this robustness to the dot product: while quantization noise remains largely uncorrelated in both standard and normalized architectures, the signal behaves differently. In nGPT, the hypersphere constraint enhances weak positive correlations among the element-wise products, leading to a constructive accumulation of the signal across the hidden dimension while the noise continues to average out. This yields a higher effective signal-to-noise ratio and a flatter loss landscape, with the effect strengthening as the hidden dimension grows, suggesting increasing advantages at scale. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/anonymous452026/ngpt-nvfp4

中文摘要翻译: 以4位精度训练大语言模型对效率至关重要。我们发现，nGPT——一种将权重和隐层表示约束在单位超球面上的架构——对低精度算术具有内在鲁棒性。这消除了为保持模型质量而采取干预措施（如应用随机Hadamard变换和执行逐张量缩放计算）的必要，并实现了稳定的端到端NVFP4训练。我们在1.2B稠密模型和高达3B/30B参数的混合（Mamba-Transformer）MoE模型上验证了该方法。我们将这种鲁棒性归因于点积：虽然量化噪声在标准架构和归一化架构中基本保持不相关，但信号的行为有所不同。在nGPT中，超球面约束增强了逐元素乘积之间微弱的正相关性，使得信号沿隐层维度产生建设性累积，而噪声则继续相互抵消。这产生了更高的有效信噪比和更平坦的损失曲面，且该效果随隐层维度的增大而增强，表明在更大规模下具有递增的优势。参考实现见https://github.com/anonymous452026/ngpt-nvfp4。

</details>


### [161] [Understanding diffusion models requires rethinking (again) generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06077)
*Pierre Marion,Yu-Han Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证扩散模型的泛化需要全新的理论框架，指出记忆与泛化在此类模型中不可兼得，主张研究应从“为何不记忆”转向“预记忆阶段学到了什么”，并通过CIFAR-10实证研究提出关键开放问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有理论（经典统计学习、良性过拟合）无法解释扩散模型的泛化现象，且学界对扩散模型为何能泛化（而非记忆）的多种解释（容量限制、隐式正则化、架构偏置）之间关系不明，亟需新的理论视角。

Method: 通过立场论述（position paper）结合CIFAR-10数据集上的扩散模型实证研究，分析模型在预记忆阶段的学习行为，并将发现提炼为具体的开放性问题。

Result: 发现在扩散模型中，训练数据记忆与对新样本的泛化互不相容；全记忆训练集的模型只能复制而非生成新数据。实证结果支持从“为何不记忆”转向“预记忆阶段学到了什么”的研究方向。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的泛化理解需要超越传统理论框架，未来研究应聚焦于模型在预记忆阶段实际学习的内容，而非为何没有发生记忆。

Original Abstract: This position paper argues that understanding generalization in diffusion models requires fundamentally new theoretical frameworks that go beyond both classical statistical learning theory and the benign overfitting paradigm developed for supervised learning. In diffusion models, unlike in supervised learning, memorization of training data and generalization to novel samples are incompatible: a model that has fully memorized its training set generates copies rather than novel data. Several theoretical explanations for why practical diffusion models nevertheless generalize have been proposed, based on capacity limitations, implicit regularization from optimization, or architectural inductive biases, but their interactions remain unclear. We argue that the field should pivot from explaining why the diffusion models do not memorize to investigating what the model actually learns during pre-memorization phase. To highlight our stance, we conduct empirical study of diffusion models trained on CIFAR-10, and we distill the findings into concrete open questions that we believe are key to improve understanding of generalization in diffusion models.

中文摘要翻译: 本立场论文认为，理解扩散模型中的泛化需要全新的理论框架，这些框架必须超越经典的统计学习理论以及为监督学习开发的良性过拟合范式。在扩散模型中，与监督学习不同，训练数据的记忆和对新样本的泛化是不相容的：一个完全记忆了训练集的模型只会生成训练数据的副本而非新数据。目前已有多种基于容量限制、优化过程的隐式正则化或架构归纳偏置的理论解释来说明为何实际中的扩散模型仍然能够泛化，但这些因素之间的相互作用尚不清楚。我们认为，该领域应从解释扩散模型为何不记忆，转向研究模型在预记忆阶段实际学到了什么。为阐明我们的立场，我们在CIFAR-10上对扩散模型进行了实证研究，并将研究结果提炼为具体的开放性问题，我们相信这些问题对增进对扩散模型泛化的理解至关重要。

</details>


### [162] [Fast Gauss-Newton for Multiclass Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06081)
*Mikalai Korbit,Mario Zanon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN)方法，通过将多类softmax交叉熵的广义Gauss-Newton (GGN)曲率分解为"真vs其余"项和"类内协方差"项，并舍弃后者来实现曲率近似。该方法在保持损失和梯度不变的前提下，仅在曲率层面引入近似，可通过共轭梯度法高效求解，适用于类别数较多的场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多类softmax交叉熵中，全广义Gauss-Newton (GGN)曲率通过softmax协方差耦合所有输出logits，导致曲率-向量乘积随类别数增加难以扩展，计算效率低下。

Method: 将多类GGN精确分解为"真vs其余"项和半正定的"类内协方差"项；FGN保留第一项舍弃第二项，得到多类GGN的半正定下近似；利用FGN曲率结构，将阻尼更新等价地写为白化行空间系统，通过共轭梯度法无矩阵求解。

Result: 当竞争类质量集中或阻尼较大时，FGN最接近完整softmax GGN；随着被舍弃的类内协方差增大，FGN与全GGN的偏差增大。

Conclusion: FGN通过合理的曲率分解与近似，在保持计算效率的同时实现了对全GGN的有效逼近，为大规模多类问题提供了一种可行的二阶优化方法。

Original Abstract: In multiclass softmax cross-entropy, the full generalized Gauss-Newton (GGN) curvature couples all output logits through the softmax covariance, making curvature-vector products harder to scale as the number of classes grows. We show that the standard multiclass GGN can be decomposed exactly into a true-vs-rest term and a positive semidefinite within-competitor covariance term. Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN) retains the first term and drops the second, yielding a positive semidefinite under-approximation of the multiclass GGN that is exact for binary classification. The derivation uses an exact true-vs-rest scalar-margin representation of softmax cross-entropy: the loss and gradient are unchanged, and the approximation enters only at the curvature level. Exploiting the FGN curvature structure, the damped update can be written as an equivalent whitened row-space system with one row per mini-batch example. We solve this system matrix-free by conjugate gradient using Jacobian-vector and vector-Jacobian products of the scalar margin map. Targeted mechanism experiments and an evaluation on a fixed-feature multiclass head support the predictions from the decomposition: FGN stays closest to the full softmax GGN when competitor mass is concentrated or damping is large, and deviates as the dropped within-competitor covariance grows.

中文摘要翻译: 在多类softmax交叉熵中，全广义Gauss-Newton (GGN)曲率通过softmax协方差耦合所有输出logits，使得曲率-向量乘积随类别数增长而难以扩展。我们证明，标准多类GGN可以精确分解为一个"真vs其余"项和一个半正定的"类内竞争"协方差项。Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN)保留第一项并舍弃第二项，得到多类GGN的一个半正定下近似，该近似在二分类情形下是精确的。该推导利用了softmax交叉熵的一个精确的"真vs其余"标量间隔表示：损失和梯度不变，近似仅在曲率层面引入。利用FGN曲率结构，阻尼更新可写为一个等价的白化行空间系统，每行对应一个小批量样本。我们通过共轭梯度法对该系统进行无矩阵求解，使用标量间隔映射的Jacobian-向量和向量-Jacobian乘积。针对性的机制实验和固定特征多类分类头的评估支持了分解的预测：当竞争类质量集中或阻尼较大时，FGN最接近完整softmax GGN；随着被舍弃的类内协方差增大，FGN与全GGN的偏差增大。

</details>


### [163] [Beyond Autoregressive RTG: Conditioning via Injection Outside Sequential Modeling in Decision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06104)
*Yongyi Wang,Hanyu Liu,Lingfeng Li,Bozhou Chen,Ang Li,Qirui Zheng,Xionghui Yang,Chucai Wang,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SlimDT通过从自回归序列中去除Return-to-Go (RTG) 令牌，将其信息注入到状态表示中，使Transformer只处理(状态, 动作)序列，从而将序列长度减少三分之一，在D4RL基准测试上超越标准DT并达到与现有最优方法相当的性能，同时提升推理效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准Decision Transformer中RTG作为一个标量令牌，包含的信息远少于状态或动作向量，却消耗相同的计算预算；且Transformer的自注意力成本随序列长度二次增长，包含RTG作为独立令牌增加了不必要的开销。

Method: SlimDT将RTG信息注入到状态表示中（在序列建模步骤之前完成），从而将RTG从自回归序列中移除，使得Transformer只需处理更紧凑的(状态, 动作)序列，序列长度减少了三分之一。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试上，SlimDT在多种任务中超越了标准DT，并取得了与现有最优方法相当的性能，同时由于序列长度减少而提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: 将稀疏的条件信号与信息丰富的序列解耦，既能带来计算上的收益，也能提升任务性能。

Original Abstract: Decision Transformer (DT) formulates offline reinforcement learning as autoregressive sequence modeling, achieving promising results by predicting actions from a sequence of Return-to-Go (RTG), state, and action tokens. However, RTG is a scalar that summarizes future rewards, containing far less information than typical state or action vectors, yet it consumes the same computational budget per token. Worse, the self-attention cost of Transformers grows quadratically with sequence length, so including RTG as a separate token adds unnecessary overhead. We propose SlimDT, which removes RTG from the autoregressive sequence. Instead, we inject RTG information into the state representations before the sequential modeling step, allowing the Transformer to process only a compact (state, action) sequence. This reduces the sequence length by one-third, directly improving inference efficiency. On the D4RL benchmark, SlimDT surpasses standard DT across various tasks and achieves performance comparable to existing state-of-the-art methods. Decoupling a sparse conditioning signal from an information-rich sequence thus yields both computational gains and higher task performance.

中文摘要翻译: 决策变换器（Decision Transformer, DT）将离线强化学习建模为自回归序列建模，通过从一组由Return-to-Go（RTG）、状态和动作令牌组成的序列中预测动作，取得了令人鼓舞的结果。然而，RTG是一个对未来奖励进行汇总的标量，所包含的信息远少于典型的状态或动作向量，却消耗相同的单令牌计算预算。更糟糕的是，Transformer的自注意力成本随序列长度呈二次增长，因此将RTG作为独立令牌引入会带来不必要的开销。我们提出SlimDT，将RTG从自回归序列中移除。取而代之的是，我们在序列建模步骤之前将RTG信息注入到状态表示中，使得Transformer仅需处理紧凑的（状态, 动作）序列。这将序列长度减少了三分之一，直接提升了推理效率。在D4RL基准测试上，SlimDT在各种任务中超越了标准DT，并取得了与现有最优方法相当的性能。因此，将稀疏的条件信号与信息丰富的序列解耦，既能带来计算上的收益，也能提升任务性能。

</details>


### [164] [Matrix-Valued Optimism is Matrix-Valued Augmentation: Additive Hybrid Designs for Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06141)
*Jiayi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了增广拉格朗日方法与乐观原始-对偶方法在矩阵值校正下的等价性，提出了加和性原理，并设计了一种混合校正规则，在非线性等式约束优化中优于纯增广或纯乐观方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究仅证明了标量参数下增广拉格朗日方法与乐观原始-对偶方法的等价性，尚不清楚该等价性是否适用于更一般的矩阵值校正参数，以及如何利用两者之间的设计自由度来改进优化性能。

Method: 本文首先证明了加和性原理：对于对称矩阵参数，理想原始轨迹仅取决于总校正矩阵，而非其在增广通道与乐观通道之间的分配方式。进而提出了一种混合规则——以闭式解形式选择矩阵校正量，将其分解到两个通道中，并使用局部谱权重选择原始步长和对偶步长。

Result: 在非线性等式约束问题上的实验表明，混合设计优于纯增广和纯乐观端点，紧密跟踪网格搜索的混合最优解，且在轻度至中度病态条件下与一阶原始-对偶基线方法具有竞争力。实验同时揭示了局限性：当约束雅可比矩阵病态时，实现精确抵消需要越来越大的矩阵校正量。

Conclusion: 增广校正与乐观校正在矩阵参数下具有加和性等价关系，但二者在有限步可行性上存在差异，混合设计可有效利用这一自由度提升优化性能。

Original Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian and optimistic primal--dual methods stabilize equality-constrained optimization through seemingly different mechanisms: the former adds constraint-dependent primal curvature, while the latter adds dual memory. Recent work has shown that these mechanisms are equivalent for scalar parameters. We extend this equivalence to matrix-valued correction. We prove an additivity principle: for symmetric matrix parameters, the ideal primal trajectory depends only on the summed correction matrix, not on how it is split between augmented and optimistic channels. This exposes a design freedom: algebraically equivalent decompositions can have different finite-step feasibility because augmented correction affects primal curvature, whereas optimistic correction affects the scale of the dual memory correction. We formulate the resulting step-size-limited design problem and derive a closed-form hybrid rule that selects a matrix correction, splits it between the two channels, and chooses primal and dual steps using local spectral weights. Experiments on nonlinear equality-constrained problems with controlled constraint-Jacobian conditioning show that the hybrid design improves over pure augmented and pure optimistic endpoints, closely tracks a grid-search hybrid oracle, and is competitive with first-order primal--dual baselines under mild-to-moderate ill-conditioning. The experiments also identify the expected limitation: exact cancellation requires increasingly large matrix corrections as the constraint Jacobian becomes ill-conditioned.

中文摘要翻译: 增广拉格朗日方法和乐观原始-对偶方法通过看似不同的机制稳定等式约束优化：前者添加依赖于约束的原始曲率，而后者添加对偶记忆。近期工作表明，对于标量参数，这两种机制是等价的。本文将这一等价性推广到矩阵值校正。我们证明了加和性原理：对于对称矩阵参数，理想原始轨迹仅取决于总校正矩阵，而不依赖于其在增广通道和乐观通道之间的分配方式。这揭示了一个设计自由度：代数等价的分解可能具有不同的有限步可行性，因为增广校正影响原始曲率，而乐观校正影响对偶记忆校正的尺度。我们据此构建了受步长限制的设计问题，推导出一种闭式混合规则——该规则选择一个矩阵校正量，将其分解到两个通道中，并使用局部谱权重选择原始步长和对偶步长。在约束雅可比矩阵条件数可控的非线性等式约束问题上的实验表明，混合设计优于纯增广和纯乐观端点，紧密追踪网格搜索的混合最优解，且在轻度至中度病态条件下与一阶原始-对偶基线方法具有竞争力。实验同时指出了预期的局限性：当约束雅可比矩阵病态时，实现精确抵消需要越来越大的矩阵校正量。

</details>


### [165] [Unifying Goal-Conditioned RL and Unsupervised Skill Learning via Control-Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06145)
*Alireza Modirshanechi,Benjamin Eysenbach,Peter Dayan,Eric Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论层面统一了目标条件强化学习（GCRL）与互信息技能学习（MISL），指出两者可归结为控制最大化问题。通过识别三种典型GCRL公式并证明其本质不等价，论文揭示了MISL目标函数与下游GCRL任务之间的精确对应关系，为强化学习预训练奠定了理论基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 互信息技能学习（MISL）在实践中已被广泛用于目标条件强化学习（GCRL）的预训练，但其为何能促进下游目标达成任务在理论上仍是一个谜。GCRL和MISL都是宽泛的概念，不同GCRL任务使用不同的性能度量标准，不同MISL方法优化不同的行为多样性概念，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解二者之间的关系。

Method: 将GCRL和MISL统一为控制最大化（control maximization）问题。识别三种典型GCRL公式并证明其本质不等价性，同时揭示它们共同的核心解释：良好策略的未来轨迹应对目标高度敏感。进一步指出MISL目标可理解为技能敏感度（skill-sensitivity）度量，并证明MISL目标受公式特定的下游目标敏感度（goal-sensitivity）所约束，从而建立MISL方法与GCRL任务之间的精确对应关系。

Result: 三种典型GCRL公式在理论上本质不等价，即使在相同环境下也可能导致不相容的最优策略；MISL目标函数与下游GCRL任务之间存在精确对应关系：对每种GCRL公式，存在匹配的MISL目标，使得更多样化的技能提供更大的下游目标敏感度。

Conclusion: 本文为强化学习预训练奠定了理论基础，并为实践提供指导：当用户关心特定类型的下游任务时，应当选择与之匹配的预训练目标。

Original Abstract: Unsupervised pretraining has driven empirical advances in goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), but its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In particular, an influential class of methods, mutual information skill learning (MISL), discovers behaviorally diverse skills that can later be used for downstream goal-reaching. However, it remains a theoretical mystery why skills learned through MISL should support goal-reaching. A subtle challenge is that both GCRL and MISL are umbrella terms: different GCRL tasks use distinct criteria for measuring goal-reaching performance, while different MISL methods optimize distinct notions of behavioral diversity. We address this challenge and unify GCRL and MISL as instances of control maximization. We identify three canonical GCRL formulations and prove that they are fundamentally inequivalent: they can induce incompatible optimal policies even in the same environment. Nevertheless, they all share a common interpretation: a well-performing goal-conditioned policy is one whose future trajectory is highly sensitive to the commanded goal, with the precise notion of sensitivity determined by the GCRL formulation. Noting that MISL objectives can be understood as measures of skill-sensitivity akin to goal-sensitivity, we show that MISL objectives are bounded by formulation-specific downstream goal-sensitivities. These bounds establish a precise correspondence between MISL methods and downstream GCRL tasks: for every GCRL formulation, there exists a matching MISL objective for which more diverse skills afford greater downstream goal sensitivity. Our results thus lay a theoretical foundation for RL pretraining and have important practical implications, such as suggesting which pretraining objectives to use when a user cares about a specific class of downstream tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 无监督预训练推动了目标条件强化学习（GCRL）的实证进展，但其理论基础仍缺乏深入理解。特别地，一类有影响力的方法——互信息技能学习（MISL）——能够发现行为多样化的技能，这些技能随后可用于下游目标达成任务。然而，通过MISL学得的技能为何能够支持目标达成，在理论上仍是一个谜。一个微妙的挑战在于，GCRL和MISL都是宽泛术语：不同的GCRL任务使用不同的标准来衡量目标达成性能，而不同的MISL方法优化不同的行为多样性概念。我们应对这一挑战，将GCRL和MISL统一为控制最大化问题。我们识别出三种典型GCRL公式，并证明它们在本质上是不等价的：即使在相同的环境中，它们也可能导致不相容的最优策略。然而，它们都共享一个共同解释：表现良好的目标条件策略，其未来轨迹对命令目标高度敏感，敏感度的具体定义取决于GCRL公式。注意到MISL目标可理解为类似于目标敏感度的技能敏感度度量，我们证明MISL目标受公式特定的下游目标敏感度所约束。这些约束建立了MISL方法与下游GCRL任务之间的精确对应关系：对于每种GCRL公式，存在一个匹配的MISL目标，其中更多样化的技能带来更大的下游目标敏感度。我们的结果因此为强化学习预训练奠定了理论基础，并具有重要的实践意义，例如建议用户在关心特定类别下游任务时应使用何种预训练目标。

</details>


### [166] [AdaGamma: State-Dependent Discounting for Temporal Adaptation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06149)
*Yaomin Wang,Jianting Pan,Ran Tian,Xiaoyang Li,Yu Zhang,Hengle Qin,Tianshu YU*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdaGamma，一种实用的深度演员-评论家方法，通过状态依赖折扣和回报一致性目标，在避免TD误差坍塌的同时提升强化学习性能，并在连续控制基准和京东物流平台A/B测试中取得显著改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统深度强化学习在所有状态下使用单一固定折扣因子，限制了模型的表达能力。虽然状态依赖折扣概念上具有吸引力，但朴素的深度演员-评论家实现会变得不稳定并退化到TD误差坍塌。

Method: 提出AdaGamma方法，学习一个状态依赖的折扣函数，并结合回报一致性目标（return-consistency objective）来正则化由此产生的备份结构，防止退化行为。

Result: 理论上分析了状态依赖折扣诱导的贝尔曼算子的适定性；实验上AdaGamma可集成到SAC和PPO中，在连续控制基准上取得一致性改进，并在京东物流平台的在线A/B测试中取得统计显著的收益提升。

Conclusion: 状态依赖折扣在耦合回报一致性目标以防止退化目标操纵时，可以在深度强化学习中有效发挥作用。

Original Abstract: The discount factor in reinforcement learning controls both the effective planning horizon and the strength of bootstrapping, yet most deep RL methods use a single fixed value across all states. While state-dependent discounting is conceptually appealing, naive deep actor--critic implementations can become unstable and degenerate toward TD-error collapse. We propose AdaGamma, a practical deep actor--critic method for state-dependent discounting that learns a state-dependent discount function together with a return-consistency objective to regularize the induced backup structure. On the theory side, we analyze the Bellman operator induced by state-dependent discounting and establish its basic well-posedness properties under suitable conditions. Empirically, AdaGamma integrates into both SAC and PPO, yielding consistent improvements on continuous-control benchmarks, and achieves statistically significant gains in an online A/B test on the JD Logistics platform. These results suggest that state-dependent discounting can be made effective in deep RL when coupled with a return-consistency objective that prevents degenerate target manipulation.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习中的折扣因子既控制着有效规划视野，也控制着自举的强度，然而大多数深度强化学习方法在所有状态下都使用单一的固定值。虽然状态依赖折扣在概念上具有吸引力，但朴素的深度演员-评论家实现可能变得不稳定并退化为TD误差坍塌。我们提出AdaGamma，一种实用的深度演员-评论家状态依赖折扣方法，该方法学习一个状态依赖的折扣函数，并结合回报一致性目标来正则化由此产生的备份结构。在理论方面，我们分析了状态依赖折扣诱导的贝尔曼算子，并建立了其在合适条件下的基本适定性性质。在实验方面，AdaGamma可集成到SAC和PPO中，在连续控制基准上取得一致性改进，并在京东物流平台的在线A/B测试中取得统计显著的收益提升。这些结果表明，当耦合回报一致性目标以防止退化目标操纵时，状态依赖折扣可以在深度强化学习中有效发挥作用。

</details>


### [167] [Entropy-Regularized Adjoint Matching for Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06156)
*Abdelghani Ghanem,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ME-AM通过最大熵正则化和混合行为先验，解决了基于流的离线强化学习中存在的流行度偏差和支持约束问题，在稀疏奖励连续控制任务上达到或超越SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于流匹配模型的离线强化学习方法（如QAM）在策略优化时受限于固定的行为分布，导致两个关键问题：(1) 流行度偏差——低密度区域的高奖励动作被抑制；(2) 支持约束——限制了离流形探索。添加残差高斯策略等现有方案会重新引入单峰分布的表述瓶颈。

Method: 提出最大熵伴随匹配（ME-AM）框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 镜像下降熵最大化目标，缓解流行度偏差以从离线数据中提取最优策略；(2) 混合行为先验，数学上扩展几何支持以涵盖分布外的高奖励区域，同时保持生成向量场的绝对连续性。

Result: 在多种稀疏奖励连续控制环境中，ME-AM相比先前SOTA方法展现出竞争性或更优的性能。

Conclusion: ME-AM通过扩展的几何支持识别出鲁棒的动作，同时保持了生成向量场的绝对连续性，为基于流的离线RL提供了统一且有效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Integrating expressive generative policies, such as flow-matching models, into offline reinforcement learning (RL) allows agents to capture complex, multi-modal behaviors. While Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) stabilizes policy optimization via the continuous adjoint method, it remains inherently bound to the fixed behavior distribution. This dependence induces a \textit{popularity bias} that can suppress high-reward actions in low-density regions, and creates a \textit{support binding} that restricts off-manifold exploration. Existing workarounds, such as appending \textit{residual} Gaussian policies, often re-introduce the expressivity bottlenecks associated with unimodal distributions. In this work, we propose \textit{Maximum Entropy Adjoint Matching} (ME-AM), a unified framework that addresses these limitations within the continuous flow formulation. ME-AM incorporates two mechanisms: (1) a Mirror Descent entropy maximization objective that mitigates the popularity bias to facilitate the extraction of optimal policies from offline datasets, and (2) a \textit{Mixture Behavior Prior} that mathematically broadens the geometric support to encompass out-of-distribution high-reward regions. By exploring this extended geometry, ME-AM identifies robust actions while preserving the absolute continuity of the generative vector field. Empirically, ME-AM demonstrates competitive or superior performance compared to prior state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods across a diverse suite of sparse-reward continuous control environments.

中文摘要翻译: 将富有表现力的生成策略（如流匹配模型）集成到离线强化学习（RL）中，使得智能体能够捕获复杂的多模态行为。尽管伴随匹配Q学习（QAM）通过连续伴随方法稳定了策略优化，但它本质上仍受限于固定的行为分布。这种依赖性会诱发一种\"流行度偏差\"，抑制低密度区域中的高奖励动作，并产生一种\"支持约束\"，限制了离流形探索。现有的变通方案（如附加\"残差\"高斯策略）往往会重新引入与单峰分布相关的表达瓶颈。在这项工作中，我们提出了最大熵伴随匹配（ME-AM），一个在连续流框架内解决这些局限性的统一框架。ME-AM包含两种机制：(1) 镜像下降熵最大化目标，缓解流行度偏差以促进从离线数据集中提取最优策略；(2) \"混合行为先验\"，在数学上扩展几何支持以涵盖分布外的高奖励区域。通过探索这种扩展后的几何结构，ME-AM在保持生成向量场绝对连续性的同时，识别出鲁棒的动作。实验结果表明，在多种稀疏奖励连续控制环境中，ME-AM相比先前最先进的（SOTA）方法表现出竞争性或更优的性能。

</details>


### [168] [One Algorithm, Two Goals: Dual Scoring for Parameter and Data Selection in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06166)
*Xinrui Chen,Liu Yang,Ou Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualSFT，一种基于共享梯度统计的联合参数-数据选择微调方法，利用单一评分规则同时生成参数掩码和数据子集，在3B-9B大语言模型上取得更优的联合约束权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在LLM微调中，参数选择和数选择通常由独立的评分机制驱动，当两者共同决定受限微调时，这种分离会导致冗余开销并难以协调选择。

Method: 将参数选择和数据选择建模为在共同验证目标下的双层选择问题，推导出共享的局部响应替代评分规则。在一阶和二阶验证改进近似下，参数重要性和数据效用表现为单个梯度交互矩阵的列向和行向聚合，形成闭式行列对应关系，从而同时提取两种信号。基于此结构提出DualSFT，一种一次性的双评分为法，从共享梯度统计中生成参数掩码和数据子集。

Result: 在3B-9B大语言模型上，单轴DualSFT变体在各自比较组内增强了目标任务性能和稳定性-可塑性权衡；完整DualSFT在匹配预算下比顺序混合基线取得了更有利的联合约束权衡。

Conclusion: 通过统一参数选择和数据选择的评分机制，可以有效降低冗余开销并实现更优的协调选择，DualSFT在联合约束场景下优于顺序混合方法。

Original Abstract: In Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuning, parameter and data selection are common strategies for reducing fine-tuning cost, yet they are typically driven by separate scoring mechanisms. When a parameter mask and data subset jointly determine restricted fine-tuning, this separation incurs redundant overhead and makes coordinated selection difficult. We cast parameter and data selection as two bilevel selection problems under a common validation objective and derive a shared local response-surrogate scoring rule. Under first- and second-order validation-improvement approximations, parameter importance and data utility emerge as column-wise and row-wise aggregations of a single gradient interaction matrix, yielding a closed-form row-column correspondence for co-extracting both signals. Building on this structure, we propose DualSFT (Dual-Selection Fine-Tuning), a one-shot dual-scoring algorithm that produces a parameter mask and data subset from shared gradient statistics. On 3B-9B LLMs, single-axis DualSFT variants strengthen target-task performance and stability-plasticity trade-offs within their comparison groups, while full DualSFT yields a more favorable joint-constrained trade-off than sequential hybrid baselines under matched budgets.

中文摘要翻译: 在大语言模型微调中，参数选择和数据选择是降低微调成本的常见策略，但它们通常由独立的评分机制驱动。当参数掩码和数据子集共同决定受限微调时，这种分离会带来冗余开销并使得协调选择变得困难。本文将参数选择和数据选择建模为在共同验证目标下的两个双层选择问题，并推导出一个共享的局部响应替代评分规则。在一阶和二阶验证改进近似下，参数重要性和数据效用表现为单个梯度交互矩阵的列向和行向聚合，形成了同时提取两种信号的闭式行列对应关系。基于这一结构，我们提出了DualSFT（双选择微调），一种一次性的双评分算法，从共享梯度统计中生成参数掩码和数据子集。在3B-9B大语言模型上，单轴DualSFT变体在各自比较组内增强了目标任务性能和稳定性-可塑性权衡，而完整DualSFT在匹配预算下比顺序混合基线取得了更有利的联合约束权衡。

</details>


### [169] [When Labels Have Structure: Improving Image Classification with Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06274)
*April Chan,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HACE 是一种替代标准交叉熵损失的层级感知损失函数，通过预测聚合和祖先标签平滑将类别层级结构融入损失中，在分类任务上取得了显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失平等对待所有错误分类，忽略了类别层级结构中蕴含的语义距离信息，无法利用类别间的层次关系来指导模型学习。

Method: HACE 包含两个组件：(1) 预测聚合——将模型的预测概率沿层级向上传播，使父节点累积子节点的置信度；(2) 祖先标签平滑——将真实标签信号沿从真实类别到根节点的路径进行分布平滑。

Result: 在端到端训练中，HACE 在 18 个架构-数据集组合中的 15 个上优于标准交叉熵，平均提升 4.66%；在冻结 DINOv2-Large 特征的线性探测中，HACE 在所有三个数据集上均优于所有对比方法，平均比次优基线提升 2.18%。

Conclusion: HACE 作为一种即插即用的损失函数，能有效利用类别层级结构信息，在多种架构和数据集上一致提升分类性能，且无需修改模型结构。

Original Abstract: Standard cross-entropy is the default classification loss across virtually all of machine learning, yet it treats all misclassifications equally, ignoring the semantic distances that a class hierarchy encodes. We propose Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE), a drop-in replacement for standard cross-entropy that incorporates a known class hierarchy directly into the loss. HACE combines two components: prediction aggregation, which propagates the model's probability mass upward through the class hierarchy to ensure that parent nodes accumulate the confidence of their children; and ancestral label smoothing, which distributes the ground-truth signal along the path from the true class to the root. We evaluate HACE on CIFAR-100, FGVC Aircraft, and NABirds in two regimes: end-to-end training across six architectures spanning convolutional and attention-based designs, and linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features. In end-to-end training, HACE improves accuracy over standard cross-entropy in 15 out of 18 architecture--dataset pairs, with a mean gain of 4.66\%. In linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features, HACE outperforms all competing methods on all three datasets, with a mean improvement of 2.18\% over the next best baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 标准交叉熵几乎是机器学习中默认的分类损失函数，但它平等对待所有错误分类，忽略了类别层级编码的语义距离。我们提出层级感知交叉熵（HACE），一种可直接替代标准交叉熵的损失函数，将已知的类别层级结构直接融入损失中。HACE 包含两个组件：预测聚合——将模型的预测概率沿类别层级向上传播，确保父节点累积其子节点的置信度；以及祖先标签平滑——将真实标签信号沿从真实类别到根节点的路径进行分布。我们在 CIFAR-100、FGVC Aircraft 和 NABirds 上评估了 HACE，在两种设定下进行测试：跨越六种卷积和注意力机制架构的端到端训练，以及在冻结的 DINOv2-Large 特征上进行线性探测。在端到端训练中，HACE 在 18 个架构-数据集组合中的 15 个上优于标准交叉熵，平均提升 4.66%。在冻结的 DINOv2-Large 特征线性探测中，HACE 在所有三个数据集上均优于所有竞争方法，平均比次优基线提升 2.18%。

</details>


### [170] [Memory Efficient Full-gradient Attacks (MEFA) Framework for Adversarial Defense Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06357)
*Yuan Du,Mitchel Hill,HanQin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对迭代随机净化防御的白盒对抗攻击鲁棒性评估问题，提出了一种基于梯度检查点技术的内存高效全梯度评估框架，证明精确梯度计算对可靠评估此类防御至关重要。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有迭代随机净化防御的评估方法因内存限制往往采用近似反向传播，这会削弱攻击信号并高估模型鲁棒性；同时，迭代过程中的随机性未得到有效控制，不同净化轨迹可能导致鲁棒性指标显著变化。因此需要一种内存高效且能计算精确全梯度的评估方法。

Method: 提出结合梯度检查点（Gradient Checkpointing）的反向传播方法，以额外重计算换取大幅降低的内存开销，从而在长净化轨迹上实现精确的端到端梯度计算；同时设计评估协议控制随机变异性，综合形成内存高效的全梯度评估框架。

Result: 在基于扩散的纯化和基于能量模型（EBM）的朗之万采样防御上，全梯度攻击能够发现近似梯度评估遗漏的漏洞；该方法在ℓ∞和ℓ2白盒攻击上达到了当前最优的更强攻击效果，并支持对分布外鲁棒性的探测。

Conclusion: 精确梯度评估对于迭代随机防御的可靠基准测试至关重要，全梯度攻击揭示了此类防御中之前被低估的脆弱性。

Original Abstract: This work studies the robust evaluation of iterative stochastic purification defenses under white-box adversarial attacks. Our key technical insight is that gradient checkpointing makes exact end-to-end gradient computation through long purification trajectories practical by trading additional recomputation for substantially lower memory usage. This enables full-gradient adaptive attacks against diffusion- and Langevin-based purification defenses, where prior evaluations often resort to approximate backpropagation due to memory constraints. These approximations can weaken the attack signal and risk overestimating robustness. In parallel, stochasticity in iterative purification is frequently under-controlled, even though different purification trajectories can substantially change reported robustness metrics. Building on this insight, we introduce a memory-efficient full-gradient evaluation framework for stochastic purification defenses. The framework combines checkpointed backpropagation with evaluation protocols that control stochastic variability, thereby reducing memory bottlenecks while preserving exact gradients. We evaluate diffusion-based purification and Langevin sampling with Energy-Based Models (EBMs), demonstrating that full-gradient attacks uncover vulnerabilities missed by approximate-gradient evaluations. Our framework yields stronger state-of-the-art $\ell_{\infty}$ and $\ell_{2}$ white-box attacks and further supports probing out-of-distribution robustness. Overall, our results show that exact-gradient evaluation is essential for reliable benchmarking of iterative stochastic defenses.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究探讨了在白盒对抗攻击下，迭代随机净化防御的鲁棒性评估问题。我们的关键技术洞察在于：梯度检查点技术通过以额外重计算换取代为降低的内存使用量，使得通过长净化轨迹进行精确的端到端梯度计算成为可能。这实现了针对基于扩散和朗之万采样的净化防御的全梯度自适应攻击——而先前的评估因内存限制常常采用近似反向传播。这些近似会削弱攻击信号，并有高估鲁棒性的风险。与此同时，即使不同的净化轨迹会显著改变报告的鲁棒性指标，迭代净化中的随机性也常常未被充分控制。基于这一洞察，我们引入了一种内存高效的全梯度评估框架，用于随机净化防御。该框架将检查点反向传播与能够控制随机变异性的评估协议相结合，从而在保留精确梯度的同时减少了内存瓶颈。我们评估了基于扩散的净化以及基于能量模型（EBM）的朗之万采样，证明全梯度攻击能够发现近似梯度评估所遗漏的漏洞。我们的框架在ℓ∞和ℓ2白盒攻击上实现了当前最优的更强攻击性能，并进一步支持探测分布外鲁棒性。总体而言，我们的结果表明，精确梯度评估对于迭代随机防御的可靠基准测试至关重要。

</details>


### [171] [In-Context Black-Box Optimization with Unreliable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06187)
*Nicolas Samuel Blumer,Julien Martinelli,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈感知的上下文黑盒优化（FICBO），通过结构化反馈先验训练Transformer，使其在测试时能同时利用历史观测和辅助反馈信号，在保持对弱或误导性反馈鲁棒的同时有效利用有用信息，提升优化性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有黑盒优化方法要么一次只处理单一反馈源（难以泛化到多种反馈），要么只利用优化历史（忽略测试时可用的廉价辅助反馈），缺乏能同时利用多种异构反馈信号且具备跨任务泛化能力的方法。

Method: 提出结构化反馈先验，建模反馈源在可访问性、相关性和失真度三个维度的变化；基于该先验预训练一个反馈感知的Transformer，使其在测试时能通过比较观测目标值与辅助信号来上下文估计各反馈源的可靠性，从而改进查询选择。

Result: 在合成任务和真实世界任务上，FICBO能有效利用有信息量的反馈，同时对弱或误导性反馈保持鲁棒，性能优于其他基线方法。实验还揭示了模型如何感知测试时的反馈源，提供了可解释性方面的洞见。

Conclusion: FICBO通过预训练反馈感知的Transformer，实现了在测试时自适应地利用多种辅助反馈信号进行黑盒优化，兼具利用有用反馈和抵抗误导性噪声的能力，为反馈感知的黑盒优化提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Black-box optimization in science and engineering often comes with side information: experts, simulators, pretrained predictors, or heuristics can suggest which candidates look promising. This information can accelerate search, but it can also be biased, input-dependent, or misleading. Feedback-aware BO methods typically handle one task at a time, limiting their ability to generalize over multiple sources of feedback. In-context optimizers address cross-task adaptation, but usually assume that optimization history is the only available signal at test time. We study feedback-informed in-context black-box optimization (FICBO), where a pretrained optimizer conditions on both the observed history and cheap auxiliary feedback for the current candidate set. We introduce a structured feedback prior that models how feedback sources vary in their access, relevance, and distortion relative to the true objective, and use it to pretrain a feedback-aware transformer. At test time, the model estimates source reliability in context by comparing observed objective values with auxiliary signals, improving query selection. On synthetic and real-world tasks, FICBO effectively exploits informative feedback while remaining robust to weak or misleading sources, improving over other baselines. Empirical investigations further illustrate how the model perceives test-time sources, offering insights into its interpretability and decision-making process.

中文摘要翻译: 科学与工程中的黑盒优化通常伴随着辅助信息：专家、模拟器、预训练预测器或启发式方法可以提示哪些候选方案看起来有希望。这些信息可以加速搜索，但也可能具有偏差、依赖于输入或具有误导性。反馈感知的贝叶斯优化方法通常一次只处理一个任务，限制了它们在多个反馈源上的泛化能力。上下文优化器能够处理跨任务适应，但通常假设测试时唯一可用的信号是优化历史。我们研究了反馈感知的上下文黑盒优化（FICBO），其中预训练的优化器同时基于观测历史和当前候选集的廉价辅助反馈进行条件建模。我们引入了一种结构化反馈先验，该先验建模反馈源在可访问性、相关性和相对于真实目标的失真度方面的变化，并利用它预训练一个反馈感知的Transformer。在测试时，模型通过比较观测到的目标值与辅助信号，在上下文环境中估计信源的可靠性，从而改进查询选择。在合成任务和真实世界任务上，FICBO有效地利用了有信息量的反馈，同时对弱或误导性源保持鲁棒，优于其他基线方法。实验研究进一步阐明了模型如何感知测试时的信源，为其可解释性和决策过程提供了洞见。

</details>


### [172] [Constrained Contextual Bandits with Adversarial Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06190)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预算约束下的对抗性上下文赌博机问题，基于SquareCB框架提出一个模块化方法，利用在线回归预言机将约束问题转化为无约束问题，在对抗性上下文中获得优于以往工作的保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有预算约束上下文赌博机的工作主要针对随机上下文，对于更一般的对抗性上下文缺乏有效方法；同时需要同时控制遗憾和预算约束违背，问题具有挑战性。

Method: 基于SquareCB框架，提出一个简单模块化的框架，通过在线回归预言机将带约束的上下文赌博机问题转化为具有自适应定义的替代奖励函数的无约束上下文赌博机问题。

Result: 相比以往关注随机上下文的工作，该方法在更一般的对抗性上下文中获得了更优的保证，并且算法高效、分析简洁透明。

Conclusion: 提出的框架有效解决了对抗性上下文下的预算约束赌博机问题，兼具理论保证和实际效率。

Original Abstract: We study budget-constrained contextual bandits with adversarial contexts, where each action yields a random reward and incurs a random cost. We adopt the standard realizability assumption: conditioned on the observed context, rewards and costs are drawn independently from fixed distributions whose expectations belong to known function classes. We focus on the continuing setting, in which the algorithm operates over the entire horizon even after the budget for cumulative cost is exhausted. In this setting, the objective is to simultaneously control regret and the violation of the budget constraint. Building on the seminal $\mathsf{SquareCB}$ framework of Foster et al. [2018], we propose a simple and modular framework that leverages online regression oracles to reduce the constrained problem to a standard unconstrained contextual bandit problem with adaptively defined surrogate reward functions. In contrast to prior works, which focus on stochastic contexts, our reduction yields improved guarantees for more general adversarial contexts, together with an efficient algorithm with a compact and transparent analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究具有对抗性上下文的预算约束上下文赌博机问题，其中每个动作产生随机奖励并消耗随机成本。我们采用标准的可实现性假设：在给定观察到的上下文条件下，奖励和成本独立地从固定分布中抽取，其期望属于已知的函数类。我们关注持续学习场景，即算法在累积成本耗尽预算后仍在整个时间范围内运行。在此场景中，目标是同时控制遗憾和预算约束的违背。基于Foster等人[2018]的开创性SquareCB框架，我们提出了一个简单且模块化的框架，该框架利用在线回归预言机将约束问题转化为具有自适应定义的替代奖励函数的标准无约束上下文赌博机问题。与以往关注随机上下文的工作相比，我们的约简方法在更一般的对抗性上下文中获得了改进的保证，同时提供了一种高效且分析简洁透明的算法。

</details>


### [173] [E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06415)
*Qingjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无量纲控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)，用于预测混合专家模型（MoE）中专家生态的健康程度（是否出现死专家）。通过12组对照实验（8个视觉任务、4个语言任务）验证，当 E >= 0.5 时即可保证零死专家，无需手工设计的负载均衡辅助损失。此外还发现了死专家可复苏、正交毒性具有数据集依赖性、任务复杂度改变E阈值、过拟合与专家生态解耦、三层MoE自发坍缩为两层、生态结构在50倍温度范围内保持不变等六个额外发现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MoE训练中常依赖手工设计的负载均衡辅助损失来防止专家坍缩（死专家），但缺乏统一的、可预测的自调节控制参数。本文旨在提出一个无量纲控制参数，能够统一诊断MoE训练的专家生态健康程度，并消除对辅助损失的依赖。

Method: 提出控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)，其中T为路由温度，H为路由熵权重，O为oracle权重，B为平衡权重。通过12组对照实验（8个视觉任务在CIFAR-10/100、TinyImageNet-200上，4个语言任务在WikiText-2/103上），总计超过11,000个训练epoch，系统验证E参数与死专家比例、专家生态健康之间的定量关系。

Result: E >= 0.5 即可保证零死专家，无需负载均衡辅助损失；死专家可以复苏；正交毒性具有数据集依赖性；任务复杂度改变E的临界阈值；模型过拟合与专家生态健康解耦；三层MoE自发坍缩为两层功能结构；专家生态结构在50倍温度范围内保持不变。

Conclusion: E参数可作为MoE训练的统一诊断工具，类比于流体动力学中的雷诺数。该参数消除了对人工设计负载均衡损失的依赖，并揭示了MoE训练中多个此前未被发现的动态行为模式。

Original Abstract: We introduce E = T*H/(O+B), a dimensionless control parameter that predicts whether Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models will develop a healthy expert ecology or collapse into dead experts. E combines four hyperparameters -- routing temperature T, routing entropy weight H, oracle weight O, and balance weight B -- into a single quantity. Through 12 controlled experiments (8 vision, 4 language) totaling over 11,000 training epochs, we establish that E >= 0.5 alone is sufficient to guarantee zero dead experts, removing the necessity for handcrafted load-balancing auxiliary losses. We validate this cross-modally on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet-200, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. Six additional findings emerge: (1) dead experts can resuscitate -- triggered by balance loss driving router re-exploration; (2) ortho toxicity is dataset-dependent, not universal; (3) task complexity shifts the critical E threshold; (4) model overfitting is decoupled from expert ecological health; (5) three-tier MoE spontaneously collapses into a two-tier functional structure; (6) ecological structure is temperature-invariant across a 50x range. We propose that E serves as a unified diagnostic for MoE training, analogous to the Reynolds number in fluid dynamics.

中文摘要翻译: 我们引入 E = T*H/(O+B)，这是一个无量纲控制参数，用于预测混合专家模型（MoE）是会形成健康的专家生态还是会坍缩为死专家。E将四个超参数——路由温度T、路由熵权重H、oracle权重O和平衡权重B——组合为一个单一数值。通过12组对照实验（8个视觉任务、4个语言任务），总计超过11,000个训练epoch，我们验证了 E >= 0.5 足以保证零死专家，从而消除了对手工设计负载均衡辅助损失的需求。我们在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet-200、WikiText-2和WikiText-103上跨模态验证了这一结论。此外还发现了六项额外结果：(1) 死专家可以复苏——由平衡损失驱动路由器重新探索所触发；(2) 正交毒性具有数据集依赖性，并非普遍存在；(3) 任务复杂度会改变E的临界阈值；(4) 模型过拟合与专家生态健康解耦；(5) 三层MoE自发坍缩为两层功能结构；(6) 生态结构在50倍温度范围内保持不变。我们认为E可作为MoE训练的统一诊断工具，类似于流体动力学中的雷诺数。

</details>


### [174] [Bandit Learning in General Open Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06202)
*Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的开放系统多臂赌博机框架，该框架允许智能体随时间动态到达和离开，并解决了由此产生的非平稳性、信息累积和遗憾尺度增长等挑战。作者引入了预训练程度、稳定性和全局动态遗憾等新概念，并开发了具有可证明保证的全局-UCB学习方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有关于开放系统赌博机学习的工作依赖于频繁被实际应用违反的结构性假设，缺乏一个能处理异构奖励和一般智能体模式的统一学习范式。新到达的智能体引发内生非平稳性，智能体模式决定信息累积速度，且新智能体使遗憾随时间范围进一步扩大，这些挑战亟待解决。

Method: 本文提出了一个统一的开放系统赌博机问题框架，涵盖一般动力学、异构奖励和一般智能体模式。引入了三个新概念：预训练程度（量化新智能体携带的信息量）、稳定性（衡量新智能体对系统的影响）和全局动态遗憾（比较所有活跃智能体的累积期望奖励与变化的优臂）。在此基础上，开发了全局-UCB学习算法并提供了可证明的遗憾界保证。

Result: 理论分析表明：进入不确定性通过预训练程度线性进入遗憾界；在稳定系统中，遗憾由识别持久最优臂所需时间以及智能体模式共同决定；通过困难实例的下界证明了这些依赖关系是紧的。

Conclusion: 本文为开放系统中的赌博机学习提供了统一的理论框架，所提出的概念和算法有效地刻画了开放系统特有的复杂性，且遗憾界的紧性验证了方法的理论最优性。

Original Abstract: Recent developments in digital platforms have highlighted the prevalence of open systems, where agents can arrive and depart over time. While bandit learning in open systems has recently received initial attention, existing work imposes structural assumptions that are frequently violated in practice. A learning paradigm for general open systems creates fresh challenges: newly arriving agents induce endogenous non-stationarity; agent patterns determine how quickly information accumulates; and new agents make regret scale further with the time horizon. To this end, we formulate a unified open-system bandit problem with general dynamics, including heterogeneous rewards and general agent patterns. We introduce new concepts to capture the inherent complexities: the \emph{pre-training degree} of new agents quantifies how much information an agent carries upon entry, \emph{stability} measures the impact of new agents on the system, and \emph{global dynamic regret} compares the cumulative expected reward of all active agents with that of the varying optimal arms. We develop certified global-UCB learning methodologies with provable guarantees. Our regret bounds reveal that entry uncertainty enters linearly via the pre-training degree, while in stable regimes, regret is governed by the time needed to identify a persistent optimal arm, as well as by the agent patterns. We further show that these dependencies are tight via lower bounds in hard instances.

中文摘要翻译: 数字平台的最新发展凸显了开放系统的普遍性，在这类系统中，智能体可以随时间到达和离开。尽管开放系统中的赌博机学习近期已获得初步关注，但现有工作施加的结构性假设在实践中经常被违反。针对一般开放系统的学习范式带来了全新的挑战：新到达的智能体会引发内生的非平稳性；智能体模式决定了信息累积的速度；新智能体使得遗憾随时间范围进一步扩大。为此，我们构建了一个具有一般动力学特性的统一开放系统赌博机问题，包括异构奖励和一般智能体模式。我们引入了新概念来刻画固有的复杂性：新智能体的\textit{预训练程度}量化了智能体进入系统时携带的信息量，\textit{稳定性}衡量新智能体对系统的影响，而\textit{全局动态遗憾}则比较所有活跃智能体的累积期望奖励与变化的最优臂之间的差距。我们开发了具有可证明保证的认证全局-UCB学习方法。我们的遗憾界表明，进入不确定性通过预训练程度线性进入，而在稳定系统中，遗憾由识别持久最优臂所需的时间以及智能体模式共同决定。我们进一步通过困难实例的下界证明了这些依赖关系是紧的。

</details>


### [175] [Agentic AIs Are the Missing Paradigm for Out-of-Distribution Generalization in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06522)
*Xin Wang,Haibo Chen,Wenxuan Liu,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了基础模型面临的分布外（OOD）问题在结构上区别于传统OOD研究，由于预训练和后训练分布仅部分可观测，传统以模型为中心的方法存在参数覆盖上限，而智能体系统（具备感知、策略选择、外部动作和闭环验证）构成解决该问题的必要补充范式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基础模型在开放世界部署中面临知识边界、能力上限、组合偏移和开放式任务变化等新型OOD现象，这些现象与传统OOD设定有本质不同，且现代基础模型的预训练和后训练分布往往仅被部分观测，现有以模型为中心的范式无法应对。

Method: 通过四步论证：（1）提出阶段感知的OOD形式化框架，容纳部分可观测的多阶段训练分布；（2）证明参数覆盖上限定理，表明存在模型中心方法（无论训练时或测试时）无法在容忍度ε内处理的输入；（3）刻画智能体OOD系统的四个结构性质（感知、策略选择、外部动作、闭环验证），证明其严格扩展了可达集；（4）回应七个反驳论点，承认其中两个。

Result: 证明了参数覆盖上限的存在，表明纯模型中心方法存在固有局限性；证明了智能体系统能严格扩展可达集超越该上限；回应了七个潜在反驳，承认其中两个的有效性。

Conclusion: 基础模型的OOD问题不能仅靠以模型为中心的范式解决，智能体范式与之互补，应被明确认可为第一类研究方向。

Original Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) are increasingly deployed in open-world settings where distribution shift is the rule rather than the exception. The out-of-distribution (OOD) phenomena they face -- knowledge boundaries, capability ceilings, compositional shifts, and open-ended task variation -- differ in kind from the settings that have shaped prior OOD research, and are further complicated because the pretraining and post-training distributions of modern FMs are often only partially observed. Our position is that OOD for foundation models is a structurally distinct problem that cannot be solved within the prevailing model-centric paradigm, and that agentic systems constitute the missing paradigm required to address it. We defend this claim through four steps. First, we give a stage-aware formalization of OOD that accommodates partially observed multi-stage training distributions. Second, we prove a parameter coverage ceiling: there exist practically relevant inputs that no model-centric method (training-time or test-time) can handle within tolerance $\varepsilon$, for reasons intrinsic to parameter-based representation. Third, we characterize agentic OOD systems by four structural properties -- perception, strategy selection, external action, and closed-loop verification -- and show that they strictly extend the reachable set beyond the ceiling. Fourth, we respond to seven counterarguments, conceding two, and outline a research agenda. We do not claim that agentic methods subsume model-centric ones; we argue that the two are complementary, and that progress on FM-OOD requires explicit recognition of the agentic paradigm as a first-class research direction.

中文摘要翻译: 基础模型（FM）越来越多地部署在开放世界环境中，在该环境中分布偏移是常态而非例外。它们所面临的分布外（OOD）现象——知识边界、能力上限、组合偏移和开放式任务变化——在种类上不同于塑造了先前OOD研究的设定，并且由于现代FM的预训练和后训练分布通常仅被部分观测，这一问题变得更加复杂。我们的立场是：基础模型的OOD是一个结构上独特的难题，无法在盛行的以模型为中心的范式内解决，而智能体系统构成了解决该问题所缺失的范式。我们通过四个步骤来捍卫这一主张。首先，我们给出一个阶段感知的OOD形式化框架，该框架能够容纳部分可观测的多阶段训练分布。其次，我们证明了参数覆盖上限：存在实际相关的输入，任何以模型为中心的方法（无论是训练时还是测试时）都无法在容忍度ε内处理，原因在于基于参数表示的固有特性。第三，我们通过四个结构性质——感知、策略选择、外部动作和闭环验证——来刻画智能体OOD系统，并证明它们严格扩展了可达集使其超越该上限。第四，我们回应了七个反驳论点，承认其中两个的有效性，并概述了一个研究议程。我们并不声称智能体方法取代了以模型为中心的方法；我们认为两者是互补的，并且FM-OOD的进展需要明确承认智能体范式作为第一类研究方向。

</details>


### [176] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在极限学习框架下研究对比式的识别与生成问题，学习者只能观察正负例之间的无序对比对，而无法知晓具体标签。文章给出了无噪声下的精确可识别性刻画、对比闭包维度与样本复杂度分析，并发现对比式生成与识别之间存在不可比层次结构；进一步证明在有限对抗噪声下，对比式方法比正例学习更具鲁棒性。核心技术工具是“共同交叉图”，统一刻画了成对歧义性、族级生成障碍与噪声缺陷。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 经典的极限识别与生成学习均假设数据以正例或全标注形式呈现，但许多自然监督信号本质上是关系型的（如对比对），而非单例标签。作者希望探索在这种对比式呈现下，学习是否依然可能，以及其与经典正例学习在可识别性、生成能力和噪声鲁棒性方面的差异。

Method: 引入“对比式呈现”形式（无序对比对{h(x)≠h(y)}），提出对比式极限识别与生成框架；利用“共同交叉图”（common crossing graph）作为统一的技术对象，编码成对歧义性、族级生成障碍和噪声缺陷；基于此推导出对比可识别类的精确几何刻画（对Angluin tell-tale条件的精细改进）、对比闭包维度及样本复杂度紧界。

Result: （1）给出了对比可识别类的一行几何刻画；（2）定义了对比闭包维度，精确刻画了均匀对比生成能力及其样本复杂度紧界；（3）证明对比式生成与对比式识别之间存在严格层次结构且相互不可比；（4）在有限对抗噪声下，存在某些类从对比对中可被单一预算无关算法识别，但仅一个噪声正例就导致正例学习失败，呈现显著翻转。

Conclusion: 对比式学习在极限框架下具有独特的理论性质：其可识别性条件与正例学习不同，生成与识别能力不可比，且在有限对抗噪声下具有显著更强的鲁棒性。共同交叉图是贯穿这些结果的核心工具。

Original Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

中文摘要翻译: 在Gold [1967]的极限识别经典模型中，学习者逐轮接收正例流，并必须最终恢复目标假设。Kleinberg和Mullainathan [2024]近期提出了极限生成，要求学习者最终输出目标支持集中的新颖元素。这两条研究线路均关注正例或全标注数据。然而，许多自然的监督信号本质上是关系型的而非单例的，它们编码的是样本之间的关系而非单个样本的标签。本文首次研究了极限下的对比式识别与生成问题，其中学习者观察对比式的数据呈现：一个由无序对{x,y}组成的流，满足h(x)≠h(y)，其中h是一个未知的二元目标假设，但哪个元素为正对学习者隐藏。我们首先在无噪声设定下给出三个结果：对比可识别类的精确刻画（Angluin [1980]的tell-tale条件的一个单行几何精化）、一个称为对比闭包维度的组合维度（Raman等人[2025]中闭包维度的对比类比）及其精确刻画均匀对比生成能力并给出紧样本复杂度，以及一个严格层次结构表明对比生成与识别互不相容。随后我们证明在有限对抗噪声下存在一个尖锐反转：存在某些类，在任意有限噪声预算下可由单一预算无关算法从对比对中识别，但即使只遭受一个噪声观测，也无法从正例中识别。统一的技术对象是共同交叉图，它在一个覆盖与关联的语言中统一编码了成对歧义性、族级生成障碍和噪声缺陷。

</details>


### [177] [Soft Deterministic Policy Gradient with Gaussian Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06228)
*Hyunjun Na,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出软确定性策略梯度（Soft-DPG），通过高斯平滑的贝尔曼方程定义新的动作价值函数，消除对评论员动作梯度的依赖，解决稀疏/离散奖励场景下策略梯度不稳定问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统确定性策略梯度（DPG）依赖评论员对动作的梯度可微性，但在稀疏或离散奖励的实际控制问题中，该假设被违反，导致策略梯度定义不当且学习不稳定。

Method: 通过高斯平滑构造平滑贝尔曼方程，定义新的动作价值函数，推导出软确定性策略梯度（Soft-DPG），消除对评论员动作梯度的显式依赖，确保梯度在非光滑Q函数下仍有良好定义，并实例化为Soft DDPG算法。

Result: 在标准连续控制基准及离散化奖励变体上，Soft DDPG在密集奖励设置中保持竞争力，在大多数离散化奖励环境中相比标准DDPG取得明显提升，标准DDPG对不规则评论员曲面更敏感。

Conclusion: 所提Soft-DPG框架为处理非光滑Q函数下的策略优化提供了原则性替代方案，有效缓解了DPG在稀疏/离散奖励场景下的不稳定问题。

Original Abstract: Deterministic policy gradient (DPG) is widely utilized for continuous control; however, it inherently relies on the differentiability of the critic with respect to the action during policy updates. This assumption is violated in practical control problems involving sparse or discrete rewards, leading to ill-defined policy gradients and unstable learning. To address these challenges, we propose a principled alternative based on a smoothed Bellman equation formulated via Gaussian smoothing. Specifically, we define a novel action-value function based on a smoothed Bellman equation and derive the soft deterministic policy gradient (Soft-DPG). Our formulation eliminates explicit dependence on critic action-gradients and ensures that the gradient remains well-defined even for non-smooth Q-functions. We instantiate this framework into a deep reinforcement learning algorithm, which we call soft deep deterministic policy gradient (Soft DDPG). Empirical evaluations on standard continuous control benchmarks and their discretized-reward variants show that Soft DDPG remains competitive in dense-reward settings and provides clear gains in most discretized-reward environments, where standard DDPG is more sensitive to irregular critic landscapes.

中文摘要翻译: 确定性策略梯度（DPG）被广泛用于连续控制，但其本质上依赖于策略更新时评论员对动作的可微性。在涉及稀疏或离散奖励的实际控制问题中，这一假设被违反，导致策略梯度定义不当且学习不稳定。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种基于高斯平滑的平滑贝尔曼方程的原则性替代方案。具体而言，我们基于平滑贝尔曼方程定义了一种新的动作价值函数，并推导出软确定性策略梯度（Soft-DPG）。我们的公式消除了对评论员动作梯度的显式依赖，并确保即使在非光滑Q函数下梯度仍保持良好定义。我们将该框架实例化为一种深度强化学习算法，称为软深度确定性策略梯度（Soft DDPG）。在标准连续控制基准及其离散化奖励变体上的经验评估表明，Soft DDPG在密集奖励设置中保持竞争力，并在大多数离散化奖励环境中提供明显增益，而标准DDPG对不规则的评论员曲面更为敏感。

</details>


### [178] [Band Together: Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination against Evasion-based Promotion Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06238)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态推荐系统中基于逃避的推广攻击，提出了一种多模态协调的非目标对抗训练方法（UAT-MC），通过梯度对齐机制解决跨模态梯度不匹配问题，在保持推荐性能的同时显著提升鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有防御主要局限于单模态设置且聚焦于投毒攻击，逃避攻击未被充分探索；此外，在多用户推广场景下存在跨模态梯度不匹配问题，视觉和文本扰动因不同用户群的支配而优化方向不一致，削弱了攻击效果并导致鲁棒训练低估最坏情况风险。

Method: 提出UAT-MC方法：1) 将所有物品视为潜在目标以应对逃避攻击中未知目标物品的挑战；2) 引入梯度对齐机制显式校正视觉模态与文本模态之间的梯度不匹配，确保跨模态扰动同步优化，从而最大化对抗强度用于鲁棒训练。

Result: 大量实验表明，UAT-MC在防御-准确性权衡下，显著提升了对推广攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持了可接受的推荐性能。

Conclusion: UAT-MC有效解决了多模态推荐系统中逃避攻击的防御难题，为多模态鲁棒推荐提供了新的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Multimodal recommender systems exploit visual and textual signals to alleviate data sparsity, but this also makes them more vulnerable to evasion-based promotion attacks. Existing defenses are largely limited to single-modal settings and mainly focus on poisoning-based threats, leaving evasion-based threats underexplored. In this work, we first identify a cross-modal gradient mismatch under the multi-user promotion setting, where visual and textual perturbations are optimized in inconsistent directions due to the dominance of distinct user groups. This phenomenon dilutes the attack effectiveness and leads robust training to underestimate worst-case risks. To address this issue, we propose Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination (UAT-MC). UAT-MC tackles the challenge of unknown targeted items in evasion-based attacks (as opposed to poisoning-based attacks) by treating all items as potential targets, and introduces a gradient alignment mechanism to explicitly correct this mismatch. This design ensures synchronized perturbations across modalities, thereby maximizing adversarial strength for robust training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UAT-MC significantly improves robustness against promotion attacks while maintaining acceptable recommendation performance under the defense-accuracy trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态推荐系统利用视觉和文本信号来缓解数据稀疏性，但这也使其更容易受到基于逃避的推广攻击。现有防御主要局限于单模态设置，且主要聚焦于投毒攻击威胁，而逃避攻击威胁尚未得到充分探索。在这项工作中，我们首先发现多用户推广场景下存在跨模态梯度不匹配现象，即由于不同用户群的支配，视觉和文本扰动在不一致的方向上被优化。这一现象削弱了攻击有效性，并导致鲁棒训练低估最坏情况风险。为解决该问题，我们提出具有多模态协调的非目标对抗训练（UAT-MC）。UAT-MC通过将所有物品视为潜在目标来应对逃避攻击（相对于投毒攻击）中未知目标物品的挑战，并引入梯度对齐机制显式校正这种不匹配。该设计确保了跨模态扰动的同步性，从而最大化对抗强度以用于鲁棒训练。大量实验表明，UAT-MC在防御-准确性权衡下，显著提升了对推广攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持了可接受的推荐性能。代码已在 https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC 开源。

</details>


### [179] [When Graph Language Models Go Beyond Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06239)
*Masatsugu Yamada,Mahito Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种校准的诊断协议，用于区分图语言模型是真正学习了结构规律还是仅仅记忆了训练图。结果表明，大规模模型主要在高频子图模式上表现出了超越记忆的结构对齐能力，而低频模式仍覆盖不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的聚合保真度指标无法区分图语言模型是学习了结构规律还是仅仅记忆了训练图，因此需要一种更精细的诊断方法来解开这一混淆。

Method: 提出了一种结合频繁子图挖掘、图级bootstrap基线和三级频率分层的校准诊断协议，用于解耦记忆效应与结构对齐能力。

Result: 在五个TU基准上，LLaMA风格图语言模型虽然达到高子图秩相关，但在多数情况下被记忆bootstrap匹配或超越；小规模时诊断结果与逐字回忆难以区分；大规模（375万图）时逐字记忆急剧下降但秩相关保持高位；高频模式得到良好复现，低频模式覆盖不足；该现象在两种序列化方式下均稳健成立。

Conclusion: 图语言模型能够在规模扩展时真正获取超越记忆的结构规律，但这种能力主要体现在高频子图模式上，低频模式的复现能力仍然有限。

Original Abstract: It remains unclear whether graph language models learn structural regularities or merely memorize training graphs; this cannot be resolved by current aggregate fidelity metrics alone. We develop a calibrated diagnostic protocol that combines frequent subgraph mining, a graph-level bootstrap baseline, and three-level frequency stratification to disentangle memorization from structural alignment. Using this framework, we show that graph language models can acquire structural regularities beyond memorization at scale, primarily in the high-frequency regime. This is supported by the following empirical evidence: On five TU benchmarks, LLaMA-style graph language models reach high subgraph-rank correlation, yet their alignment is matched or exceeded by the memorization bootstrap in most cases. At small scale, under our bootstrap diagnostic, fidelity is largely indistinguishable from verbatim recall. In contrast, at large scale with 3.75M graphs, verbatim memorization drops sharply while rank correlation remains near ceiling. Crucially, in a separate fixed-subsample analysis, frequent subgraph mining restricted to the novel-only subset closely tracks the corresponding all-generation Spearman correlation, providing evidence that the alignment is not driven solely by verbatim recall. Across all scales, high-frequency patterns are well reproduced, while rare patterns remain poorly covered, and this deficit narrows only marginally as capacity increases. We observe the same scale-dependent crossover under two distinct graph serializations (canonical DFS code and action sequences), providing evidence of robustness in our analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 图语言模型是学习了结构规律还是仅仅记忆了训练图，这一问题尚不清楚；现有的聚合保真度指标无法单独解决这一困惑。我们开发了一种校准的诊断协议，结合了频繁子图挖掘、图级bootstrap基线以及三级频率分层，以解耦记忆效应与结构对齐能力。利用这一框架，我们证明图语言模型能够在规模扩展时获取超越记忆的结构规律，且主要发生在高频子图区域。以下实验证据支持该结论：在五个TU基准数据集上，LLaMA风格的图语言模型达到了较高的子图秩相关，但在大多数情况下，其对齐水平被记忆bootstrap基线匹配或超越。在小规模设置下，根据我们的bootstrap诊断，保真度与逐字回忆几乎不可区分。相比之下，在大规模设置下（375万张图），逐字记忆急剧下降，而秩相关仍保持在接近上限的水平。关键的是，在另设的固定子样本分析中，仅针对新颖子集进行的频繁子图挖掘结果与对应的全生成Spearman相关性高度一致，这表明对齐并非仅由逐字回忆驱动。在所有规模下，高频模式均能得到良好复现，而稀有模式覆盖不足，且这种差距随容量增加仅略有缩小。我们在两种不同的图序列化（规范DFS编码和动作序列）下观察到相同的尺度依赖交叉现象，证明了我们分析的稳健性。

</details>


### [180] [Cumulative-Goodness Free-Riding in Forward-Forward Networks: Real, Repairable, but Not Accuracy-Dominant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06240)
*Amirhossein Yousefiramandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Forward-Forward (FF)训练中存在的“层搭便车”现象：后续层会继承前面层已部分分离的任务，导致深层梯度呈指数衰减。提出了三种局部修复方法（逐块、硬度门控、深度缩放），在不依赖反向传播梯度的情况下恢复当前层的分离度量。实验表明这些方法可显著改善深层分离统计（4×–45×提升），但最终准确率变化不足1个百分点，说明累积搭便车虽是真实且可修复的优化病态，但并非限制FF训练精度的主导因素。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在累积好度（cumulative-goodness）变体的Forward-Forward训练中，后续层会继承前面层已经部分分离的任务，导致深层无法学到有效的表示。作者将这种现象形式化为“层搭便车”（layer free-riding），并发现softplus FF准则下，块d收到的类别判别梯度随前面块累积的正间隔呈指数衰减，亟需修复。

Method: 提出了三种不依赖反向传播梯度的局部修复方法：(1) 逐块（per-block）方法，独立计算每块的损失；(2) 硬度门控（hardness-gated）方法，根据样本难度动态调整损失权重；(3) 深度缩放（depth-scaled）方法，按深度缩放损失信号。这些方法旨在恢复当前层的分离度量。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，三种修复方法使深层分离统计量提升4×–45×，但对非退化训练过程的准确率改变不足1个百分点。Tiny ImageNet验证了相同结论：层健康诊断与最终准确率之间存在定性差距。校准实验表明，架构和数据增强选择对最终准确率的影响大于训练规则修改。

Conclusion: 累积搭便车是Forward-Forward训练中真实存在且可修复的优化病态，但在所研究的FF训练规则、架构和数据集上，它并非限制可达精度的主导因素。

Original Abstract: Forward-Forward (FF) training allows each layer to learn from a local goodness criterion. In cumulative-goodness variants, however, later layers can inherit a task that earlier layers have already partially separated. We formalize this phenomenon as layer free-riding: under the softplus FF criterion, the class-discrimination gradient reaching block $d$ decays exponentially with the positive margin accumulated by preceding blocks. We then study three local remedies -- per-block, hardness-gated, and depth-scaled -- that recover current-layer separation measures without relying on backpropagated gradients. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, these remedies dramatically improve layer-separation statistics, with $4\times$--$45\times$ gains in deeper layers, while changing accuracy by less than one percentage point for non-degenerate training procedures. Tiny ImageNet provides a tougher cross-dataset check for our selected block-wise configuration and reveals the same qualitative gap between layer-health diagnostics and final accuracy.
